import sqlite3
import hashlib
import os
from datetime import datetime

DB_PATH = "station.db"

def hash_mdp(password: str) -> str:
    salt = os.urandom(16)
    key = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode("utf-8"), salt, 100000)
    return f"{salt.hex()}${key.hex()}"

def verifier_mdp(password: str, stored_hash: str) -> bool:
    if not stored_hash:
        return False
    if "$" not in stored_hash:
        return hashlib.sha256(password.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() == stored_hash
    try:
        salt_hex, key_hex = stored_hash.split("$")
        salt = bytes.fromhex(salt_hex)
        expected_key = bytes.fromhex(key_hex)
        new_key = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", password.encode("utf-8"), salt, 100000)
        return new_key == expected_key
    except Exception:
        return False

def get_db_connection():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=25.0)
    # PRAGMAS D'ACCÉLÉRATION EN CACHE RAM
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA cache_size = -64000;")        # 64 Mo de cache RAM
    conn.execute("PRAGMA temp_store = MEMORY;")        # Tris et calculs en RAM
    conn.execute("PRAGMA mmap_size = 268435456;")      # Lecture fichier mémoire projetée
    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

def init_db():
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS utilisateurs (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            nom_complet TEXT NOT NULL,
            password TEXT NOT NULL,
            role TEXT NOT NULL,
            cin TEXT DEFAULT '',
            cnss TEXT DEFAULT '',
            telephone TEXT DEFAULT '',
            date_embauche TEXT DEFAULT '',
            salaire_base REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            adresse TEXT DEFAULT '',
            statut TEXT DEFAULT 'ACTIF'
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS prix_vente (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            date TEXT DEFAULT '',
            date_effet TEXT NOT NULL,
            produit TEXT NOT NULL,
            prix REAL NOT NULL,
            statut TEXT DEFAULT 'ACTIF'
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cloture_quart (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, 
            date TEXT, 
            operateur TEXT,
            fusion REAL DEFAULT 0, 
            tpe REAL DEFAULT 0, 
            especes REAL DEFAULT 0, 
            credits REAL DEFAULT 0,
            total_net REAL DEFAULT 0, 
            resultat REAL DEFAULT 0,
            details_json TEXT,
            credits_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]'
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS compteurs (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, 
            date TEXT, 
            type TEXT,
            pompe_id INTEGER, 
            index_val REAL
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS transactions_jour (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            date_releve TEXT,
            sale_id TEXT,
            date_vente TEXT,
            heure TEXT,
            pump_id INTEGER,
            type TEXT,
            carburant TEXT,
            volume REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            money REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            initial_vol REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            final_vol REAL DEFAULT 0.0
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notes (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            titre TEXT NOT NULL,
            contenu TEXT NOT NULL,
            categorie TEXT DEFAULT 'Général',
            couleur TEXT DEFAULT 'yellow',
            est_epingle INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            date_creation TEXT NOT NULL,
            auteur TEXT DEFAULT 'Admin'
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fournisseurs (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            code TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            nom TEXT NOT NULL,
            type_fournisseur TEXT DEFAULT 'Carburant',
            contact_nom TEXT DEFAULT '',
            telephone TEXT DEFAULT '',
            email TEXT DEFAULT '',
            adresse TEXT DEFAULT '',
            conditions_paiement TEXT DEFAULT '',
            solde_initial REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_fidelite REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            document TEXT DEFAULT '',
            doc_categorie TEXT DEFAULT 'Autre',
            doc_description TEXT DEFAULT '',
            notes TEXT DEFAULT '',
            statut TEXT DEFAULT 'Actif',
            date_creation TEXT DEFAULT ''
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS achats_fournisseurs (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            fournisseur_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            numero_facture TEXT NOT NULL,
            date TEXT NOT NULL,
            reference_bl TEXT DEFAULT '',
            produit TEXT NOT NULL,
            volume_litres REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            prix_unitaire REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_ht REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            taux_tva REAL DEFAULT 10.0,
            montant_tva REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_total REAL NOT NULL,
            observations TEXT DEFAULT '',
            fichier_facture TEXT DEFAULT '',
            lignes_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
            FOREIGN KEY (fournisseur_id) REFERENCES fournisseurs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reglements_fournisseurs (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            fournisseur_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            date TEXT NOT NULL,
            reference_paiement TEXT NOT NULL,
            mode_paiement TEXT NOT NULL,
            montant REAL NOT NULL,
            banque TEXT DEFAULT '',
            observations TEXT DEFAULT '',
            FOREIGN KEY (fournisseur_id) REFERENCES fournisseurs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clients (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            code TEXT UNIQUE,
            nom TEXT NOT NULL,
            type_client TEXT DEFAULT 'Entreprise',
            contact_nom TEXT DEFAULT '',
            telephone TEXT DEFAULT '',
            email TEXT DEFAULT '',
            adresse TEXT DEFAULT '',
            ice TEXT DEFAULT '',
            plafond REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            solde REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            delai_paiement INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            mode_paiement TEXT DEFAULT 'Espèces',
            statut TEXT DEFAULT 'ACTIF',
            date_creation TEXT DEFAULT '',
            document TEXT DEFAULT '',
            doc_rc TEXT DEFAULT '',
            doc_statuts TEXT DEFAULT '',
            doc_cin TEXT DEFAULT ''
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS factures_clients (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            client_id INTEGER,
            numero_facture TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            date_facture TEXT NOT NULL,
            date_debut TEXT DEFAULT '',
            date_fin TEXT DEFAULT '',
            destinataire_nom TEXT DEFAULT '',
            destinataire_info TEXT DEFAULT '',
            taux_tva REAL DEFAULT 10.0,
            remise_pourcent REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_remise REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_ht REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_tva REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
            montant_ttc REAL NOT NULL,
            mode_paiement TEXT DEFAULT 'Espèces',
            notes TEXT DEFAULT '',
            lignes_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
            statut TEXT DEFAULT 'Validée',
            date_creation TEXT DEFAULT '',
            FOREIGN KEY (client_id) REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
        )
    ''')

    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS client_encaissements (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            client_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            client_nom_libre TEXT DEFAULT '',
            date_paiement TEXT NOT NULL,
            mode_paiement TEXT DEFAULT 'Espèces',
            banque TEXT DEFAULT '',
            reference TEXT DEFAULT '',
            description TEXT DEFAULT '',
            montant REAL NOT NULL,
            notes TEXT DEFAULT '',
            fichier TEXT DEFAULT '',
            statut TEXT DEFAULT 'Validé',
            operateur TEXT DEFAULT 'Safouane'
        )
    ''')

    # Migration douce pour toutes les colonnes additionnelles
    migrations = [
        ("achats_fournisseurs", "lignes_json", "TEXT DEFAULT '[]'"),
        ("client_encaissements", "client_nom_libre", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"),
        ("prix_vente", "date_effet", "TEXT DEFAULT ''")
    ]
    for table, col, col_type in migrations:
        try:
            cursor.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {table} ADD COLUMN {col} {col_type}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ========================================================
    # INDEX DE PERFORMANCE CRITIQUES & INDEX D'EXPRESSION
    # ========================================================
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trans_jour ON transactions_jour(date_releve, type, pump_id);")
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trans_date_vente ON transactions_jour(date_vente);")
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fact_client ON factures_clients(client_id, date_facture);")
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_enc_client ON client_encaissements(client_id, date_paiement);")
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_achats_fourn ON achats_fournisseurs(fournisseur_id, date);")
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_prix_statut ON prix_vente(produit, statut);")
    cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trans_dates ON transactions_jour(date_releve, date_vente, type);")

    # Index calculé pour supprimer le freeze sur les rapports de ventes
    try:
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trans_calc_date ON transactions_jour(COALESCE(NULLIF(substr(date_vente, 1, 10), ''), date_releve), type);")
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cloture_sub_date ON cloture_quart(substr(date, 1, 10));")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Données par défaut
    cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM utilisateurs")
    if cursor.fetchone()[0] == 0:
        cursor.executemany(
            """INSERT INTO utilisateurs 
            (username, nom_complet, password, role, cin, cnss, telephone, date_embauche, salaire_base, adresse, statut) 
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
            [
                ("safouane_adm", "SAFOUANE", hash_mdp("admin123"), "admin", "U123456", "102938475", "+212 600 000 000", "2024-01-01", 8500.0, "Oujda", "ACTIF"),
                ("comptable_cpt", "M. COMPTABLE", hash_mdp("compte123"), "comptable", "U654321", "203948576", "+212 611 111 111", "2024-03-01", 6000.0, "Oujda", "ACTIF"),
                ("agent_emp", "AGENT POMPISTE", hash_mdp("emp123"), "employe", "S998877", "304958671", "+212 622 222 222", "2024-06-15", 3500.0, "Oujda", "ACTIF")
            ]
        )

    cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM prix_vente")
    if cursor.fetchone()[0] == 0:
        cursor.execute("INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES ('2026-01-01', '2026-01-01', 'GAZOIL', 14.00, 'ACTIF')")
        cursor.execute("INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES ('2026-01-01', '2026-01-01', 'SSP', 15.00, 'ACTIF')")

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def parse_to_iso_datetime(dt_str: str) -> str:
    if not dt_str:
        return ""
    dt_str = str(dt_str).strip()
    if "/" in dt_str:
        parts = dt_str.split(" ")
        d_parts = parts[0].split("/")
        if len(d_parts) == 3:
            heure = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else "00:00:00"
            if len(heure.split(":")) == 2:
                heure += ":00"
            return f"{d_parts[2]}-{d_parts[1].zfill(2)}-{d_parts[0].zfill(2)} {heure}"
    if len(dt_str) == 10 and "-" in dt_str:
        return f"{dt_str} 00:00:00"
    return dt_str

def get_prix_carburant_a_date(produit: str, date_str: str) -> float:
    prod_upper = str(produit).upper().strip()
    is_diesel = any(k in prod_upper for k in ["DIESEL", "GAZOIL", "G10", "GO", "G50"])
    
    iso_date_norm = parse_to_iso_datetime(date_str) if date_str else ""
    clean_date = iso_date_norm[:10] if iso_date_norm else datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    target_dt = f"{clean_date} 23:59:59"

    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT id, produit, prix, date_effet, date, statut FROM prix_vente")
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()

    candidats = []
    for r in rows:
        r_prod = str(r["produit"]).upper()
        r_is_diesel = any(k in r_prod for k in ["DIESEL", "GAZOIL", "G10", "GO", "G50"])
        
        if is_diesel == r_is_diesel:
            raw_date = r["date_effet"] or r["date"]
            iso_d = parse_to_iso_datetime(raw_date)
            if iso_d and iso_d <= target_dt:
                prio_actif = 1 if str(r["statut"]).upper() == "ACTIF" else 0
                candidats.append((iso_d, prio_actif, r["id"], float(r["prix"])))

    if candidats:
        candidats.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1], x[2]), reverse=True)
        return float(candidats[0][3])

    for r in rows:
        r_prod = str(r["produit"]).upper()
        if is_diesel == any(k in r_prod for k in ["DIESEL", "GAZOIL", "G10", "GO", "G50"]):
            if str(r["statut"]).upper() == "ACTIF":
                return float(r["prix"])

    return 14.00 if is_diesel else 15.00

def generer_prochain_code_fournisseur() -> str:
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT code FROM fournisseurs")
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()

    max_num = 0
    for r in rows:
        code_str = str(r["code"] or "").strip().upper()
        if code_str.startswith("FOUR-"):
            part = code_str.replace("FOUR-", "").strip()
            if part.isdigit():
                max_num = max(max_num, int(part))
        elif code_str.startswith("F") and code_str[1:].isdigit():
            max_num = max(max_num, int(code_str[1:]))

    return f"FOUR-{max_num + 1:03d}"

def generer_prochain_numero_facture() -> str:
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    today_code = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM achats_fournisseurs WHERE numero_facture LIKE ?", (f"FAC-{today_code}%",))
    count = cursor.fetchone()[0] + 1
    conn.close()
    return f"FAC-{today_code}-{count:03d}"

def get_achats_filtres(date_debut: str = None, date_fin: str = None, fournisseur_id: int = None, min_montant: float = None, max_montant: float = None):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    query = """
        SELECT a.*, f.nom as fournisseur_nom, f.code as fournisseur_code
        FROM achats_fournisseurs a
        LEFT JOIN fournisseurs f ON a.fournisseur_id = f.id
        WHERE 1=1
    """
    params = []
    if date_debut:
        query += " AND a.date >= ?"
        params.append(date_debut)
    if date_fin:
        query += " AND a.date <= ?"
        params.append(date_fin)
    if fournisseur_id and fournisseur_id > 0:
        query += " AND a.fournisseur_id = ?"
        params.append(fournisseur_id)
    if min_montant is not None and min_montant > 0:
        query += " AND a.montant_total >= ?"
        params.append(min_montant)
    if max_montant is not None and max_montant > 0:
        query += " AND a.montant_total <= ?"
        params.append(max_montant)
        
    query += " ORDER BY a.date DESC, a.id DESC"
    cursor.execute(query, params)
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    return [dict(r) for r in rows]

def ajouter_achat(fournisseur_id: int, date: str, montant_total: float, numero_facture: str = "", reference_bl: str = "", produit: str = "Diesel", volume_litres: float = 0.0, prix_unitaire: float = 0.0, montant_ht: float = 0.0, taux_tva: float = 10.0, montant_tva: float = 0.0, observations: str = "", fichier_facture: str = ""):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    if not numero_facture or numero_facture.strip() == "":
        numero_facture = generer_prochain_numero_facture()
    if montant_ht <= 0 and montant_total > 0:
        montant_ht = montant_total / (1 + (taux_tva / 100.0))
        montant_tva = montant_total - montant_ht

    cursor.execute("""
        INSERT INTO achats_fournisseurs 
        (fournisseur_id, numero_facture, date, reference_bl, produit, volume_litres, prix_unitaire, montant_ht, taux_tva, montant_tva, montant_total, observations, fichier_facture)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, (fournisseur_id, numero_facture, date, reference_bl, produit, volume_litres, prix_unitaire, montant_ht, taux_tva, montant_tva, montant_total, observations, fichier_facture))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def supprimer_achat(achat_id: int):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("DELETE FROM achats_fournisseurs WHERE id = ?", (achat_id,))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def get_fournisseurs():
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM fournisseurs ORDER BY id ASC")
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    return [dict(r) for r in rows]

def get_fournisseur_complet(fournisseur_id: int = None):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    if fournisseur_id:
        cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM fournisseurs WHERE id = ?", (fournisseur_id,))
    else:
        cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM fournisseurs ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT 1")
    fournisseur = cursor.fetchone()
    if not fournisseur:
        conn.close()
        return None
    f_id = fournisseur["id"]
    cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*), COALESCE(SUM(montant_total), 0.0) FROM achats_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = ?", (f_id,))
    nb_achats, total_achats = cursor.fetchone()
    cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*), COALESCE(SUM(montant), 0.0) FROM reglements_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = ?", (f_id,))
    nb_paiements, total_paye = cursor.fetchone()
    dette = (fournisseur["solde_initial"] + total_achats) - total_paye

    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT 'ACHAT' as type_op, id, date, COALESCE(numero_facture, reference_bl) as ref, 
               produit as details, 
               montant_total as montant_achat, 0.0 as montant_paye, observations, '' as mode, '' as banque
        FROM achats_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = ?
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 'PAIEMENT' as type_op, id, date, reference_paiement as ref, 
               mode_paiement || (CASE WHEN banque != '' THEN ' (' || banque || ')' ELSE '' END) as details, 
               0.0 as montant_achat, montant as montant_paye, observations, mode_paiement as mode, banque
        FROM reglements_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = ?
        ORDER BY date DESC, id DESC
    """, (f_id, f_id))
    operations = [dict(r) for r in cursor.fetchall()]
    conn.close()
    return {
        "fournisseur": dict(fournisseur),
        "nb_achats": nb_achats,
        "nb_paiements": nb_paiements,
        "total_achats": total_achats,
        "total_paye": total_paye,
        "dette": dette,
        "operations": operations
    }

def ajouter_fournisseur(code: str, nom: str, type_f: str = "Carburant", contact: str = "", tel: str = "", email: str = "", adresse: str = "", cond: str = "", solde_init: float = 0.0, statut: str = "Actif", montant_fidelite: float = 0.0, document: str = "", doc_categorie: str = "Autre", doc_description: str = "", notes: str = ""):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("""
        INSERT INTO fournisseurs (
            code, nom, type_fournisseur, contact_nom, telephone, 
            email, adresse, conditions_paiement, solde_initial, 
            montant_fidelite, document, doc_categorie, doc_description, 
            notes, statut, date_creation
        )
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, date('now'))
    """, (code, nom, type_f, contact, tel, email, adresse, cond, solde_init, montant_fidelite, document, doc_categorie, doc_description, notes, statut))
    new_id = cursor.lastrowid
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return new_id

def modifier_fournisseur(f_id: int, code: str, nom: str, type_f: str, contact: str, tel: str, email: str, adresse: str, cond: str, solde_init: float, statut: str):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE fournisseurs 
        SET code = ?, nom = ?, type_fournisseur = ?, contact_nom = ?, telephone = ?, email = ?, adresse = ?, conditions_paiement = ?, solde_initial = ?, statut = ?
        WHERE id = ?
    """, (code, nom, type_f, contact, tel, email, adresse, cond, solde_init, statut, f_id))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def supprimer_fournisseur(f_id: int):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("DELETE FROM achats_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = ?", (f_id,))
    cursor.execute("DELETE FROM reglements_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = ?", (f_id,))
    cursor.execute("DELETE FROM fournisseurs WHERE id = ?", (f_id,))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def ajouter_reglement_fournisseur(fournisseur_id: int, date: str, reference: str, mode: str, montant: float, banque: str = "", obs: str = ""):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("""
        INSERT INTO reglements_fournisseurs (fournisseur_id, date, reference_paiement, mode_paiement, montant, banque, observations)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, (fournisseur_id, date, reference, mode, montant, banque, obs))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# ========================================================
# GESTION DES UTILISATEURS (CONNEXION & PROFIL)
# ========================================================

def verifier_utilisateur(username: str, password_clair: str):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT id, username, nom_complet, password, role FROM utilisateurs WHERE username = ?", (username,))
    user = cursor.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    if user and verifier_mdp(password_clair, user["password"]):
        user_dict = dict(user)
        user_dict.pop("password", None)
        return user_dict
    return None

def lister_utilisateurs():
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM utilisateurs ORDER BY id DESC")
    users = [dict(u) for u in cursor.fetchall()]
    conn.close()
    return users

def get_utilisateur_by_id(user_id: int):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM utilisateurs WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
    row = cursor.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return dict(row) if row else None

def ajouter_utilisateur(username: str, nom_complet: str, password: str, role: str, cin: str = "", cnss: str = "", telephone: str = "", date_embauche: str = "", salaire_base: float = 0.0, adresse: str = "", statut: str = "ACTIF"):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute(
        """INSERT INTO utilisateurs 
        (username, nom_complet, password, role, cin, cnss, telephone, date_embauche, salaire_base, adresse, statut) 
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
        (username, nom_complet, hash_mdp(password), role, cin, cnss, telephone, date_embauche, salaire_base, adresse, statut)
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return True

def modifier_utilisateur(user_id: int, nom_complet: str, username: str, role: str, password: str = None, cin: str = "", cnss: str = "", telephone: str = "", date_embauche: str = "", salaire_base: float = 0.0, adresse: str = "", statut: str = "ACTIF"):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    if password and password.strip():
        cursor.execute(
            """UPDATE utilisateurs 
            SET nom_complet = ?, username = ?, role = ?, password = ?, 
                cin = ?, cnss = ?, telephone = ?, date_embauche = ?, 
                salaire_base = ?, adresse = ?, statut = ? 
            WHERE id = ?""",
            (nom_complet, username, role, hash_mdp(password), cin, cnss, telephone, date_embauche, salaire_base, adresse, statut, user_id)
        )
    else:
        cursor.execute(
            """UPDATE utilisateurs 
            SET nom_complet = ?, username = ?, role = ?, 
                cin = ?, cnss = ?, telephone = ?, date_embauche = ?, 
                salaire_base = ?, adresse = ?, statut = ? 
            WHERE id = ?""",
            (nom_complet, username, role, cin, cnss, telephone, date_embauche, salaire_base, adresse, statut, user_id)
        )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def supprimer_utilisateur(user_id: int):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("DELETE FROM utilisateurs WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

init_db()

# fusion_reader.py
import re
import io
import pandas as pd
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

def nettoyer_nombre(valeur):
    """Convertit une chaîne ou valeur brute en float en ignorant dates et heures."""
    if pd.isna(valeur) or valeur is None:
        return 0.0
    val_str = str(valeur).strip().replace('\xa0', '').replace(' ', '').replace(',', '.')
    if '#' in val_str or not val_str or val_str.lower() in ['nan', 'none', 'null', '--']:
        return 0.0
    
    # Exclusion stricte des dates et heures
    if '/' in val_str or ':' in val_str:
        return 0.0
    if re.match(r'^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}', val_str):
        return 0.0

    val_clean = re.sub(r'[^\d.]', '', val_str)
    try:
        return float(val_clean) if val_clean else 0.0
    except ValueError:
        return 0.0

def trouver_et_aligner_entetes(df):
    """Localise automatiquement la ligne des en-têtes réels."""
    mots_cles = ['fp', 'pump', 'pompe', 'fuel', 'grade', 'carburant', 'volume', 'sale', 'montant', 'amount', 'totalizer', 'nozzle', 'hose']
    
    matches_cols = sum(1 for c in df.columns if any(m == str(c).lower().strip() or m in str(c).lower().strip() for m in mots_cles))
    if matches_cols >= 2:
        return df

    for i in range(min(25, len(df))):
        row_vals = [str(val).lower().strip() for val in df.iloc[i].values if pd.notna(val)]
        matches = sum(1 for val in row_vals if any(m == val or m in val for m in mots_cles))
        if matches >= 2:
            new_df = df.iloc[i+1:].copy()
            new_df.columns = [str(val).strip() for val in df.iloc[i].values]
            return new_df

    return df

def extraire_donnees_dataframe(df):
    indexes = {"ESS": {}, "DIE": {}}
    volumes_vendus = {"ESS": {}, "DIE": {}}
    transactions_list = []

    if df is None or df.empty:
        return {"indexes": indexes, "volumes": volumes_vendus, "transactions": []}

    df = trouver_et_aligner_entetes(df)
    df.columns = [str(c).strip().lower() for c in df.columns]

    # Détection automatique des colonnes
    col_sale = next((c for c in df.columns if any(k in c for k in ['sale', 'trx', 'trans', 'seq', 'vente', 'ticket', 'n°'])), None)
    col_date = next((c for c in df.columns if 'date' in c), None)
    col_time = next((c for c in df.columns if any(k in c for k in ['time', 'heure'])), None)
    col_pump = next((c for c in df.columns if c in ['fp', 'pmp'] or any(k in c for k in ['pump', 'pompe', 'fueling point', 'pistolet', 'pos'])), None)
    col_fuel = next((c for c in df.columns if any(k in c for k in ['grade', 'fuel', 'carburant', 'produit', 'prod', 'article'])), None)
    
    col_vol = next((c for c in df.columns if any(k in c for k in ['volume', 'vol', 'quantit', 'litres', 'qte']) and 'tot' not in c and 'init' not in c and 'fin' not in c), None)
    col_money = next((c for c in df.columns if any(k in c for k in ['money', 'amount', 'montant', 'total', 'valeur', 'dh', 'prix']) and 'tot' not in c), None)
    
    col_initial = next((c for c in df.columns if ('date' not in c and 'time' not in c and 'heure' not in c) and any(k in c for k in ['start totalizer', 'start tot', 'initial_vol', 'vol_init', 'start_vol', 'vol initial', 'tot_init', 'vol. initial'])), None)
    col_final = next((c for c in df.columns if ('date' not in c and 'time' not in c and 'heure' not in c) and any(k in c for k in ['end totalizer', 'end tot', 'final_vol', 'vol_fin', 'end_vol', 'vol final', 'tot_vol', 'vol. final'])), None)

    cols = list(df.columns)

    if not col_fuel:
        for c in cols:
            val_exemples = " ".join(df[c].dropna().astype(str).head(20).values).upper()
            if any(k in val_exemples for k in ["SSP", "SP", "GASOIL", "DIESEL", "G10", "ESSENCE"]):
                col_fuel = c
                break

    if not col_pump:
        for c in cols:
            val_exemples = [nettoyer_nombre(v) for v in df[c].dropna().head(15).values]
            if val_exemples and all(1 <= v <= 32 for v in val_exemples if v > 0):
                col_pump = c
                break

    records = df.to_dict(orient='records')

    for row in records:
        try:
            p_val = nettoyer_nombre(row.get(col_pump))
            if p_val <= 0 or p_val > 50:
                continue
            pump_id = int(p_val)

            f_val = str(row.get(col_fuel, '')).strip().upper()
            if any(k in f_val for k in ["SSP", "SP", "ESS", "SUPER", "SANS PLOMB", "95", "98"]):
                c_type = "ESS"
                carburant_nom = "Essence (SP)"
            elif any(k in f_val for k in ["GASOIL", "G10", "DIE", "DIESEL", "GO", "G50"]):
                c_type = "DIE"
                carburant_nom = "Gasoil 10"
            else:
                continue

            vol = nettoyer_nombre(row.get(col_vol, 0.0))
            money = nettoyer_nombre(row.get(col_money, 0.0)) if col_money else 0.0
            
            raw_sale = str(row.get(col_sale, '--')).strip()
            sale_id = raw_sale[:-2] if raw_sale.endswith('.0') else raw_sale
            
            date_vente = str(row.get(col_date, '')).strip()
            if ' ' in date_vente:
                date_vente = date_vente.split(' ')[0]
            heure_vente = str(row.get(col_time, '--:--')).strip()

            init_vol = nettoyer_nombre(row.get(col_initial, 0.0)) if col_initial else 0.0
            fin_vol = nettoyer_nombre(row.get(col_final, 0.0)) if col_final else 0.0

            if init_vol > 0 and fin_vol == 0 and vol > 0:
                fin_vol = round(init_vol + vol, 2)
            elif fin_vol > 0 and init_vol == 0 and vol > 0:
                init_vol = round(fin_vol - vol, 2)

            volumes_vendus[c_type][pump_id] = volumes_vendus[c_type].get(pump_id, 0.0) + vol

            transactions_list.append({
                "sale_id": sale_id,
                "date": date_vente,
                "end_date": date_vente,
                "heure": heure_vente,
                "end_time": heure_vente,
                "pump_id": pump_id,
                "type": c_type,
                "carburant": carburant_nom,
                "volume": round(vol, 2),
                "money": round(money, 2),
                "initial_vol": round(init_vol, 2),
                "final_vol": round(fin_vol, 2)
            })

            cand_index = fin_vol if fin_vol > 0 else (init_vol + vol)
            if cand_index > 0:
                cur_max = indexes[c_type].get(pump_id, 0.0)
                if cand_index > cur_max:
                    indexes[c_type][pump_id] = round(cand_index, 2)

        except Exception:
            continue

    resultat_indexes = {"ESS": {}, "DIE": {}}
    for c_type in ["ESS", "DIE"]:
        for p_id in volumes_vendus[c_type]:
            if p_id in indexes[c_type] and indexes[c_type][p_id] > 0:
                resultat_indexes[c_type][p_id] = indexes[c_type][p_id]
            else:
                resultat_indexes[c_type][p_id] = round(volumes_vendus[c_type][p_id], 2)

    return {
        "indexes": resultat_indexes,
        "volumes": volumes_vendus,
        "transactions": transactions_list
    }

def parse_fusion_text(text: str):
    """Analyse les données textuelles (TSV / CSV / Presse-papiers)."""
    if not text or not text.strip():
        return {"indexes": {"ESS": {}, "DIE": {}}, "transactions": []}
    text = text.replace('\x00', '').strip()
    first_line = text.splitlines()[0]
    sep = '\t' if '\t' in first_line else (';' if ';' in first_line else ',')
    try:
        df = pd.read_csv(io.StringIO(text), sep=sep, header=None, engine='python', on_bad_lines='skip', dtype=str)
        return extraire_donnees_dataframe(df)
    except Exception:
        return {"indexes": {"ESS": {}, "DIE": {}}, "transactions": []}

def parse_xml_spreadsheet_2003(content: bytes):
    """Décodeur XML Spreadsheet 2003 pour Fusion."""
    try:
        root = ET.fromstring(content)
        rows_data = []
        for row in root.iter():
            if row.tag.endswith('Row'):
                row_cells = []
                for cell in row:
                    if cell.tag.endswith('Cell'):
                        data_el = next((c for c in cell if c.tag.endswith('Data')), None)
                        val = data_el.text if data_el is not None and data_el.text else ""
                        row_cells.append(val)
                if row_cells and any(row_cells):
                    rows_data.append(row_cells)
        if rows_data:
            df = pd.DataFrame(rows_data)
            return extraire_donnees_dataframe(df)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None

def parse_fusion_transactions(file_content: bytes):
    """Analyse tous les formats de fichiers Fusion (XML, XLS, XLSX, HTML, CSV)."""
    res_xml = parse_xml_spreadsheet_2003(file_content)
    if res_xml and res_xml["transactions"]:
        return res_xml

    try:
        excel_file = pd.ExcelFile(io.BytesIO(file_content))
        for sheet_name in excel_file.sheet_names:
            df = excel_file.parse(sheet_name, header=None, dtype=str)
            res = extraire_donnees_dataframe(df)
            if res["transactions"]:
                return res
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        tables = pd.read_html(io.BytesIO(file_content), header=None)
        if tables:
            for t in tables:
                res = extraire_donnees_dataframe(t.astype(str))
                if res["transactions"]:
                    return res
    except Exception:
        pass

    text = None
    for enc in ['utf-8-sig', 'utf-8', 'latin-1', 'cp1252', 'iso-8859-1']:
        try:
            text = file_content.decode(enc)
            break
        except Exception:
            continue
    if text is None:
        text = file_content.decode('latin-1', errors='ignore')

    return parse_fusion_text(text)

import os
import urllib.parse
from pathlib import Path
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse, FileResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
import uvicorn

from database import init_db
from security import verifier_session  # <-- Import depuis security

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

# ================= 1. PERMISSIONS PAR RÔLE =================
PERMISSIONS = {
    "admin": ["*"],
    "administrateur": ["*"],
    "comptable": ["/", "/rapport-quart", "/clients", "/prix", "/notes", "/fournisseurs", "/achats", "/releve", "/factures", "/encaissements", "/centre-rapports", "/api"],
    "employe": ["/rapport-quart", "/releve"]
}

# ================= 2. MIDDLEWARE D'AUTHENTIFICATION =================
class AuthMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next):
        path = request.url.path
        
        if path in ["/login", "/logout", "/favicon.ico"] or path.startswith("/static"):
            return await call_next(request)
        
        cookie_session = request.cookies.get("session_user")
        user = verifier_session(cookie_session)
        
        if not user:
            response = RedirectResponse(url="/login", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)
            response.delete_cookie("session_user")
            return response
        
        role = str(user.get("role", "employe")).lower().strip()
        request.state.user = user

        if role in ["admin", "administrateur"]:
            return await call_next(request)
        
        allowed_routes = PERMISSIONS.get(role, [])
        is_allowed = any(path == r or (r != "/" and path.startswith(r)) for r in allowed_routes)

        if not is_allowed:
            if role == "employe" and path == "/":
                return RedirectResponse(url="/rapport-quart", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

            return HTMLResponse(
                content=f"""
                <div style="text-align: center; margin-top: 100px; font-family: sans-serif;">
                    <h1 style="color: #e11d48; font-size: 2rem;">Accès Refusé (403)</h1>
                    <p style="color: #64748b;">Votre rôle <b>{role.upper()}</b> n'a pas accès à cette section.</p>
                    <a href="/rapport-quart" style="display: inline-block; margin-top: 15px; padding: 10px 20px; background: #0284c7; color: white; border-radius: 8px; text-decoration: none;">Retour au poste</a>
                </div>
                """, 
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN
            )

        return await call_next(request)

# ================= 3. INITIALISATION DE L'APPLICATION =================
@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    init_db()
    (BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "factures").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "clients").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "encaissements").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "fournisseurs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    yield

app = FastAPI(title="PETROLAZ - Station Service", lifespan=lifespan)
app.add_middleware(AuthMiddleware)

static_folder = BASE_DIR / "static"
static_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# SÉCURISATION CONTRE LE PATH TRAVERSAL / LFI
@app.get("/static/uploads/clients/{nom_fichier:path}")
async def servir_document_client(nom_fichier: str):
    nom_fichier = urllib.parse.unquote(nom_fichier)
    upload_client_dir = (BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "clients").resolve()
    target_file = (upload_client_dir / nom_fichier).resolve()

    if not str(target_file).startswith(str(upload_client_dir)):
        return HTMLResponse("Accès interdit", status_code=403)

    if target_file.is_file():
        ext = target_file.suffix.lower()
        media_types = {
            ".pdf": "application/pdf",
            ".png": "image/png",
            ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
            ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
            ".webp": "image/webp"
        }
        return FileResponse(str(target_file), media_type=media_types.get(ext, "application/octet-stream"))

    return HTMLResponse("Document introuvable sur le disque", status_code=404)

# Montage static
app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(static_folder)), name="static")

# ==================================================
# FICHIER : .\security.py
# ==================================================

import os
import json
import time
import hmac
import hashlib
import base64

SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("PETROLAZ_SECRET_KEY", "station_petrolaz_secret_key_secure_2026").encode("utf-8")
SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400 * 7  # 7 jours

def signer_session(data: dict) -> str:
    payload = data.copy()
    payload["exp"] = int(time.time()) + SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS
    json_bytes = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
    payload_b64 = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(json_bytes).decode("utf-8")
    signature = hmac.new(SECRET_KEY, payload_b64.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
    return f"{payload_b64}.{signature}"

def verifier_session(cookie_str: str) -> dict | None:
    if not cookie_str or "." not in cookie_str:
        return None
    try:
        payload_b64, signature = cookie_str.rsplit(".", 1)
        expected_sig = hmac.new(SECRET_KEY, payload_b64.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(signature, expected_sig):
            return None
        json_bytes = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(payload_b64.encode("utf-8"))
        data = json.loads(json_bytes.decode("utf-8"))
        if data.get("exp", 0) < int(time.time()):
            return None
        return data
    except Exception:
        return None

def get_mastation_logo(height: int = 48) -> str:
    """
    Génère le logo vectoriel SVG haute visibilité :
    - Écusson 3D dynamique avec facettes Or royal et Platine ultra-brillant
    - Halo de lumière (Glow doré) pour une lisibilité parfaite à distance
    - Typographie percutante : 'Ma' blanc pur & 'Station' Or intense
    - Sous-titre officiel 'PETROLAZ' rehaussé
    """
    width = int(height * (340 / 76))
    return f"""
    <svg viewBox="0 0 340 76" height="{height}" width="{width}" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="select-none inline-block overflow-visible">
        <defs>
            <linearGradient id="goldVividMS" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="90%">
                <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#FFF5D6" />
                <stop offset="25%" stop-color="#FBBF24" />
                <stop offset="60%" stop-color="#F59E0B" />
                <stop offset="85%" stop-color="#B45309" />
                <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#FDE68A" />

        </g>
    </svg>
    """


# ==============================================================================
# 2. INTERFACE DE CONNEXION / LOGIN (SECOURS / TEMPLATE UI)
# ==============================================================================
def login_page(error: str = None) -> str:
    error_banner = f"""
    <div class="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/20 border border-rose-500/40 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-200 text-xs font-semibold text-center animate-fadeIn">
        {html.escape(error)}
    </div>
    """ if error else ""

    return f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="fr" class="h-full">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
    <title>Connexion - MaStation</title>
    
    <link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 64 64'%3E%3Crect x='2' y='2' width='60' height='60' rx='16' fill='%23082232' stroke='%23C08B3E' stroke-width='2.5'/%3E%3Ctext x='32' y='44' text-anchor='middle' font-family='Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif' font-weight='900' font-size='30' letter-spacing='-1'%3E%3Ctspan fill='%23E5A842'%3EM%3C/tspan%3E%3Ctspan fill='%23FFFFFF'%3ES%3C/tspan%3E%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E">

    <script>
        if (localStorage.theme === 'dark' || (!('theme' in localStorage) && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches)) {{
            document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
        }} else {{
            document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
        }}
    </script>

    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <script>
        tailwind.config = {{
            darkMode: 'class',
            theme: {{
                extend: {{
                    colors: {{
                        brand: {{
                            gold: '#E5A842',
                            'gold-hover': '#f5b752',
                            dark: '#0A1622',
                            petrol: '#184E68',
                            bg: '#EEF2F6',
                            'bg-dark': '#060C12'
                        }}
                    }},
                    keyframes: {{
                        fadeIn: {{
                            '0%': {{ opacity: '0', transform: 'translateY(-4px)' }},
                            '100%': {{ opacity: '1', transform: 'translateY(0)' }},
                        }}
                    }},
                    animation: {{
                        fadeIn: 'fadeIn 0.15s ease-out forwards',
                    }}
                }}
            }}
        }}
    </script>
    
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest"></script>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        body {{
            font-family: 'Plus Jakarta Sans', sans-serif;
            background-color: #EEF2F6;
            background-image: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(238, 242, 246, 0.40) 0%, rgba(203, 213, 225, 0.60) 100%), 
                              url('/static/station_bg_Claire.jpg');
            background-size: cover;
            background-position: center center;
            background-repeat: no-repeat;
            background-attachment: fixed;
            transition: background 0.3s ease-in-out;
        }}

        html.dark body {{
            background-color: #060C12;
            background-image: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(6, 12, 18, 0.65) 0%, rgba(3, 7, 12, 0.85) 100%), 
                              url('/static/station_bg_sombre.png');
            background-size: cover;
            background-position: center center;
            background-repeat: no-repeat;
            background-attachment: fixed;
        }}
    </style>
</head>
<body class="h-full flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4 relative text-slate-800 dark:text-white select-none">

    <div class="absolute top-5 right-5 z-50">
        <button type="button" onclick="toggleTheme()" class="px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/75 dark:bg-[#102434]/65 backdrop-blur-xl text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-[#18354c] border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10 transition shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer" title="Changer de thème">
            <i data-lucide="sun" class="w-4 h-4 hidden dark:block text-amber-400"></i>
            <i data-lucide="moon" class="w-4 h-4 block dark:hidden text-slate-700"></i>
            <span class="text-xs font-bold hidden sm:inline text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Mode</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <div class="w-full max-w-[420px] p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl bg-white/85 dark:bg-[#0A1622]/80 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/80 dark:border-white/10 shadow-2xl transition-all duration-200">
        
        <div class="flex justify-center mb-6">
            {get_mastation_logo(height=58)}
        </div>

        {error_banner}

        <form action="/login" method="POST" class="space-y-4">
            <div class="space-y-1.5">
                <div class="relative flex items-center">
                    <i data-lucide="user" class="absolute left-3.5 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400"></i>
                    <input type="text" name="username" required placeholder="admin" autocomplete="username"
                           class="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl bg-blue-50/70 dark:bg-[#132332]/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 text-slate-900 dark:text-white placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#E5A842] transition shadow-inner">
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="space-y-1.5">
                <div class="relative flex items-center">
                    <i data-lucide="lock" class="absolute left-3.5 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400"></i>
                    <input type="password" id="passwordField" name="password" required placeholder="••••••••" autocomplete="current-password"
                           class="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-3 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl bg-blue-50/70 dark:bg-[#132332]/90 border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 text-slate-900 dark:text-white placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#E5A842] transition shadow-inner">
                    <button type="button" onclick="togglePasswordVisibility()" class="absolute right-3.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-white transition" title="Afficher/Masquer le mot de passe">
                        <i data-lucide="eye" id="eyeIcon" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <button type="submit" class="w-full mt-2 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0284C7] to-[#0369A1] dark:from-[#EA580C] dark:to-[#F59E0B] text-white font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xs shadow-lg shadow-sky-500/25 dark:shadow-orange-500/25 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-95 cursor-pointer">
                <span>Se connecter</span>
                <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </button>
        </form>
    </div>

    <script>
        function toggleTheme() {{
            const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark');
            if (isDark) {{
                document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
                localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
            }} else {{
                document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
                localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
            }}
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function togglePasswordVisibility() {{
            const input = document.getElementById('passwordField');
            const icon = document.getElementById('eyeIcon');
            if (input.type === 'password') {{
                input.type = 'text';
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'eye-off');
            }} else {{
                input.type = 'password';
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'eye');
            }}
            lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }});
        if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
    </script>
</body>
</html>"""


# ==============================================================================
# 3. LAYOUT PRINCIPAL AVEC HARMONISATION DU SIDEBAR & NAVBAR
# ==============================================================================
def layout(content: str, active_tab: str = "supervision", request: Request = None):
    user = getattr(request.state, "user", None) if request else None

    role = (user.get("role", "employe") if user else "employe").upper()
    nom_complet = (user.get("nom_complet", "Safouane") if user else "Safouane").title()
    initiale = nom_complet[0].upper() if nom_complet else "S"

    is_admin_or_cpt = role.lower() in ["admin", "administrateur", "comptable", "gerant"]

    # ------------------ TOP NAV ITEMS ------------------
    top_nav_items = ""
    is_calcul_active = active_tab in ['rapport', 'rapport-quart', 'supervision', 'compteurs', 'historique']
    
    if is_admin_or_cpt:
        top_nav_items += f"""
        <div class="relative group h-full flex items-center">
            <a href="/rapport-quart" class="nav-top-link {'active' if is_calcul_active else ''}">
                <i data-lucide="calculator" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                <span>Calcul du Jour</span>
            </a>
            
            <div class="absolute top-full left-0 w-60 pt-2 hidden group-hover:block z-50 animate-fadeIn">
                <div class="nav-dropdown-menu rounded-2xl p-2.5 space-y-1">
                    <a href="/rapport-quart" class="nav-sub-link {'active' if active_tab in ['supervision', 'rapport', 'rapport-quart'] else ''}">
                        <i data-lucide="shield-check" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        <span>Contrôle & Écarts</span>
                    </a>
                    <a href="/rapport-quart/compteurs" class="nav-sub-link {'active' if active_tab == 'compteurs' else ''}">
                        <i data-lucide="gauge" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        <span>Compteurs</span>
                    </a>
                    <a href="/rapport-quart/historique" class="nav-sub-link {'active' if active_tab == 'historique' else ''}">
                        <i data-lucide="history" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        <span>Historique des Quarts</span>
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        """
    else:
        top_nav_items += f"""
        <a href="/rapport-quart" class="nav-top-link {'active' if is_calcul_active else ''}">
            <i data-lucide="calculator" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Calcul du Jour</span>
        </a>
        """

    if is_admin_or_cpt:
        is_client_active = active_tab in ["clients", "factures", "encaissements"]
        top_nav_items += f"""
        <div class="relative group h-full flex items-center">
            <a href="/clients" class="nav-top-link {'active' if is_client_active else ''}">
                <i data-lucide="users" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                <span>Clients</span>
            </a>
            
            <div class="absolute top-full left-0 w-56 pt-2 hidden group-hover:block z-50 animate-fadeIn">
                <div class="nav-dropdown-menu rounded-2xl p-2.5 space-y-1">
                    <a href="/factures" class="nav-sub-link {'active' if active_tab == 'factures' else ''}">
                        <i data-lucide="file-text" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        <span>Factures</span>
                    </a>
                    <a href="/encaissements" class="nav-sub-link {'active' if active_tab == 'encaissements' else ''}">
                        <i data-lucide="wallet" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        <span>Encaissements</span>
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
        """

    if is_admin_or_cpt:
        is_achats_active = active_tab == 'achats'
        top_nav_items += f"""
        <a href="/achats" class="nav-top-link {'active' if is_achats_active else ''}">
            <i data-lucide="shopping-cart" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Achats</span>
        </a>
        """

    # ------------------ MOBILE BOTTOM NAV ------------------
    mobile_menu_items = f"""
    <a href="/rapport-quart" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 py-1 {'text-[#EA580C] font-bold' if is_calcul_active else 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400'}">
        <i data-lucide="calculator" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        <span class="text-[10px] mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap">Calcul</span>
    </a>
    <a href="/clients" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 py-1 {'text-[#EA580C] font-bold' if active_tab == 'clients' else 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400'}">
        <i data-lucide="users" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        <span class="text-[10px] mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap">Clients</span>
    </a>
    <a href="/factures" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 py-1 {'text-[#EA580C] font-bold' if active_tab == 'factures' else 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400'}">
        <i data-lucide="file-text" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        <span class="text-[10px] mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap">Factures</span>
    </a>
    <a href="/encaissements" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 py-1 {'text-[#EA580C] font-bold' if active_tab == 'encaissements' else 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400'}">
        <i data-lucide="wallet" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        <span class="text-[10px] mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap">Encaissements</span>
    </a>
    <a href="/achats" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 py-1 {'text-[#EA580C] font-bold' if active_tab == 'achats' else 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400'}">
        <i data-lucide="shopping-cart" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
        <span class="text-[10px] mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap">Achats</span>
    </a>
    """

    # ------------------ SIDEBAR DYNAMIQUE AÉRÉE & TEXTE BLANC / ORANGE ------------------
    sidebar_html = f"""
    <div id="sidebar-trigger-wrapper" class="group/sidebar shrink-0 flex h-full z-30 pt-16">
        <div class="w-3.5 h-full group-hover/sidebar:w-0 transition-all duration-300 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer">

        <aside id="sidebar-menu" class="w-0 opacity-0 group-hover/sidebar:w-64 group-hover/sidebar:opacity-100 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out overflow-hidden flex flex-col bg-white/95 dark:bg-[#0A1622] backdrop-blur-none border-r border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 p-0 group-hover/sidebar:p-5 shadow-2xl">
            <div class="w-56 flex flex-col h-full">
                
                <!-- Profil Utilisateur -->
                <div class="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100/80 dark:bg-[#102434] border border-slate-200 dark:border-white/10 shadow-xs mb-6 shrink-0">

    <script>
        function toggleTheme() {{
            const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains('dark');
            if (isDark) {{
                document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
                localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
            }} else {{
                document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
                localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
            }}
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}
        
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {{
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {{
                document.querySelectorAll('[id^="modal"]').forEach(m => m.classList.add('hidden'));
            }}
        }});

        // GESTION DU SCROLL INTELLIGENT
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();

            const navbar = document.getElementById('main-navbar');
            const scrollContainer = document.getElementById('main-content-scroll');
            
            if (navbar && scrollContainer) {{
                let lastScrollTop = 0;
                const deltaThreshold = 8;

                scrollContainer.addEventListener('scroll', () => {{
                    let currentScroll = scrollContainer.scrollTop;

                    if (Math.abs(lastScrollTop - currentScroll) <= deltaThreshold) return;

                    if (currentScroll > lastScrollTop && currentScroll > 25) {{
                        navbar.classList.add('navbar-hidden');
                    }} else if (currentScroll < lastScrollTop) {{
                        navbar.classList.remove('navbar-hidden');
                    }}

                    lastScrollTop = currentScroll <= 0 ? 0 : currentScroll;
                }}, {{ passive: true }});
            }}
        }});

        if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
    </script>
</body>
</html>"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import html
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Form, UploadFile, File, Query, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse

import database
from database import get_db_connection
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

UPLOAD_DIR = Path("static/uploads/factures")
UPLOAD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {".pdf", ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp"}
ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES = {"application/pdf", "image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/webp"}

def s_assurer_colonne_lignes_json():
    """Vérifie et ajoute la colonne lignes_json si absente."""
    try:
        conn = get_db_connection()
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE achats_fournisseurs ADD COLUMN lignes_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]'")
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    except Exception:
        pass

s_assurer_colonne_lignes_json()

def format_mad(valeur):
    """Formatage propre en MAD."""
    try:
        val = float(valeur or 0.0)
        return f"{val:,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",") + " MAD"
    except Exception:
        return "0,00 MAD"

def formater_date_fr(date_str: str) -> str:
    if not date_str:
        return "—"
    try:
        if "-" in date_str:
            p = date_str.split("-")
            if len(p) == 3:
                return f"{p[2]}/{p[1]}/{p[0]}"
    except Exception:
        pass
    return date_str

@router.get("/achats", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_achats(
    request: Request,
    date_debut: str = Query(""),
    date_fin: str = Query(""),
    fournisseur_id: int = Query(0),
    min_m: float = Query(0.0),
    max_m: float = Query(0.0)
):
    fournisseurs = database.get_fournisseurs()
    achats = database.get_achats_filtres(
        date_debut=date_debut if date_debut else None,
        date_fin=date_fin if date_fin else None,
        fournisseur_id=int(fournisseur_id) if fournisseur_id else None,
        min_montant=float(min_m) if min_m else None,
        max_montant=float(max_m) if max_m else None
    )

    total_ttc = sum(float(a.get("montant_total") or 0.0) for a in achats)
    total_ht = sum(float(a.get("montant_ht") or 0.0) for a in achats)
    total_tva = sum(float(a.get("montant_tva") or 0.0) for a in achats)
    nb_achats = len(achats)

    prochain_num = database.generer_prochain_numero_facture()
    today_iso = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    # Sélection automatique de OKAY ENERGY par défaut
    okay_energy_id = None
    for f in fournisseurs:
        if "OKAY" in f.get("nom", "").upper():
            okay_energy_id = f["id"]
            break

    # Options Fournisseurs
    fournisseurs_options_filter = '<option value="0"' + (' selected' if fournisseur_id == 0 else '') + '>Tous les fournisseurs</option>'
    fournisseurs_options_modal = ''

    for f in fournisseurs:
        f_nom_safe = html.escape(f.get("nom", ""))
        f_code_safe = html.escape(f.get("code", "FRN"))
        
        sel_filter = " selected" if fournisseur_id == f["id"] else ""
        fournisseurs_options_filter += f'<option value="{f["id"]}"{sel_filter}>{f_nom_safe}</option>'

        is_default = (f["id"] == okay_energy_id) or (okay_energy_id is None and f == fournisseurs[0])
        sel_modal = " selected" if is_default else ""
        fournisseurs_options_modal += f'<option value="{f["id"]}"{sel_modal}>{f_nom_safe} ({f_code_safe})</option>'

    # Tableau des achats
    rows_html = ""
    for a in achats:
        a_id = a.get("id")
        raw_date = a.get("date") or ""
        date_affichee = formater_date_fr(raw_date)
        fnom = a.get("fournisseur_nom") or "OKAY ENERGY"
        fcode = a.get("fournisseur_code") or "OKF"
        num_facture = a.get("numero_facture") or "FAC-DIRECT"
        fichier_path = a.get("fichier_facture") or ""
        montant_ht = float(a.get("montant_ht") or 0.0)
        montant_tva = float(a.get("montant_tva") or 0.0)
        montant_ttc = float(a.get("montant_total") or 0.0)
        taux_tva = float(a.get("taux_tva") or 10.0)
        notes = a.get("observations") or ""
        produit_principal = a.get("produit") or "Diesel"

        lignes_json_raw = a.get("lignes_json") or "[]"
        try:
            lignes_achetees = json.loads(lignes_json_raw)
        except Exception:
            lignes_achetees = []

        if not lignes_achetees:
            lignes_achetees = [{
                "produit": produit_principal,
                "citerne": "Citerne 1",
                "qte": float(a.get("volume_litres") or 0.0),
                "pu_ht": float(a.get("prix_unitaire") or 0.0),
                "montant_ht": montant_ht
            }]

        achat_json_data = {
            "id": a_id,
            "numero_facture": num_facture,
            "date": raw_date,
            "fournisseur_id": a.get("fournisseur_id"),
            "fournisseur_nom": fnom,
            "fournisseur_code": fcode,
            "montant_ht": montant_ht,
            "taux_tva": taux_tva,
            "montant_tva": montant_tva,
            "montant_total": montant_ttc,
            "observations": notes,
            "fichier_facture": fichier_path,
            "lignes": lignes_achetees
        }
        achat_json_escaped = html.escape(json.dumps(achat_json_data), quote=True)

        if fichier_path:
            is_pdf = fichier_path.lower().endswith(".pdf")
            icon_file = "file-text" if is_pdf else "image"
            badge_file = (
                f'<a href="{fichier_path}" target="_blank" onclick="event.stopPropagation()" '
                f'class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-sky-400 font-bold hover:underline mt-0.5">'
                f'<i data-lucide="{icon_file}" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Facture</a>'
            )
        else:
            badge_file = """
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 font-medium mt-0.5">
                <i data-lucide="file" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Facture
            </span>
            """

        search_blob = f"{num_facture} {fnom} {fcode} {notes}".lower()

        rows_html += f"""
        <tr onclick="afficherDetailAchat({achat_json_escaped})" 
            class="achat-row cursor-pointer border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/50 hover:bg-sky-50/50 dark:hover:bg-[#132738]/50 transition text-sm group"
            data-search="{html.escape(search_blob, quote=True)}"
            data-date="{raw_date}"
            data-fournisseur="{a.get('fournisseur_id', 0)}"
            data-ttc="{montant_ttc}">
            <td class="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 text-[13px] whitespace-nowrap">
                {date_affichee}
            </td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                <div class="font-extrabold text-[13.5px] text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400 group-hover:underline font-mono">{html.escape(num_facture)}</div>
                {badge_file}
            </td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                <span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-[#132738] border border-slate-200/80 dark:border-[#224563] text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs font-bold">
                    {html.escape(fnom)}
                </span>
            </td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-[#E5A842] text-[14.5px] whitespace-nowrap">
                {montant_ttc:,.2f} <span class="text-[11px] font-sans font-semibold text-slate-400">MAD</span>
            </td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right whitespace-nowrap" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
                <div class="relative inline-block text-left actions-dropdown-container">
                    <button type="button" 
                            onclick="toggleMenuAchat(event, 'menu-achat-{a_id}')" 
                            class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] dark:hover:bg-[#18354c] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center transition shadow-2xs cursor-pointer"
                            title="Actions">
                        <i data-lucide="more-vertical" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </button>

                    <div id="menu-achat-{a_id}" 
                         class="dropdown-menu-achat hidden absolute right-0 mt-1.5 w-44 rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-200/90 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-2xl py-1.5 z-50 text-left">
                        <button type="button" 
                                onclick="afficherDetailAchat({achat_json_escaped}); fermerTousLesMenusAchats();" 
                                class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-left text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 hover:bg-sky-50 dark:hover:bg-[#132738] flex items-center gap-2.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <i data-lucide="eye" class="w-4 h-4 text-sky-500"></i>
                            <span>Détails</span>
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" 
                                onclick="ouvrirModalModification({achat_json_escaped}); fermerTousLesMenusAchats();" 
                                class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-left text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-[#E5A842] hover:bg-amber-500/10 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-4 h-4 text-[#E5A842]"></i>
                            <span>Modifier</span>
                        </button>
                        <div class="my-1 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/60"></div>
                        <button type="button" 
                                onclick="confirmerSuppression({a_id}, '{html.escape(num_facture)}'); fermerTousLesMenusAchats();" 
                                class="w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-left text-xs font-bold text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-500/10 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500"></i>
                            <span>Supprimer</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>
        </tr>
        """

    if not rows_html:
        rows_html = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center py-12 text-slate-400 text-xs font-medium">Aucun achat enregistré.</td></tr>'

                <button type="button" onclick="fermerModalDetails()" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-white/10 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-white/20 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center transition">

                            <thead class="bg-slate-100 dark:bg-[#070E15] border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">

                <div class="flex justify-end pt-2">
