                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                            <span class="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold">Montant HT :</span>
                            <span id="da-ht" class="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">0,00 MAD</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                            <span id="da-tva-label" class="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold">TVA (10%) :</span>
                            <span id="da-tva" class="font-mono font-bold text-amber-500">0,00 MAD</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] pt-1.5 flex justify-between items-center">
                            <span class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs uppercase">Montant TTC :</span>
                            <span id="da-ttc" class="font-mono font-black text-base text-[#E5A842]">0,00 MAD</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div id="da-piece-container" class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] hidden">
                    <span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block mb-1">Pièce Jointe :</span>
                    <div id="da-piece-content"></div>
                </div>

                <div id="da-notes-container" class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] hidden">
                    <span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 block mb-0.5">Notes :</span>
                    <p id="da-notes" class="text-xs text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 italic"></p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="p-3.5 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] flex items-center justify-end">
                <button type="button" onclick="fermerModalDetails()" class="px-5 py-2 bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] hover:bg-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-[#1c3850] text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition cursor-pointer">
                    Fermer
                </button>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>
    """

    # Bloc JS non f-string : aucune erreur de syntaxe possible
    js_script = """
    <script>
        let lignesAchat = [];

        const LISTE_PRODUITS = [
            "Diesel",
            "Essence",
            "10W40",
            "15W40 CF4",
            "SAE 50",
            "Lavage",
            "Autre"
        ];

        const LISTE_CITERNES = [
            "Citerne 1",
            "Citerne 2",
            "Citerne 3",
            "Stock Lubrifiants",
            "Autre"
        ];

        function toggleMenuAchat(event, menuId) {
            event.stopPropagation();
            const targetMenu = document.getElementById(menuId);
            if (!targetMenu) return;

            const isHidden = targetMenu.classList.contains('hidden');
            fermerTousLesMenusAchats();

            if (isHidden) {
                targetMenu.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }

        function fermerTousLesMenusAchats() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-menu-achat').forEach(menu => {
                menu.classList.add('hidden');
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (!e.target.closest('.actions-dropdown-container')) {
                fermerTousLesMenusAchats();
            }
        });

        function filtrerAchatsEnDirect() {
            fermerTousLesMenusAchats();
            const q = (document.getElementById('live-search-q').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
            const dDebut = document.getElementById('live-date-debut').value;
            const dFin = document.getElementById('live-date-fin').value;
            const fournId = document.getElementById('live-filter-fournisseur').value;

            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.achat-row');
            let countVisible = 0;
            let sumTtc = 0;

            rows.forEach(row => {
                const searchBlob = row.getAttribute('data-search') || '';
                const rowDate = row.getAttribute('data-date') || '';
                const rowFourn = row.getAttribute('data-fournisseur') || '0';
                const ttc = parseFloat(row.getAttribute('data-ttc')) || 0;

                let match = true;

                if (q && !searchBlob.includes(q)) match = false;
                if (match && dDebut && rowDate && rowDate < dDebut) match = false;
                if (match && dFin && rowDate && rowDate > dFin) match = false;
                if (match && fournId !== "0" && rowFourn !== fournId) match = false;

                if (match) {
                    row.style.display = '';
                    countVisible++;
                    sumTtc += ttc;
                } else {
                    row.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('live-count-achats').innerText = countVisible;
            document.getElementById('live-total-ttc').innerText = sumTtc.toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) + ' MAD';

            const emptyEl = document.getElementById('achats-empty-state');
            if (emptyEl) {
                emptyEl.classList.toggle('hidden', countVisible > 0 || rows.length === 0);
            }
        }

        function ouvrirModalNouvelAchat() {
            document.getElementById('fa-id').value = "";
            document.getElementById('modal-form-title').innerText = "Nouvel Achat";
            document.getElementById('fa-btn-submit').innerText = "Enregistrer l'Achat";
            document.getElementById('fa-numero').value = PROCHAIN_NUM_DEFAULT;
            document.getElementById('fa-date').value = TODAY_ISO_DEFAULT;
            document.getElementById('fa-notes').value = "";
            document.getElementById('fa-taux-tva').value = "10";

            lignesAchat = [
                { produit: "Diesel", citerne: "Citerne 2", qte: 0, pu_ht: 0, montant_ht: 0 }
            ];
            renderLignesAchat();

            document.getElementById('modal-form-achat').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }

        function ouvrirModalModification(achat) {
            document.getElementById('fa-id').value = achat.id;
            document.getElementById('modal-form-title').innerText = "Modifier l'Achat " + achat.numero_facture;
            document.getElementById('fa-btn-submit').innerText = "Mettre à jour";
            document.getElementById('fa-numero').value = achat.numero_facture || "";
            document.getElementById('fa-date').value = achat.date || "";
            document.getElementById('fa-notes').value = achat.observations || "";
            document.getElementById('fa-taux-tva').value = String(achat.taux_tva || 10);
            
            if (achat.fournisseur_id) {
                document.getElementById('fa-fournisseur').value = achat.fournisseur_id;
            }

            if (achat.lignes && achat.lignes.length > 0) {
                lignesAchat = achat.lignes;
            } else {
                lignesAchat = [
                    { produit: "Diesel", citerne: "Citerne 2", qte: 1, pu_ht: achat.montant_ht, montant_ht: achat.montant_ht }
                ];
            }
            renderLignesAchat();

            document.getElementById('modal-form-achat').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }

        function fermerModalAchat() {
            document.getElementById('modal-form-achat').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function ajouterLigneAchat() {
            lignesAchat.push({
                produit: "Diesel",
                citerne: "Citerne 1",
                qte: 0,
                pu_ht: 0,
                montant_ht: 0
            });
            renderLignesAchat();
        }

        function supprimerLigneAchat(index) {
            if (lignesAchat.length > 1) {
                lignesAchat.splice(index, 1);
                renderLignesAchat();
            } else {
                alert("Vous devez avoir au moins une ligne d'achat.");
            }
        }

        function updateLigneVal(index, field, value) {
            lignesAchat[index][field] = value;
            if (field === 'qte' || field === 'pu_ht') {
                const qte = parseFloat(lignesAchat[index].qte) || 0;
                const pu = parseFloat(lignesAchat[index].pu_ht) || 0;
                lignesAchat[index].montant_ht = qte * pu;
            }
            renderLignesAchat();
        }

        function renderLignesAchat() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('fa-lignes-body');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';

            lignesAchat.forEach((ligne, idx) => {
                let optionsProduits = LISTE_PRODUITS.map(p => 
                    `<option value="${p}" ${p === ligne.produit ? 'selected' : ''}>${p}</option>`
                ).join('');

                let optionsCiternes = LISTE_CITERNES.map(c => 
                    `<option value="${c}" ${c === ligne.citerne ? 'selected' : ''}>${c}</option>`
                ).join('');

                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "text-xs";
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="p-2">
                        <select onchange="updateLigneVal(${idx}, 'produit', this.value)" class="w-full bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                            ${optionsProduits}
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td class="p-2">
                        <select onchange="updateLigneVal(${idx}, 'citerne', this.value)" class="w-full bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                            ${optionsCiternes}
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td class="p-2">
                        <input type="number" step="0.01" value="${ligne.qte}" oninput="updateLigneVal(${idx}, 'qte', this.value)" class="w-full bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 font-mono font-bold text-center text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                    </td>
                    <td class="p-2">
                        <input type="number" step="0.0001" value="${ligne.pu_ht}" oninput="updateLigneVal(${idx}, 'pu_ht', this.value)" class="w-full bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 font-mono font-bold text-right text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                    </td>
                    <td class="p-2 text-right font-mono font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                        ${(ligne.montant_ht || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                    </td>
                    <td class="p-2 text-center">
                        <button type="button" onclick="supprimerLigneAchat(${idx})" class="p-1 text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-600 cursor-pointer">
                            <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        </button>
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });

            calculerTotauxAchat();
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }

        function calculerTotauxAchat() {
            let totalHT = 0;
            lignesAchat.forEach(l => {
                totalHT += parseFloat(l.montant_ht) || 0;
            });

            const tauxTVA = parseFloat(document.getElementById('fa-taux-tva').value) || 0;
            const montantTVA = totalHT * (tauxTVA / 100);
            const montantTTC = totalHT + montantTVA;

            document.getElementById('fa-montant-ht').value = totalHT.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('fa-montant-tva').value = montantTVA.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('fa-hidden-ttc').value = montantTTC.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('fa-display-ttc').innerText = montantTTC.toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) + ' MAD';

            document.getElementById('fa-hidden-lignes').value = JSON.stringify(lignesAchat);
        }

        function afficherDetailAchat(a) {
            document.getElementById('da-num').innerText = "Détails : " + a.numero_facture;
            document.getElementById('da-date').innerText = "Date : " + a.date;
            document.getElementById('da-fournisseur').innerText = a.fournisseur_nom;
            document.getElementById('da-numero-facture').innerText = a.numero_facture;

            document.getElementById('da-ht').innerText = a.montant_ht.toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 }) + ' MAD';
            document.getElementById('da-tva-label').innerText = "TVA (" + a.taux_tva + "%) :";
            document.getElementById('da-tva').innerText = a.montant_tva.toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 }) + ' MAD';
            document.getElementById('da-ttc').innerText = a.montant_total.toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 }) + ' MAD';

            const tbody = document.getElementById('da-lignes-body');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            if (a.lignes && a.lignes.length > 0) {
                a.lignes.forEach(l => {
                    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                    tr.className = "text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200";
                    tr.innerHTML = `
                        <td class="p-2.5 font-bold">${l.produit || 'Produit'}</td>
                        <td class="p-2.5 text-center font-mono">${l.qte || 0}</td>
                        <td class="p-2.5 text-right font-mono">${(parseFloat(l.pu_ht) || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })} MAD</td>
                        <td class="p-2.5 text-right font-mono font-bold">${(parseFloat(l.montant_ht) || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })} MAD</td>
                        <td class="p-2.5 text-slate-500">${l.citerne || '-'}</td>
                    `;
                    tbody.appendChild(tr);
                });
            } else {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="p-4 text-center text-slate-400 italic">Aucun détail de ligne pour cet achat.</td></tr>';
            }

            const pieceCont = document.getElementById('da-piece-container');
            const pieceContent = document.getElementById('da-piece-content');
            if (a.fichier_facture && a.fichier_facture.trim() !== '') {
                pieceCont.classList.remove('hidden');
                pieceContent.innerHTML = `
                    <a href="${a.fichier_facture}" target="_blank" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-sky-500/10 text-sky-500 hover:bg-sky-500/20 rounded-xl font-bold transition">
                        <i data-lucide="external-link" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                        <span>Consulter la pièce jointe</span>
                    </a>
                `;
            } else {
                pieceCont.classList.add('hidden');
            }

            const notesCont = document.getElementById('da-notes-container');
            if (a.observations && a.observations.trim() !== '') {
                notesCont.classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('da-notes').innerText = a.observations;
            } else {
                notesCont.classList.add('hidden');
            }

            document.getElementById('modal-details-achat').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }

        function fermerModalDetails() {
            document.getElementById('modal-details-achat').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function confirmerSuppression(id, num) {
            if (confirm("Voulez-vous vraiment supprimer définitivement l'achat " + num + " ?")) {
                window.location.href = "/achats/supprimer/" + id;
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            filtrerAchatsEnDirect();
        });
    </script>
    """

    variables_js = f"""
    <script>
        const PROCHAIN_NUM_DEFAULT = "{prochain_num}";
        const TODAY_ISO_DEFAULT = "{today_iso}";
    </script>
    """

    content = html_markup + variables_js + js_script
    return HTMLResponse(content=layout(content, active_tab="achats", request=request))

@router.post("/achats/enregistrer")
async def action_enregistrer_achat(
    achat_id: Optional[str] = Form(None),
    fournisseur_id: int = Form(...),
    numero_facture: str = Form(""),
    date_achat: str = Form(...),
    montant_ht: float = Form(0.0),
    taux_tva: float = Form(10.0),
    montant_tva: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_total: float = Form(...),
    observations: str = Form(""),
    lignes_json: str = Form("[]"),
    fichier: UploadFile = File(None)
):
    fichier_facture = None
    if fichier and fichier.filename:
        ext = os.path.splitext(fichier.filename)[1].lower()
        content_type = (fichier.content_type or "").lower()

        if ext in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS or content_type in ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES:
            clean_num = "".join(c for c in numero_facture if c.isalnum() or c in ("-", "_")) or "FAC"
            nom_unique = f"facture_{int(time.time())}_{clean_num}{ext}"
            chemin_disque = UPLOAD_DIR / nom_unique
            
            with open(chemin_disque, "wb") as buffer:
                buffer.write(await fichier.read())
            fichier_facture = f"/static/uploads/factures/{nom_unique}"

    produit_principal = "Diesel"
    volume_total = 0.0
    pu_premier = 0.0
    try:
        lignes = json.loads(lignes_json)
        if lignes:
            produit_principal = lignes[0].get("produit", "Diesel")
            volume_total = sum(float(l.get("qte", 0.0)) for l in lignes)
            pu_premier = float(lignes[0].get("pu_ht", 0.0))
    except Exception:
        pass

    conn = get_db_connection()
    try:
        if achat_id and str(achat_id).strip().isdigit():
            aid = int(achat_id)
            if fichier_facture:
                conn.execute("""
                    UPDATE achats_fournisseurs 
                    SET fournisseur_id = ?, numero_facture = ?, date = ?, produit = ?, volume_litres = ?, prix_unitaire = ?,
                        montant_ht = ?, taux_tva = ?, montant_tva = ?, montant_total = ?, observations = ?, 
                        lignes_json = ?, fichier_facture = ?
                    WHERE id = ?
                """, (fournisseur_id, numero_facture.strip(), date_achat, produit_principal, volume_total, pu_premier, montant_ht, taux_tva, montant_tva, montant_total, observations.strip(), lignes_json, fichier_facture, aid))
            else:
                conn.execute("""
                    UPDATE achats_fournisseurs 
                    SET fournisseur_id = ?, numero_facture = ?, date = ?, produit = ?, volume_litres = ?, prix_unitaire = ?,
                        montant_ht = ?, taux_tva = ?, montant_tva = ?, montant_total = ?, observations = ?, 
                        lignes_json = ?
                    WHERE id = ?
                """, (fournisseur_id, numero_facture.strip(), date_achat, produit_principal, volume_total, pu_premier, montant_ht, taux_tva, montant_tva, montant_total, observations.strip(), lignes_json, aid))
        else:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO achats_fournisseurs 
                (fournisseur_id, numero_facture, date, produit, volume_litres, prix_unitaire, montant_ht, taux_tva, montant_tva, montant_total, observations, fichier_facture, lignes_json)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (fournisseur_id, numero_facture.strip(), date_achat, produit_principal, volume_total, pu_premier, montant_ht, taux_tva, montant_tva, montant_total, observations.strip(), fichier_facture or "", lignes_json))
        
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()

    return RedirectResponse(url="/achats", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.get("/achats/supprimer/{achat_id}")
async def action_supprimer_achat(achat_id: int):
    database.supprimer_achat(achat_id)
    return RedirectResponse(url="/achats", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

async def hub_rapports_page(request: Request):
    cards_html = ""
    for r in RAPPORTS_CONFIG:
        if r["disponible"]:
            badge_html = """

                <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"></span>

                Bientôt disponible
            </span>

        cards_html += f"""
        <a {click_attr} 
           class="group relative overflow-hidden bg-white/80 dark:bg-[#0D1624]/85 hover:dark:bg-[#121E31]/90 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-200/90 dark:border-white/10 hover:border-slate-400 dark:hover:border-white/25 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-lg hover:shadow-2xl hover:-translate-y-1 transition-all duration-200 flex flex-col justify-between">
            
            <div class="flex items-start gap-4">
                <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br {r['gradient']} flex items-center justify-center text-white shrink-0 shadow-md group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-200"
                     style="box-shadow: 0 8px 16px {r['glow']};">
                    <i data-lucide="{r['icone']}" class="w-6 h-6 stroke-[2.2]"></i>
                </div>

                <div class="space-y-1 pr-1">
                    <h3 class="text-sm font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight group-hover:text-sky-500 dark:group-hover:text-sky-400 transition-colors">
                        {r['titre']}
                    </h3>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 leading-snug line-clamp-2">
                        {r['description']}
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between">
                {badge_html}
                <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-sky-400 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-all"></i>
            </div>
        </a>
        """

    content = f"""
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 py-4 space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
        <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-3 border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/10">

                <h1 class="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <i data-lucide="bar-chart-3" class="w-5 h-5 text-sky-500"></i>
                    Centre de Rapports & Registres
                </h1>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    Accédez aux registres opérationnels et visualisez l'état comptable de la station.
                </p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="relative w-full sm:w-64">
                <i data-lucide="search" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"></i>
                <input type="text" id="reportSearch" placeholder="Rechercher un rapport..." 
                       onkeyup="filterReports()"
                       class="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-black/30 border border-slate-300 dark:border-white/10 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white placeholder-slate-400 outline-none focus:border-sky-500 transition">
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="reportsGrid" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {cards_html}
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function filterReports() {{
            const input = document.getElementById('reportSearch').value.toLowerCase();
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#reportsGrid > a');
            cards.forEach(card => {{
                const title = card.querySelector('h3').innerText.toLowerCase();
                const desc = card.querySelector('p').innerText.toLowerCase();
                if (title.includes(input) || desc.includes(input)) {{
                    card.classList.remove('hidden');
                }} else {{
                    card.classList.add('hidden');
                }}
            }});
        }}
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "centre_rapports", request=request))

# pages/clients.py
import json
import os
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request, Query, status, UploadFile, File
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse
from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection
from utils.client_helpers import generate_next_client_code

router = APIRouter()

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
UPLOAD_CLIENTS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "clients"
UPLOAD_CLIENTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(BASE_DIR / "templates"))

def sauvegarder_fichier_client(fichier: UploadFile, prefixe: str) -> str:
    if not fichier or not fichier.filename:
        return ""
    nom_fichier = os.path.basename(fichier.filename).replace(' ', '_')
    nom_securise = f"{prefixe}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S%f')}_{nom_fichier}"
    destination = UPLOAD_CLIENTS_DIR / nom_securise
    with open(destination, "wb") as f_out:
        shutil.copyfileobj(fichier.file, f_out)
    return f"/static/uploads/clients/{nom_securise}"

def formater_nom_client(type_client: str, nom_raw: str, prenom_raw: str = "") -> str:
    if type_client == "Particulier":
        nom_clean = nom_raw.strip().upper()
        prenom_clean = prenom_raw.strip().title()
        if prenom_clean:
            return f"{nom_clean} {prenom_clean}"
        parts = nom_clean.split()
        if len(parts) > 1:
            return f"{parts[0].upper()} {' '.join(parts[1:]).title()}"
        return nom_clean
    return nom_raw.strip().upper()

# ==========================================================
# 1. LISTE DES CLIENTS
# ==========================================================
@router.get("/clients", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def clients_page(
    request: Request, 
    q: str = Query(None), 
    filtre: str = Query("tous")
):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    query = """
        SELECT c.*, 
               COALESCE((SELECT SUM(montant) FROM client_encaissements WHERE client_id = c.id), 0.0) AS total_paye
        FROM clients c
        WHERE 1=1
    """
    params = []
    
    if q:
        query += " AND (c.code LIKE ? OR c.nom LIKE ? OR c.telephone LIKE ? OR c.ice LIKE ?)"
        params.extend([f"%{q}%", f"%{q}%", f"%{q}%", f"%{q}%"])
        
    if filtre == "actif":
        query += " AND c.statut = 'ACTIF'"
    elif filtre == "inactif":
        query += " AND c.statut = 'INACTIF'"
    elif filtre == "debiteur":
        query += " AND c.solde > 0"
        
    query += " ORDER BY c.nom ASC"

    clients_raw = conn.execute(query, params).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    clients = []
    for c in clients_raw:
        c_dict = dict(c)
        nom_raw = c_dict.get("nom") or "Sans Nom"
        solde = float(c_dict.get("solde") or 0.0)
        total_paye = float(c_dict.get("total_paye") or 0.0)

        clients.append({
            **c_dict,
            "solde": solde,
            "total_paye": total_paye,
            "search_blob": f"{nom_raw} {c_dict.get('code','')} {c_dict.get('type_client','')} {c_dict.get('telephone','')} {c_dict.get('ice','')}".lower()
        })

    template = templates.get_template("clients/clients_liste.html")
    inner_html = template.render({
        "request": request,
        "clients": clients,
        "q": q or "",
        "filtre": filtre,
        "auto_code": generate_next_client_code(),
        "total_clients": len(clients)
    })
    return HTMLResponse(layout(inner_html, "clients", request=request))

# ==========================================================
# 2. ACTIONS POST
# ==========================================================
@router.post("/clients/add")
async def add_client(
    code: str = Form(...), 
    type_client: str = Form("Entreprise"),
    nom_entreprise: str = Form(""),
    nom_particulier: str = Form(""),
    prenom_particulier: str = Form(""),
    telephone: str = Form(""), 
    ice: str = Form(""), 
    plafond: float = Form(0.0), 
    solde: float = Form(0.0),
    doc_rc_file: UploadFile = File(None),
    doc_statuts_file: UploadFile = File(None),
    doc_cin_file: UploadFile = File(None)
):
    nom_final = formater_nom_client("Particulier", nom_particulier, prenom_particulier) if type_client == "Particulier" else formater_nom_client("Entreprise", nom_entreprise)
    if not nom_final:
        nom_final = "SANS NOM"

    doc_rc = sauvegarder_fichier_client(doc_rc_file, "rc")
    doc_statuts = sauvegarder_fichier_client(doc_statuts_file, "statuts")
    doc_cin = sauvegarder_fichier_client(doc_cin_file, "cin")

    conn = get_db_connection()
    now = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
    try:
        conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO clients 
            (code, nom, type_client, telephone, ice, plafond, solde, statut, date_creation, doc_rc, doc_statuts, doc_cin)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIF', ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (code.strip().upper(), nom_final, type_client, telephone.strip(), ice.strip(), plafond, solde, now, doc_rc, doc_statuts, doc_cin))
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/clients", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/clients/edit-info")
async def edit_client_info(
    client_id: int = Form(...),
    code: str = Form(...),
    type_client: str = Form("Entreprise"),
    nom_entreprise: str = Form(""),
    nom_particulier: str = Form(""),
    prenom_particulier: str = Form(""),
    statut: str = Form("ACTIF"),
    telephone: str = Form(""),
    ice: str = Form(""),
    plafond: float = Form(0.0),
    solde: float = Form(0.0),
    doc_rc_file: UploadFile = File(None),
    doc_statuts_file: UploadFile = File(None),
    doc_cin_file: UploadFile = File(None)
):
    nom_final = formater_nom_client("Particulier", nom_particulier, prenom_particulier) if type_client == "Particulier" else formater_nom_client("Entreprise", nom_entreprise)
    if not nom_final:
        nom_final = "SANS NOM"

    conn = get_db_connection()
    try:
        cur_client = conn.execute("SELECT doc_rc, doc_statuts, doc_cin FROM clients WHERE id = ?", (client_id,)).fetchone()
        doc_rc = cur_client["doc_rc"] if cur_client else ""
        doc_statuts = cur_client["doc_statuts"] if cur_client else ""
        doc_cin = cur_client["doc_cin"] if cur_client else ""

        if doc_rc_file and doc_rc_file.filename:
            doc_rc = sauvegarder_fichier_client(doc_rc_file, "rc")
        if doc_statuts_file and doc_statuts_file.filename:
            doc_statuts = sauvegarder_fichier_client(doc_statuts_file, "statuts")
        if doc_cin_file and doc_cin_file.filename:
            doc_cin = sauvegarder_fichier_client(doc_cin_file, "cin")

        conn.execute("""
            UPDATE clients 
            SET code = ?, nom = ?, type_client = ?, statut = ?, telephone = ?, ice = ?, plafond = ?, solde = ?,
                doc_rc = ?, doc_statuts = ?, doc_cin = ?
            WHERE id = ?
        """, (code.strip().upper(), nom_final, type_client, statut, telephone.strip(), ice.strip(), plafond, solde, doc_rc, doc_statuts, doc_cin, client_id))
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url=f"/clients/{client_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/clients/delete")
async def action_supprimer_client(client_id: int = Form(...)):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    try:
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM client_encaissements WHERE client_id = ?", (client_id,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM factures_clients WHERE client_id = ?", (client_id,))
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM clients WHERE id = ?", (client_id,))
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/clients", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

# ==========================================================
# 3. DOSSIER CLIENT (HISTORIQUE DES FACTURES & RÈGLEMENTS)
# ==========================================================
@router.get("/clients/{client_id}", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def client_detail_page(request: Request, client_id: int, tab: str = Query("factures")):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    client = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM clients WHERE id = ?", (client_id,)).fetchone()
    if not client:
        conn.close()
        return RedirectResponse(url="/clients", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

    c_dict = dict(client)
    factures_raw = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM factures_clients WHERE client_id = ? ORDER BY date_facture DESC, id DESC", (client_id,)).fetchall()
    encaissements_raw = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM client_encaissements WHERE client_id = ? ORDER BY date_paiement DESC, id DESC", (client_id,)).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    factures = []
    for f in factures_raw:
        f_dict = dict(f)
        lignes_brutes = f_dict.get('lignes_json') or '[]'
        try:
            lignes_obj = json.loads(lignes_brutes) if isinstance(lignes_brutes, str) else lignes_brutes
        except Exception:
            lignes_obj = []

        f_payload = {
            "id": f_dict["id"],
            "numero_facture": f_dict.get("numero_facture", ""),
            "date_facture": f_dict.get("date_facture", ""),
            "montant_ht": float(f_dict.get("montant_ht") or 0.0),
            "montant_tva": float(f_dict.get("montant_tva") or 0.0),
            "montant_ttc": float(f_dict.get("montant_ttc") or 0.0),
            "lignes": lignes_obj
        }
        factures.append({
            **f_dict,
            "montant_ht": float(f_dict.get('montant_ht') or 0.0),
            "montant_tva": float(f_dict.get('montant_tva') or 0.0),
            "montant_ttc": float(f_dict.get('montant_ttc') or 0.0),
            "periode_str": f"{f_dict.get('date_debut')} au {f_dict.get('date_fin')}" if f_dict.get('date_debut') else "Direct",
            "json_payload": json.dumps(f_payload)
        })

    encaissements = []
    for e in encaissements_raw:
        e_dict = dict(e)
        fichier_url = f"/{e_dict['fichier']}" if e_dict.get('fichier') else ""
        e_payload = {
            "id": e_dict["id"],
            "date_paiement": e_dict.get("date_paiement", ""),
            "montant": float(e_dict.get("montant", 0.0)),
            "mode_paiement": e_dict.get("mode_paiement", "Espèces"),
            "reference": e_dict.get("reference", ""),
            "description": e_dict.get("description", ""),
            "fichier": fichier_url
        }
        encaissements.append({
            **e_dict,
            "montant": float(e_dict.get("montant") or 0.0),
            "fichier_url": fichier_url,
            "json_payload": json.dumps(e_payload)
        })

    total_factures = sum(f['montant_ttc'] for f in factures)
    total_encaisse = sum(e['montant'] for e in encaissements)

    template = templates.get_template("clients/client_detail.html")
    inner_html = template.render({
        "request": request,
        "client": c_dict,
        "tab": tab,
        "factures": factures,
        "encaissements": encaissements,
        "total_factures": total_factures,
        "total_encaisse": total_encaisse,
        "solde_actuel": float(c_dict.get('solde') or 0.0)
    })
    return HTMLResponse(layout(inner_html, "clients", request=request))

FICHIER : .\pages\encaissements.py

import time
import shutil
import os
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request, UploadFile, File, Query, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse
from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection
from utils.client_helpers import nombre_en_lettres

router = APIRouter()

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(BASE_DIR / "templates"))

UPLOAD_DIR = BASE_DIR / "static" / "uploads" / "encaissements"
UPLOAD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
ALLOWED_EXT = {".pdf", ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp"}

def ensure_schema(conn):
    try:
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE client_encaissements ADD COLUMN client_nom_libre TEXT DEFAULT ''")
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass

def formater_date_fr(date_str: str) -> str:
    if not date_str:
        return "—"
    try:
        if "-" in date_str:
            p = date_str.split("-")
            if len(p) == 3:
                return f"{p[2]}/{p[1]}/{p[0]}"
    except Exception:
        pass
    return date_str

# ==========================================================
# 1. PAGE GLOBALE : TOUS LES ENCAISSEMENTS
# ==========================================================
@router.get("/encaissements", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_tous_les_encaissements(
    request: Request,
    q: str = Query(None),
    date_debut: str = Query(None),
    date_fin: str = Query(None)
):
    today_dt = datetime.now()
    default_debut = (today_dt - timedelta(days=7)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    default_fin = today_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    val_debut = date_debut if date_debut is not None else default_debut
    val_fin = date_fin if date_fin is not None else default_fin

    conn = get_db_connection()
    ensure_schema(conn)

    query = """
        SELECT e.*, 
               COALESCE(NULLIF(c.nom, ''), NULLIF(e.client_nom_libre, ''), 'Client Passager') as client_nom, 
               COALESCE(c.code, 'PASSAGER') as client_code, 
               c.ice as client_ice, 
               c.telephone as client_telephone, 
               c.adresse as client_adresse
        FROM client_encaissements e
        LEFT JOIN clients c ON e.client_id = c.id
        ORDER BY e.date_paiement DESC, e.id DESC
    """
    encaissements_rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()
    clients_rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, code, nom, solde, ice, telephone FROM clients WHERE statut = 'ACTIF' ORDER BY nom ASC").fetchall()
    conn.close()

    # Préparation des clients
    clients = []
    for c in clients_rows:
        cd = dict(c)
        clients.append({
            "id": cd["id"],
            "code": cd.get("code") or "—",
            "nom": cd.get("nom") or "",
            "solde": float(cd.get("solde") or 0.0),
            "telephone": cd.get("telephone") or "",
            "ice": cd.get("ice") or ""
        })

    # Préparation des encaissements
    encaissements = []
    for e in encaissements_rows:
        e_dict = dict(e)
        cid = int(e_dict.get('client_id') or 0)
        is_passager = (cid == 0)
        raw_date = e_dict.get('date_paiement') or ''
        client_nom_val = e_dict.get('client_nom') or 'Client Passager'
        mode_val = e_dict.get('mode_paiement') or 'Espèces'
        fichier_url = f"/{e_dict['fichier']}" if e_dict.get('fichier') else ""

        encaissement_payload = {
            "id": e_dict.get("id"),
            "client_id": cid,
            "client_nom": client_nom_val,
            "client_code": e_dict.get("client_code") or ("PASSAGER" if is_passager else "—"),
            "client_ice": e_dict.get("client_ice") or "—",
            "client_tel": e_dict.get("client_telephone") or "—",
            "date_paiement": raw_date,
            "montant": float(e_dict.get("montant") or 0.0),
            "mode_paiement": mode_val,
            "banque": e_dict.get("banque") or "—",
            "reference": e_dict.get("reference") or "—",
            "description": e_dict.get("description") or "—",
            "operateur": e_dict.get("operateur") or "Safouane",
            "fichier": fichier_url
        }

        search_blob = f"{client_nom_val} {e_dict.get('client_code', '')} {mode_val} {e_dict.get('reference', '')} {e_dict.get('description', '')}".lower()

        encaissements.append({
            **e_dict,
            "client_nom": client_nom_val,
            "is_passager": is_passager,
            "date_affichee": formater_date_fr(raw_date),
            "fichier_url": fichier_url,
            "search_blob": search_blob,
            "json_payload": json.dumps(encaissement_payload)
        })

    template = templates.get_template("factures/encaissements_liste.html")
    inner_html = template.render({
        "request": request,
        "encaissements": encaissements,
        "clients": clients,
        "val_debut": val_debut,
        "val_fin": val_fin,
        "today_iso": today_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
        "q": q or ""
    })
    return HTMLResponse(layout(inner_html, "encaissements", request=request))

# ==========================================================
# 2. ACTIONS CRUD & IMPRESSION
# ==========================================================
@router.post("/encaissements/ajouter")
@router.post("/clients/encaissement/add")
async def add_encaissement(
    request: Request,
    client_id: Optional[str] = Form("0"),
    client_nom_libre: str = Form(""),
    date_paiement: str = Form(...),
    montant: float = Form(...),
    mode_paiement: str = Form("Espèces"),
    reference: str = Form(""),
    description: str = Form(""),
    redirect_url: str = Form(""),
    fichier: UploadFile = File(None)
):
    user = getattr(request.state, "user", None)
    nom_op = user.get("nom_complet", "Safouane") if user else "Safouane"

    file_path = ""
    if fichier and fichier.filename:
        ext = os.path.splitext(fichier.filename)[1].lower()
        if ext in ALLOWED_EXT:
            filename = f"{int(time.time())}_{fichier.filename.replace(' ', '_')}"
            dest = UPLOAD_DIR / filename
            with dest.open("wb") as buffer:
                shutil.copyfileobj(fichier.file, buffer)
            file_path = f"static/uploads/encaissements/{filename}"

    cid = int(client_id) if client_id and str(client_id).isdigit() else 0

    conn = get_db_connection()
    ensure_schema(conn)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
        conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO client_encaissements 
            (client_id, client_nom_libre, date_paiement, mode_paiement, reference, description, montant, fichier, operateur, statut)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'Validé')
        """, (cid, client_nom_libre.strip(), date_paiement, mode_paiement, reference.strip(), description.strip(), montant, file_path, nom_op))

        if cid > 0:
            conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde - ? WHERE id = ?", (montant, cid))
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()

    if redirect_url:
        return RedirectResponse(url=redirect_url, status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)
    if cid > 0:
        return RedirectResponse(url=f"/clients/{cid}?tab=encaissements", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)
    return RedirectResponse(url="/encaissements", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/clients/encaissement/delete")
async def delete_encaissement(encaissement_id: int = Form(...), client_id: int = Form(0)):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    ensure_schema(conn)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
        e = conn.execute("SELECT montant, client_id FROM client_encaissements WHERE id = ?", (encaissement_id,)).fetchone()
        if e:
            montant = float(e['montant'] or 0.0)
            cid = int(e['client_id'] or 0)
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM client_encaissements WHERE id = ?", (encaissement_id,))
            if cid > 0:
                conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde + ? WHERE id = ?", (montant, cid))
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()

    if client_id > 0:
        return RedirectResponse(url=f"/clients/{client_id}?tab=encaissements", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)
    return RedirectResponse(url="/encaissements", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.get("/encaissements/{encaissement_id}/imprimer", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def imprimer_recu_encaissement(request: Request, encaissement_id: int):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    ensure_schema(conn)
    encaissement = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM client_encaissements WHERE id = ?", (encaissement_id,)).fetchone()
    if not encaissement:
        conn.close()
        return HTMLResponse("Encaissement introuvable.", status_code=404)

    e_dict = dict(encaissement)
    client_id = int(e_dict.get("client_id") or 0)
    client = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM clients WHERE id = ?", (client_id,)).fetchone() if client_id > 0 else None
    conn.close()

    nom_passager = e_dict.get("client_nom_libre") or "Client Passager / Particulier"
    c_dict = dict(client) if client else {
        "nom": nom_passager,
        "code": "PASSAGER",
        "telephone": "—",
        "ice": "—",
        "adresse": "Nador"
    }

    date_raw = str(e_dict.get("date_paiement") or "")
    date_fr = formater_date_fr(date_raw)

    montant = float(e_dict.get("montant") or 0.0)
    lettres = nombre_en_lettres(montant).strip()
    if "DIRHAM" not in lettres.upper():
        lettres = f"{lettres} DIRHAMS"

    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request=request,
        name="factures/recu_encaissement.html",
        context={
            "e": e_dict,
            "client": c_dict,
            "date_fr": date_fr,
            "montant_en_lettres": lettres,
            "cache_buster": datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")
        }
    )

import re
import json
import html
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request, Query, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse, JSONResponse
from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection, get_prix_carburant_a_date
from utils.client_helpers import nombre_en_lettres

router = APIRouter()

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(BASE_DIR / "templates"))

def formater_date_fr(date_str: str) -> str:
    if not date_str:
        return "—"
    try:
        if "-" in date_str:
            p = date_str.split("-")
            if len(p) == 3:
                return f"{p[2]}/{p[1]}/{p[0]}"
    except Exception:
        pass
    return date_str

# ==========================================================
@router.get("/factures", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_toutes_les_factures(
    request: Request,
    q: str = Query(None),
    date_debut: str = Query(None),
    date_fin: str = Query(None)
):
    today_dt = datetime.now()
    default_debut = (today_dt - timedelta(days=7)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    default_fin = today_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    val_debut = date_debut if date_debut is not None else default_debut
    val_fin = date_fin if date_fin is not None else default_fin

    conn = get_db_connection()
    query = """
        SELECT f.*, c.nom as client_nom, c.code as client_code, c.ice as client_ice, 
               c.telephone as client_telephone, c.adresse as client_adresse
        FROM factures_clients f
        LEFT JOIN clients c ON f.client_id = c.id
        ORDER BY f.date_facture DESC, f.id DESC
    """
    factures_rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()
    clients_rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM clients ORDER BY nom ASC").fetchall()

    last_fact = conn.execute("SELECT numero_facture FROM factures_clients ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    prochain_numero = "0001"
    if last_fact and last_fact['numero_facture']:
        digits = re.findall(r'\d+', str(last_fact['numero_facture']))
        if digits:
            prochain_numero = f"{int(digits[-1]) + 1:04d}"

    conn.close()

    # Préparation des clients
    clients = []
    for c in clients_rows:
        cd = dict(c)
        clients.append({
            "id": cd.get("id"),
            "code": cd.get("code") or "—",
            "nom": cd.get("nom") or "",
            "ice": cd.get("ice") or "",
            "telephone": cd.get("telephone") or cd.get("tel") or "",
            "adresse": cd.get("adresse") or "Nador",
            "mode_reglement": cd.get("mode_paiement") or "Virement"
        })

    # Préparation des factures
    factures = []
    for f in factures_rows:
        f_dict = dict(f)
        f_id = f_dict.get('id')
        cid = f_dict.get('client_id')
        is_passager = not bool(cid)
        client_nom = f_dict.get('client_nom') or f_dict.get('destinataire_nom') or 'Passager / Divers'
        client_code = f_dict.get('client_code') or ('PASSAGER' if is_passager else '—')
        raw_date = f_dict.get('date_facture') or ''

        # Extraction détaillée des infos destinataire
        dest_info = f_dict.get('destinataire_info') or ''
        ice_val = f_dict.get('client_ice') or ''
        tel_val = f_dict.get('client_telephone') or ''
        adr_val = f_dict.get('client_adresse') or ''

        if not ice_val and 'ICE:' in dest_info.upper():
            m_ice = re.search(r'ICE\s*:\s*([^\s\-\|]+)', dest_info, re.IGNORECASE)
            if m_ice: ice_val = m_ice.group(1).strip()
        if not tel_val and 'TEL:' in dest_info.upper():
            m_tel = re.search(r'TEL\s*:\s*([^\s\-\|]+)', dest_info, re.IGNORECASE)
            if m_tel: tel_val = m_tel.group(1).strip()

        parts_info = dest_info.split(' - ')
        if len(parts_info) >= 4:
            adr_val = parts_info[-1].strip()
        if not adr_val:
            adr_val = "Nador"

        try:
            lignes_obj = json.loads(f_dict.get("lignes_json") or "[]")
        except Exception:
            lignes_obj = []

        facture_payload = {
            "id": f_id,
            "numero_facture": f_dict.get("numero_facture"),
            "date_facture": raw_date,
            "date_debut": f_dict.get("date_debut") or "",
            "date_fin": f_dict.get("date_fin") or "",
            "client_id": cid or "",
            "destinataire_nom": f_dict.get("destinataire_nom") or client_nom,
            "destinataire_ice": ice_val,
            "destinataire_tel": tel_val,
            "destinataire_adresse": adr_val,
            "client_nom": client_nom,
            "client_code": client_code,
            "client_ice": ice_val or "—",
            "client_tel": tel_val or "—",
            "mode_paiement": f_dict.get("mode_paiement") or "Virement",
            "montant_ht": float(f_dict.get("montant_ht") or 0.0),
            "taux_tva": float(f_dict.get("taux_tva") or 0.0),
            "montant_tva": float(f_dict.get("montant_tva") or 0.0),
            "remise_pourcent": float(f_dict.get("remise_pourcent") or 0.0),
            "montant_remise": float(f_dict.get("montant_remise") or 0.0),
            "montant_ttc": float(f_dict.get("montant_ttc") or 0.0),
            "notes": f_dict.get("notes") or "",
            "lignes": lignes_obj
        }

        search_blob = f"{f_dict.get('numero_facture', '')} {client_nom} {client_code} {f_dict.get('destinataire_nom', '')} {f_dict.get('mode_paiement', '')}".lower()

        factures.append({
            **f_dict,
            "client_nom": client_nom,
            "client_code": client_code,
            "is_passager": is_passager,
            "date_affichee": formater_date_fr(raw_date),
            "search_blob": search_blob,
            "json_payload": json.dumps(facture_payload)
        })

    template = templates.get_template("factures/factures_liste.html")
    inner_html = template.render({
        "request": request,
        "factures": factures,
        "clients": clients,
        "prochain_numero": prochainNumeroFactureDefaut(prochain_numero),
        "val_debut": val_debut,
        "val_fin": val_fin,
        "today_iso": today_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
        "q": q or ""
    })
    return HTMLResponse(layout(inner_html, "factures", request=request))

def prochainNumeroFactureDefaut(num: str) -> str:
    return num

@router.get("/api/tarif-date")
async def api_tarif_date(produit: str, date_consom: str):
    p_cle = produit.strip()
    prix_fixes = {
        '10W40 (55 MAD)': 55.0,
        '10W40 (250 MAD)': 250.0,
        '15W40 CF4 (42 MAD)': 42.0,
        '15W40 CF4 (200 MAD)': 200.0,
        'SAE 50 (185 MAD)': 185.0,
        'Lavage (40 MAD)': 40.0,
        'Lavage': 40.0,
        'Autre': 0.0
    }
    if p_cle in prix_fixes:
        return JSONResponse({"produit": produit, "date": date_consom, "pu": prix_fixes[p_cle]})

    pu = get_prix_carburant_a_date(produit, date_consom)
    return JSONResponse({"produit": produit, "date": date_consom, "pu": pu})

@router.post("/factures/creer")
async def action_creer_facture(
    client_id: Optional[str] = Form(None),
    numero_facture: str = Form(...),
    date_facture: str = Form(...),
    date_debut: str = Form(""),
    date_fin: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_nom: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_ice: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_tel: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_adresse: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_info: str = Form(""),
    taux_tva: float = Form(0.0),
    remise_pourcent: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_remise: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_ht: float = Form(...),
    montant_tva: float = Form(...),
    montant_ttc: float = Form(...),
    mode_paiement: str = Form("Virement"),
    notes: str = Form(""),
    lignes_json: str = Form("[]")
):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    now = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
    cid = int(client_id) if client_id and str(client_id).isdigit() and int(client_id) > 0 else None
    
    if cid:
        c = conn.execute("SELECT nom FROM clients WHERE id = ?", (cid,)).fetchone()
        client_nom = destinataire_nom.strip() or (c['nom'] if c else "Client")
    else:
        client_nom = destinataire_nom.strip() or "Particulier"

    if not destinataire_info:
        destinataire_info = f"{client_nom} - ICE: {destinataire_ice} - TEL: {destinataire_tel} - {destinataire_adresse}"

    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
        conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO factures_clients 
            (client_id, numero_facture, date_facture, date_debut, date_fin, destinataire_nom, destinataire_info, 
             taux_tva, remise_pourcent, montant_remise, montant_ht, montant_tva, montant_ttc, mode_paiement, 
             notes, lignes_json, statut, date_creation)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'Validée', ?)
        """, (cid, numero_facture.strip(), date_facture, date_debut, date_fin, client_nom, destinataire_info, 
              taux_tva, remise_pourcent, montant_remise, montant_ht, montant_tva, montant_ttc, mode_paiement, 
              notes, lignes_json, now))

        if cid:
            conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde + ? WHERE id = ?", (montant_ttc, cid))
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()

    if cid:
        return RedirectResponse(url=f"/clients/{cid}?tab=factures", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)
    return RedirectResponse(url="/factures", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/factures/modifier")
async def action_modifier_facture(
    facture_id: int = Form(...),
    client_id: Optional[str] = Form(None),
    numero_facture: str = Form(...),
    date_facture: str = Form(...),
    destinataire_nom: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_ice: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_tel: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_adresse: str = Form(""),
    destinataire_info: str = Form(""),
    taux_tva: float = Form(0.0),
    remise_pourcent: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_remise: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_ht: float = Form(...),
    montant_tva: float = Form(...),
    montant_ttc: float = Form(...),
    mode_paiement: str = Form("Virement"),
    notes: str = Form(""),
    lignes_json: str = Form("[]")
):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
        ancienne = conn.execute("SELECT montant_ttc, client_id FROM factures_clients WHERE id = ?", (facture_id,)).fetchone()
        if not ancienne:
            conn.rollback()
            return RedirectResponse(url="/factures", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

        ancien_ttc = float(ancienne['montant_ttc'] or 0.0)
        ancien_client_id = ancienne['client_id']
        cid = int(client_id) if client_id and str(client_id).isdigit() and int(client_id) > 0 else None

        nom_final = destinataire_nom.strip() or "Particulier"
        if not destinataire_info:
            destinataire_info = f"{nom_final} - ICE: {destinataire_ice} - TEL: {destinataire_tel} - {destinataire_adresse}"

        conn.execute("""
            UPDATE factures_clients 
            SET client_id = ?, numero_facture = ?, date_facture = ?, destinataire_nom = ?, destinataire_info = ?, 
                taux_tva = ?, remise_pourcent = ?, montant_remise = ?, montant_ht = ?, montant_tva = ?, 
                montant_ttc = ?, mode_paiement = ?, notes = ?, lignes_json = ?
            WHERE id = ?
        """, (cid, numero_facture.strip(), date_facture, nom_final, destinataire_info, taux_tva, 
              remise_pourcent, montant_remise, montant_ht, montant_tva, montant_ttc, mode_paiement, 
              notes, lignes_json, facture_id))

        if ancien_client_id and cid and ancien_client_id == cid:
            delta = montant_ttc - ancien_ttc
            conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde + ? WHERE id = ?", (delta, cid))
        else:
            if ancien_client_id:
                conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde - ? WHERE id = ?", (ancien_ttc, ancien_client_id))
            if cid:
                conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde + ? WHERE id = ?", (montant_ttc, cid))

        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()

    return RedirectResponse(url="/factures", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/factures/delete")
async def action_supprimer_facture(facture_id: int = Form(...)):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
        f = conn.execute("SELECT montant_ttc, client_id FROM factures_clients WHERE id = ?", (facture_id,)).fetchone()
        if f:
            montant_ttc = float(f['montant_ttc'] or 0.0)
            cid = f['client_id']
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM factures_clients WHERE id = ?", (facture_id,))
            if cid:
                conn.execute("UPDATE clients SET solde = solde - ? WHERE id = ?", (montant_ttc, cid))
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/factures", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.get("/factures/{facture_id}/imprimer", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def imprimer_facture_pdf(request: Request, facture_id: int):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    f = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM factures_clients WHERE id = ?", (facture_id,)).fetchone()
    if not f:
        conn.close()
        return HTMLResponse("Facture introuvable.", status_code=404)

    f_dict = dict(f)
    client_id = f_dict.get('client_id')
    client = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM clients WHERE id = ?", (client_id,)).fetchone() if client_id else None
    conn.close()

    if client:
        c_dict = dict(client)
    else:
        dest_info = f_dict.get('destinataire_info') or ''
        nom = f_dict.get('destinataire_nom') or 'Particulier'
        ice = ''
        tel = ''
        adresse = 'Nador'

        if 'ICE:' in dest_info.upper():
            m_ice = re.search(r'ICE\s*:\s*([^\s\-\|]+)', dest_info, re.IGNORECASE)
            if m_ice: ice = m_ice.group(1).strip()
        if 'TEL:' in dest_info.upper():
            m_tel = re.search(r'TEL\s*:\s*([^\s\-\|]+)', dest_info, re.IGNORECASE)
            if m_tel: tel = m_tel.group(1).strip()

        c_dict = {
            "nom": nom,
            "code": "PASSAGER",
            "telephone": tel,
            "ice": ice,
            "adresse": adresse
        }

    try:
        lignes_brutes = json.loads(f_dict.get('lignes_json') or '[]')
    except Exception:
        lignes_brutes = []

    date_raw = f_dict.get('date_facture', '')
    date_fr = formater_date_fr(date_raw)

    lignes = []
    for l in lignes_brutes:
        qte = float(l.get('qte', 0.0))
        pu_ttc = float(l.get('pu', 0.0))
        total_ttc = float(l.get('total_ttc', qte * pu_ttc))
        raw_nom = str(l.get('produit') or l.get('designation') or 'Article').strip()
        date_ligne = str(l.get('date') or '').strip()

        match_date = re.search(r'\((\d{2}[/-]\d{2}[/-]\d{4}|\d{4}[/-]\d{2}[/-]\d{2})\)', raw_nom)
        if match_date:
            if not date_ligne:
                date_ligne = match_date.group(1)
            raw_nom = re.sub(r'\s*\(' + re.escape(match_date.group(1)) + r'\)\s*', '', raw_nom).strip()

        if not date_ligne:
            date_ligne = f_dict.get('date_facture', '')

        date_ligne = formater_date_fr(date_ligne)
        nom_upper = raw_nom.upper()
        if "GASOIL" in nom_upper:
            designation = "Ravitaillement de Gasoil 10"
        elif "SSP" in nom_upper or "ESSENCE" in nom_upper:
            designation = "Ravitaillement d'essence (S.S.P)"
        else:
            designation = raw_nom

        qte_str = f"{int(qte)}" if qte.is_integer() else f"{qte:,.2f}"

        lignes.append({
            "date": date_ligne,
            "designation": designation,
            "qte_str": qte_str if qte > 0 else "-",
            "pu_ttc_str": f"{pu_ttc:,.2f} DH" if pu_ttc > 0 else "-",
            "total_ttc_str": f"{total_ttc:,.2f} DH"
        })

    montant_ttc = float(f_dict.get('montant_ttc') or 0.0)
    lettres = nombre_en_lettres(montant_ttc).strip()
    if "DIRHAM" not in lettres.upper():
        lettres = f"{lettres} DIRHAMS"

    return templates.TemplateResponse(
        request=request,
        name="factures/facture.html",
        context={
            "f": f_dict,
            "client": c_dict,
            "lignes": lignes,
            "date_fr": date_fr,
            "montant_en_lettres": lettres,
            "cache_buster": datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")
        }
    )
==================================================
FICHIER : .\pages\fournisseurs.py

import html
import os
import shutil
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Form, UploadFile, File, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse

import database
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

UPLOAD_DIR = "static/uploads/fournisseurs"
os.makedirs(UPLOAD_DIR, exist_ok=True)

def format_curr(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        val_float = float(val or 0.0)
        return f"{val_float:,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

def get_initials(name: str) -> str:
    parts = (name or "").strip().split()
    if len(parts) >= 2:
        return (parts[0][:1] + parts[1][:1]).upper()
    elif len(parts) == 1 and parts[0]:
        return parts[0][:2].upper()
    return "FR"

def get_avatar_bg(name: str) -> str:
    colors = [
        "bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30",
        "bg-teal-600/20 text-teal-400 border-teal-500/30",
        "bg-purple-600/20 text-purple-400 border-purple-500/30",
        "bg-amber-600/20 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30",
        "bg-rose-600/20 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/30",
        "bg-cyan-600/20 text-cyan-400 border-cyan-500/30",
    ]
    return colors[hash(name or "") % len(colors)]

    <div class="space-y-3.5">

        <!-- BARRE DE GESTION FOURNISSEURS (IMAGE 2) -->

                    <i data-lucide="search" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"></i>
                    <input type="text" id="searchInput" oninput="filtrerFournisseurs()" placeholder="Recherche..." class="h-8 pl-8 pr-3 w-36 sm:w-48 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#09151F] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1e384d] rounded-full text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#FF6A00]">

                </div>

    <!-- SCRIPTS INTERACTIFS -->
    <script>
        function toggleMenu(menuId, event) {{
            event.stopPropagation();
            const targetMenu = document.getElementById(menuId);
            const isHidden = targetMenu.classList.contains('hidden');
            document.querySelectorAll('.card-dropdown').forEach(m => m.classList.add('hidden'));
            if (isHidden) {{
                targetMenu.classList.remove('hidden');
            }}
        }}

        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {{
            if (!e.target.closest('.card-dropdown')) {{
                document.querySelectorAll('.card-dropdown').forEach(m => m.classList.add('hidden'));
            }}
        }});

        function filtrerFournisseurs() {{
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase();
            const filterType = document.getElementById('typeFilter').value;
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.supplier-card');

            cards.forEach(card => {{
                const name = card.getAttribute('data-name');
                const code = card.getAttribute('data-code');
                const type = card.getAttribute('data-type');

                const matchSearch = name.includes(search) || code.includes(search);
                const matchType = (filterType === 'TOUS' || type === filterType);

                if (matchSearch && matchType) {{
                    card.style.display = '';
                }} else {{
                    card.style.display = 'none';
                }}
            }});
        }}

        function trierFournisseurs() {{
            const sort = document.getElementById('sortFilter').value;
            const grid = document.getElementById('suppliersGrid');
            const cards = Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll('.supplier-card'));

            cards.sort((a, b) => {{
                const nameA = a.getAttribute('data-name');
                const nameB = b.getAttribute('data-name');
                if (sort === 'nom-asc') return nameA.localeCompare(nameB);
                return nameB.localeCompare(nameA);
            }});

            cards.forEach(card => grid.appendChild(card));
        }}

        function calcAchatTotal() {{
            const vol = parseFloat(document.getElementById('achat-vol').value) || 0;
            const pu = parseFloat(document.getElementById('achat-pu').value) || 0;
            if (vol > 0 && pu > 0) {{
                document.getElementById('achat-total').value = (vol * pu).toFixed(2);
            }}
        }}

        function ouvrirAchatDirect(id, nom) {{
            document.getElementById('achat-f-id').value = id;
            document.getElementById('achat-f-nom').innerText = nom;
            document.getElementById('modal-achat').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}

        function ouvrirPaiementDirect(id, nom, dette) {{
            document.getElementById('pay-f-id').value = id;
            document.getElementById('pay-f-nom').innerText = nom;
            document.getElementById('pay-f-dette').innerText = (parseFloat(dette) || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' MAD';
            document.getElementById('modal-reglement').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}

        function ouvrirModifierDirect(id, code, nom, type, statut, tel, email) {{
            document.getElementById('mod-id').value = id;
            document.getElementById('mod-code').value = code;
            document.getElementById('mod-nom').value = nom;
            document.getElementById('mod-type').value = type;
            document.getElementById('mod-statut').value = statut;
            document.getElementById('mod-tel').value = tel;
            document.getElementById('mod-email').value = email;
            document.getElementById('modal-modifier-f').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}

        function ouvrirSupprimerDirect(id, nom) {{
            document.getElementById('sup-id').value = id;
            document.getElementById('sup-nom-text').innerText = 'Fournisseur : ' + nom;
            document.getElementById('modal-supprimer-f').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="fournisseurs", request=request))

@router.post("/fournisseurs/nouveau")
async def action_nouveau_fournisseur(
    code: str = Form(None),
    nom: str = Form(...),
    type_fournisseur: str = Form("Carburant"),
    contact_nom: str = Form(""),
    telephone: str = Form(""),
    email: str = Form(""),
    adresse: str = Form(""),
    conditions_paiement: str = Form(""),
    solde_initial: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_fidelite: float = Form(0.0),
    doc_categorie: str = Form("RC"),
    doc_description: str = Form(""),
    notes: str = Form(""),
    fichier_doc: UploadFile = File(None)
):
    clean_code = (code or "").strip().upper()
    if not clean_code:
        clean_code = database.generer_prochain_code_fournisseur()

    doc_filename = ""
    if fichier_doc and fichier_doc.filename:
        safe_name = f"{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}_{fichier_doc.filename.replace(' ', '_')}"
        dest_path = os.path.join(UPLOAD_DIR, safe_name)
        with open(dest_path, "wb") as buffer:
            shutil.copyfileobj(fichier_doc.file, buffer)
        doc_filename = f"/static/uploads/fournisseurs/{safe_name}"

    new_id = database.ajouter_fournisseur(
        code=clean_code,
        nom=nom.strip(),
        type_f=type_fournisseur,
        contact=contact_nom.strip(),
        tel=telephone.strip(),
        email=email.strip(),
        adresse=adresse.strip(),
        cond=conditions_paiement.strip(),
        solde_init=solde_initial,
        montant_fidelite=montant_fidelite or 0.0,
        document=doc_filename,
        doc_categorie=doc_categorie,
        doc_description=doc_description.strip(),
        notes=notes.strip(),
        statut="Actif"
    )
    return RedirectResponse("/fournisseurs", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/fournisseurs/modifier")

):

        contact=contact_nom.strip(),

        email=email.strip(),

    date: str = Form(...),

    volume: float = Form(0.0),
    prix_unitaire: float = Form(0.0),
    montant_total: float = Form(...),
    observations: str = Form("")
):
    database.ajouter_achat(
        fournisseur_id=fournisseur_id,
        date=date,
        montant_total=montant_total,
        reference_bl=reference_bl.strip(),
        produit=produit,
        volume_litres=volume,
        prix_unitaire=prix_unitaire,
        observations=observations.strip()
    )
    return RedirectResponse(f"/fournisseurs?id={fournisseur_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/fournisseurs/reglement")
async def action_ajout_reglement(
    fournisseur_id: int = Form(...),
    date: str = Form(...),
    reference_paiement: str = Form(...),
    mode_paiement: str = Form(...),
    montant: float = Form(...),
    banque: str = Form(""),
    observations: str = Form("")
):
    database.ajouter_reglement_fournisseur(
        fournisseur_id=fournisseur_id,
        date=date,
        reference=reference_paiement.strip(),
        mode=mode_paiement,
        montant=montant,
        banque=banque.strip(),
        obs=observations.strip()
    )
    return RedirectResponse(f"/fournisseurs?id={fournisseur_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

# pages/home.py
import json
import html
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Form, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection

router = APIRouter()

def ensure_schema(conn):
    try:
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE prix_vente ADD COLUMN date_effet TEXT")
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass

def format_num(val):
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

def get_row_val(row, key, default=""):
    if not row:
        return default
    try:
        val = row[key]
        return val if val is not None else default
    except Exception:
        return default

def update_scheduled_prices(conn):
    """Active automatiquement les prix programmés dont la date est arrivée."""
    ensure_schema(conn)
    try:
        scheduled = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, produit, date_effet, date FROM prix_vente WHERE statut = 'PROGRAMME'"
        ).fetchall()
        
        for item in scheduled:
            dt_raw = get_row_val(item, 'date_effet') or get_row_val(item, 'date')
            try:
                if "-" in dt_raw:
                    dt_parsed = datetime.strptime(dt_raw[:16], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if len(dt_raw) >= 16 else datetime.strptime(dt_raw[:10], "%Y-%m-%d")
                else:
                    dt_parsed = datetime.strptime(dt_raw[:16], "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
                
                if dt_parsed <= datetime.now():
                    prod = item['produit']
                    cibles = ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] if prod in ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else ['SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb']
                    placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(cibles))
                    conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
                    conn.execute("UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'ACTIF' WHERE id = ?", (item['id'],))
            except Exception:
                pass
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass

def render_tank_citerne_svg(tank_id, pct, color_type="SSP"):
    """Citerne industrielle épurée adaptée au style sombre translucide."""
    pct_val = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(pct)))
    fill_height = (pct_val / 100.0) * 54.0
    liquid_y = 70.0 - fill_height

    if color_type == "SSP":
        stroke_color = "#00F5A0"
        glow_color = "rgba(0, 245, 160, 0.45)"
        grad_top = "#00FFA3"
        grad_bot = "#047857"
        grad_back = "#059669"
    else:
        stroke_color = "#FF9F1C"
        glow_color = "rgba(255, 159, 28, 0.45)"
        grad_top = "#FFB347"
        grad_bot = "#C2410C"
        grad_back = "#EA580C"

    return f"""
    <div class="relative w-36 sm:w-40 select-none shrink-0">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 200 88" class="w-full h-auto drop-shadow-[0_4px_14px_{glow_color}] overflow-visible">
            <defs>
                <linearGradient id="grad_front_{tank_id}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                    <stop offset="0%" stop-color="{grad_top}" stop-opacity="0.95" />
                    <stop offset="100%" stop-color="{grad_bot}" stop-opacity="0.98" />
                </linearGradient>

                <linearGradient id="grad_back_{tank_id}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                    <stop offset="0%" stop-color="{grad_back}" stop-opacity="0.55" />
                    <stop offset="100%" stop-color="{grad_bot}" stop-opacity="0.45" />
                </linearGradient>

                <linearGradient id="rimGrad_{tank_id}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
                    <stop offset="0%" stop-color="{stroke_color}" stop-opacity="0.9" />
                    <stop offset="50%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0.3" />
                    <stop offset="100%" stop-color="{stroke_color}" stop-opacity="0.75" />
                </linearGradient>

                <clipPath id="tankMask_{tank_id}">
                    <rect x="14" y="16" width="172" height="54" rx="27" ry="27" />
                </clipPath>
            </defs>

            <!-- 1. BERCEAUX DE CUVE -->
            <g class="fill-[#1E293B] stroke-[#334155]">
                <polygon points="40,71 34,83 52,83 47,71" stroke-width="1" />
                <rect x="32" y="82" width="22" height="2.5" rx="1.2" class="fill-[#0F172A]" />
                
                <polygon points="153,71 148,83 166,83 160,71" stroke-width="1" />
                <rect x="146" y="82" width="22" height="2.5" rx="1.2" class="fill-[#0F172A]" />
            </g>

            <!-- 2. DÔME SUPÉRIEUR -->
            <g>
                <rect x="94" y="9" width="12" height="6" class="fill-[#1E293B]" stroke="{stroke_color}" stroke-width="1.2" />
                <rect x="87" y="5" width="26" height="5" rx="2.5" class="fill-[#334155]" stroke="{stroke_color}" stroke-width="1.2" />
                <circle cx="92" cy="7.5" r="1" class="fill-[#94A3B8]" />
                <circle cx="108" cy="7.5" r="1" class="fill-[#94A3B8]" />
            </g>

            <!-- 3. CORPS DE CUVE -->
            <rect x="12" y="14" width="176" height="58" rx="29" ry="29" 
                  class="fill-[#061019]" 
                  stroke="url(#rimGrad_{tank_id})" stroke-width="2" />

            <!-- 4. CONTENU INTERNE -->
            <g clip-path="url(#tankMask_{tank_id})">
                <rect x="14" y="16" width="172" height="54" class="fill-[#03080F]" />

                <!-- Anneaux internes -->
                <line x1="60" y1="16" x2="60" y2="70" stroke="currentColor" class="text-white/5" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="2 3" />
                <line x1="140" y1="16" x2="140" y2="70" stroke="currentColor" class="text-white/5" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="2 3" />

                <!-- Graduations -->
                <line x1="24" y1="30" x2="31" y2="30" stroke="currentColor" class="text-white/25" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-linecap="round" />
                <line x1="24" y1="43" x2="35" y2="43" stroke="currentColor" class="text-white/40" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" />
                <line x1="24" y1="56" x2="31" y2="56" stroke="currentColor" class="text-white/25" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-linecap="round" />

        </svg>
    </div>
