@router.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def home_page(request: Request):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    update_scheduled_prices(conn)
    today_sql = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    
    actif_gazoil = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10') AND statut = 'ACTIF' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    actif_ssp = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb') AND statut = 'ACTIF' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    
    gazoil_history = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10') ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 15").fetchall()
    ssp_history = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb') ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 15").fetchall()
    
    clients_rows = conn.execute("SELECT solde, plafond FROM clients WHERE statut = 'ACTIF'").fetchall()
    clients_a_surveiller = sum(1 for c in clients_rows if float(c['plafond'] or 0) > 0 and float(c['solde'] or 0) >= float(c['plafond'] or 0) * 0.8)
    clients_critiques = sum(1 for c in clients_rows if float(c['plafond'] or 0) > 0 and float(c['solde'] or 0) >= float(c['plafond'] or 0))
    solde_clients_total = sum(float(c['solde'] or 0) for c in clients_rows)

    clotures_jour = conn.execute("SELECT SUM(total_net) as total_caisse, SUM(especes) as total_esp FROM cloture_quart WHERE date LIKE ?", (f"{today_sql}%",)).fetchone()
    caisse_aujourdhui = float(clotures_jour['total_caisse'] or 0.0) if clotures_jour and clotures_jour['total_caisse'] else 0.0
    solde_caisse = float(clotures_jour['total_esp'] or 0.0) if clotures_jour and clotures_jour['total_esp'] else 0.0

    total_achats = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(montant_total), 0) FROM achats_fournisseurs").fetchone()[0]
    total_reglements = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(montant), 0) FROM reglements_fournisseurs").fetchone()[0]
    dettes_fournisseurs = max(0.0, float(total_achats) - float(total_reglements))

    trans_ssp = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(volume), 0) FROM transactions_jour WHERE type IN ('ESS', 'SSP') AND date_releve = ?", (today_sql,)).fetchone()[0]
    trans_go = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(volume), 0) FROM transactions_jour WHERE type IN ('DIE', 'GO', 'GAZOIL') AND date_releve = ?", (today_sql,)).fetchone()[0]
    
    releve_essence_l = f"{float(trans_ssp):,.0f}".replace(",", " ")
    releve_gazoil_l = f"{float(trans_go):,.0f}".replace(",", " ")

    stock_essence_litres = 11154
    stock_essence_pct = round((stock_essence_litres / 31600.0) * 100, 1)

    stock_gazoil_litres = 28020
    stock_gazoil_pct = round((stock_gazoil_litres / 63200.0) * 100, 1)

    conn.close()

    today_dt = datetime.now()
    date_to_val = today_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    date_from_val = (today_dt - timedelta(days=30)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    now_iso = today_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")

    prix_gazoil_val = f"{actif_gazoil['prix']:.3f}" if actif_gazoil else "12.000"
    date_gazoil_val = get_row_val(actif_gazoil, 'date_effet') or get_row_val(actif_gazoil, 'date') or today_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")

    prix_ssp_val = f"{actif_ssp['prix']:.3f}" if actif_ssp else "14.670"
    date_ssp_val = get_row_val(actif_ssp, 'date_effet') or get_row_val(actif_ssp, 'date') or today_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")

    chart_labels = [(today_dt - timedelta(days=i)).strftime("%d/%m") for i in range(25, -1, -1)]
    gazoil_sales = [5600, 700, 2700, 2900, 3200, 3400, 3600, 3000, 3200, 2900, 3069, 2800, 2750, 3000, 5800, 2900, 4200, 3000, 3800, 3600, 4800, 5400, 1500, 3800, 3600, 3100]
    essence_sales = [2900, 400, 1200, 1100, 1400, 1800, 1600, 1700, 1900, 1400, 1186, 1300, 1250, 1400, 2800, 450, 1200, 1000, 1800, 1200, 1400, 2200, 400, 1700, 1500, 3400]
    total_sales = [d + e for d, e in zip(gazoil_sales, essence_sales)]

    def generate_history_rows(rows, is_gazoil=False):
        if not rows:
            return '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center py-6 text-slate-500 text-xs italic">Aucun historique enregistré</td></tr>'
        html_out = ""
        badge_active_cls = "bg-orange-500/15 text-[#FF9F1C] border-orange-500/30" if is_gazoil else "bg-emerald-500/15 text-[#00F5A0] border-emerald-500/30"
        btn_activate_cls = "bg-orange-500/10 hover:bg-[#FF9F1C] text-[#FF9F1C] hover:text-slate-950" if is_gazoil else "bg-emerald-500/10 hover:bg-[#00F5A0] text-[#00F5A0] hover:text-slate-950"

        for r in rows:
            statut = r['statut']
            date_aff = get_row_val(r, 'date_effet') or get_row_val(r, 'date') or '-'
            if statut == 'ACTIF':
                badge = f'<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black uppercase border {badge_active_cls}">Actif</span>'
                action = ""
            elif statut == 'PROGRAMME':
                badge = '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-black uppercase bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-400 border border-sky-500/30">Programmé</span>'
                action = f"""
                <form action="/home/activer-prix" method="post" class="m-0 inline">
                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="{r['id']}">
                    <button type="submit" class="{btn_activate_cls} px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition">Activer</button>
                </form>
                """
            else:
                badge = '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-bold uppercase bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 border border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-700">Inactif</span>'
                action = f"""
                <form action="/home/activer-prix" method="post" class="m-0 inline">
                    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="{r['id']}">
                    <button type="submit" class="bg-slate-200 hover:bg-[#184E68] text-slate-700 hover:text-white dark:bg-white/5 dark:text-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase transition">Activer</button>
                </form>
                """
                
            html_out += f"""
            <tr class="border-b border-slate-200/80 dark:border-white/5 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition text-xs">
                <td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{r['prix']:.3f} <span class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 font-sans">MAD</span></td>
                <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-mono text-[11px]">{date_aff}</td>
                <td class="py-2.5 px-3">{badge}</td>
                <td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right">{action}</td>
            </tr>
            """
        return html_out

    gazoil_table = generate_history_rows(gazoil_history, is_gazoil=True)
    ssp_table = generate_history_rows(ssp_history, is_gazoil=False)

    ssp_tank_html = render_tank_citerne_svg("ssp_tank", stock_essence_pct, color_type="SSP")
    gazoil_tank_html = render_tank_citerne_svg("gazoil_tank", stock_gazoil_pct, color_type="GAZOIL")

    content = f"""
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>

    <style>
        /* Effet Glassmorphism Adaptatif Clair / Sombre */
        .glass-panel {{
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88) !important;
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(180%) !important;
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(180%) !important;
            border: 1px solid rgba(226, 232, 240, 0.90) !important;

    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {{
            const ctx = document.getElementById('salesEvolutionChart').getContext('2d');
            
            const labels = {json.dumps(chart_labels)};
            const gazoilData = {json.dumps(gazoil_sales)};
            const essenceData = {json.dumps(essence_sales)};
            const totalData = {json.dumps(total_sales)};

            new Chart(ctx, {{
                type: 'line',
                data: {{
                    labels: labels,
                    datasets: [
                        {{
                            label: 'Total Ventes',
                            data: totalData,
                            borderColor: '#818CF8',
                            borderWidth: 2,
                            borderDash: [4, 4],
                            pointRadius: 2,
                            fill: false,
                            tension: 0.35
                        }},
                        {{
                            label: 'SSP',
                            data: essenceData,
                            borderColor: '#00F5A0',
                            borderWidth: 2.5,
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 245, 160, 0.12)',
                            pointRadius: 2.5,
                            fill: true,
                            tension: 0.35
                        }},
                        {{
                            label: 'GAZOIL',
                            data: gazoilData,
                            borderColor: '#FF9F1C',
                            borderWidth: 2.5,
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 159, 28, 0.12)',
                            pointRadius: 2.5,
                            fill: true,
                            tension: 0.35
                        }}
                    ]
                }},
                options: {{
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: {{
                        legend: {{ display: false }},
                        tooltip: {{
                            callbacks: {{
                                label: function(context) {{
                                    return ' ' + context.dataset.label + ': ' + context.parsed.y.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' L';
                                }}
                            }}
                        }}
                    }},
                    scales: {{
                        x: {{ 
                            grid: {{ display: false }},
                            ticks: {{ color: '#94A3B8', font: {{ size: 10 }} }}
                        }},
                        y: {{
                            grid: {{ color: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.15)' }},
                            ticks: {{ 
                                color: '#94A3B8', 
                                font: {{ size: 10 }},
                                callback: function(value) {{ return value + ' L'; }} 
                            }}
                        }}
                    }}
                }}
            }});
        }});

        function toggleScheduledDate(isImmediate) {{
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('scheduled_date_wrapper');
            const dateInput = document.getElementById('modal_date_effet');
            if (isImmediate) {{
                wrapper.classList.add('hidden');
                dateInput.removeAttribute('required');
            }} else {{
                wrapper.classList.remove('hidden');
                dateInput.setAttribute('required', 'required');
            }}
        }}

        function openModalSinglePrice(fuelName, currentPrice) {{
            const isSSP = (fuelName === 'SSP' || fuelName === 'Essence' || fuelName === 'Sans Plomb');
            const cleanFuel = isSSP ? 'SSP' : 'GAZOIL';
            
            document.getElementById('modal_produit_nom').value = cleanFuel;
            document.getElementById('modal_title_fuel').innerText = cleanFuel;
            document.getElementById('modal_single_prix_input').value = currentPrice || '';

            const icon = document.getElementById('modal_header_icon');
            const titleSpan = document.getElementById('modal_title_fuel');
            const label = document.getElementById('modal_price_label');
            const submitBtn = document.getElementById('modal_submit_btn');
            const chk = document.getElementById('chk_immediate');

            if (isSSP) {{
                icon.className = "w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]";
                titleSpan.className = "text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]";
                label.className = "text-[10px] font-bold uppercase mb-1 block tracking-wider text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]";
                submitBtn.className = "flex-1 bg-[#00F5A0] hover:bg-[#00D084] text-slate-950 py-2 rounded-xl font-black uppercase text-[11px] shadow-sm transition active:scale-95";
                chk.className = "w-4 h-4 rounded accent-[#00F5A0] cursor-pointer";
            }} else {{
                icon.className = "w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-[#FF9F1C]";
                titleSpan.className = "text-amber-600 dark:text-[#FF9F1C]";
                label.className = "text-[10px] font-bold uppercase mb-1 block tracking-wider text-amber-600 dark:text-[#FF9F1C]";
                submitBtn.className = "flex-1 bg-[#FF9F1C] hover:bg-[#ff8f00] text-slate-950 py-2 rounded-xl font-black uppercase text-[11px] shadow-sm transition active:scale-95";
                chk.className = "w-4 h-4 rounded accent-[#FF9F1C] cursor-pointer";
            }}
            
            chk.checked = true;
            toggleScheduledDate(true);
            
            document.getElementById('modalSinglePrice').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function closeModalSinglePrice() {{
            document.getElementById('modalSinglePrice').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        function openModalHistory(fuel) {{
            switchHistoryTab(fuel);
            document.getElementById('modalHistory').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function switchHistoryTab(fuel) {{
            const isGazoil = (fuel === 'GAZOIL' || fuel === 'Gazoil' || fuel === 'Diesel' || fuel === 'GO' || fuel === 'Gasoil 10');
            const gazoilBox = document.getElementById('historyGazoilContainer');
            const sspBox = document.getElementById('historySSPContainer');
            const btnGazoil = document.getElementById('tabBtnGazoil');
            const btnSsp = document.getElementById('tabBtnSSP');

            if(isGazoil) {{
                gazoilBox.classList.remove('hidden');
                sspBox.classList.add('hidden');
                btnGazoil.className = "px-2.5 py-1 text-[10.5px] font-bold rounded-lg bg-[#FF9F1C] text-slate-950";
                btnSsp.className = "px-2.5 py-1 text-[10.5px] font-bold rounded-lg bg-slate-200 dark:bg-white/10 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300";

        function closeModalHistory() {{
            document.getElementById('modalHistory').classList.add('hidden');
        }}
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "tableau", request=request))

@router.post("/home/update-prix-unique")
async def update_prix_unique_home(
    produit: str = Form(...), 
    prix: float = Form(...),
    activer_immediat: str = Form(None),
    date_effet: str = Form(None)
):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    ensure_schema(conn)
    cibles = ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] if produit in ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else ['SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb']
    save_name = 'GAZOIL' if produit in ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else 'SSP'
    placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(cibles))

    is_immediate = (activer_immediat == "1")
    now_dt = datetime.now()
    
    if is_immediate or not date_effet:
        date_formatted = now_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
        conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIF')",
            (date_formatted, date_formatted, save_name, prix)
        )
    else:
        try:
            scheduled_dt = datetime.strptime(date_effet, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")
            date_formatted = scheduled_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
        except Exception:
            scheduled_dt = now_dt
            date_formatted = date_effet

        if scheduled_dt <= now_dt:
            conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIF')",
                (date_formatted, date_formatted, save_name, prix)
            )
        else:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PROGRAMME')",
                (date_formatted, date_formatted, save_name, prix)
            )

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/home/activer-prix")
async def activer_prix_home(id: int = Form(...)):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    row = conn.execute("SELECT produit FROM prix_vente WHERE id = ?", (id,)).fetchone()
    if row:
        prod = row['produit']
        cibles = ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] if prod in ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else ['SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb']
        placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(cibles))
        conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
        conn.execute("UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'ACTIF' WHERE id = ?", (id,))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/login")
async def login_post(username: str = Form(...), password: str = Form(...)):
    raw_user = username.strip()
    raw_pass = password.strip()

    user = verifier_utilisateur(raw_user.lower(), raw_pass) or verifier_utilisateur(
        raw_user, raw_pass
    )
    if not user:
        return RedirectResponse(
            url="/login?error=Identifiant+ou+mot+de+passe+incorrect",
            status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER,
        )

    role = str(user.get("role", "employe")).lower().strip()
    redirect_target = "/rapport-quart" if role == "employe" else "/"

    response = RedirectResponse(
        url=redirect_target, status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER
    )
    signed_cookie = signer_session(user)
    response.set_cookie(
        key="session_user",
        value=signed_cookie,
        httponly=True,
        samesite="lax",
        max_age=SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS,
    )
    return response


@router.get("/logout")
async def logout():
    response = RedirectResponse(url="/login", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)
    response.delete_cookie("session_user")
    return response

@router.get("/notes", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def notes_page(request: Request):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    raw_notes = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM notes ORDER BY id DESC").fetchall()
    notes = [dict(n) for n in raw_notes]
    conn.close()

    cards_html = ""
    for n in notes:
        n_id = n["id"]
        titre = html.escape(n.get("titre") or "NOTE")
        raw_contenu = n.get("contenu") or ""
        contenu = html.escape(raw_contenu).replace("\n", "<br>")
        
        attr_titre = html.escape(n.get("titre") or "", quote=True)
        attr_contenu = html.escape(raw_contenu, quote=True)
        
        couleur = n.get("couleur") if n.get("couleur") in NOTE_THEMES else "yellow"
        theme = NOTE_THEMES[couleur]
        date_c = n.get("date_creation") or ""
        auteur = html.escape(n.get("auteur") or "Safouane")

        cards_html += f"""
        <!-- NOTE CARD #{n_id} -->
        <div class="note-item group relative flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-b {theme['gradient']} backdrop-blur-xl border {theme['border']} p-5 {theme['shadow']} {theme['glow']} transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1.5 overflow-hidden"
             data-search="{titre.lower()} {html.escape(raw_contenu.lower(), quote=True)}">
            
            <!-- Ligne lumineuse en haut de carte -->
            <div class="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-1 bg-gradient-to-r {theme['top_bar']}"></div>

            <div>
                <!-- En-tête de carte -->

    <script>
        const THEME_DOTS = {{
            yellow: "bg-amber-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(251,191,36,1)]",
            blue: "bg-sky-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(56,189,248,1)]",
            green: "bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(52,211,153,1)]",
            pink: "bg-rose-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(251,113,133,1)]",
            purple: "bg-purple-400 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(192,132,252,1)]"
        }};

        function filterNotes(query) {{
            const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
            document.querySelectorAll('.note-item').forEach(card => {{
                const content = card.getAttribute('data-search') || '';
                card.style.display = (!q || content.includes(q)) ? 'flex' : 'none';
            }});
        }}

        function togglePalette(event, id) {{
            event.stopPropagation();
            closeAllPalettes();
            const pop = document.getElementById('palette-popover-' + id);
            if (pop) pop.classList.toggle('hidden');
        }}

        function closeAllPalettes() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('[id^="palette-popover-"]').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
        }}

        function changeColor(id, color) {{
            const form = document.createElement('form');
            form.method = 'POST';
            form.action = '/notes/set-color';

            const inId = document.createElement('input');
            inId.type = 'hidden';
            inId.name = 'id';
            inId.value = id;

            const inColor = document.createElement('input');
            inColor.type = 'hidden';
            inColor.name = 'couleur';
            inColor.value = color;

            form.appendChild(inId);
            form.appendChild(inColor);
            document.body.appendChild(form);
            form.submit();
        }}

        function selectModalColor(color, btn) {{
            document.getElementById('modal_couleur').value = color;
            document.querySelectorAll('.modal-color-opt').forEach(b => {{
                b.className = "modal-color-opt w-8 h-8 rounded-xl transition hover:scale-105 opacity-60 " + b.className.split(' ').find(c => c.startsWith('bg-'));
            }});
            if (btn) {{
                btn.className = "modal-color-opt w-8 h-8 rounded-xl ring-2 ring-white ring-offset-2 ring-offset-slate-900 transition hover:scale-105 opacity-100 " + btn.className.split(' ').find(c => c.startsWith('bg-'));
            }}
            const dot = document.getElementById('modal_dot');
            if (dot && THEME_DOTS[color]) {{
                dot.className = "w-3 h-3 rounded-full " + THEME_DOTS[color];
            }}
        }}

        function openAddModal() {{
            document.getElementById('modal_id').value = '0';
            document.getElementById('modal_title').innerText = 'Nouveau Mémo';
            document.getElementById('modal_titre').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modal_contenu').value = '';
            selectModalColor('green', document.querySelector('.modal-color-opt[data-color="green"]'));
            document.getElementById('noteModal').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function openEditModal(btn) {{
            const id = btn.getAttribute('data-id');
            const titre = btn.getAttribute('data-titre') || '';
            const contenu = btn.getAttribute('data-contenu') || '';
            const couleur = btn.getAttribute('data-couleur') || 'green';

            document.getElementById('modal_id').value = id;
            document.getElementById('modal_title').innerText = 'Modifier le Mémo';
            document.getElementById('modal_titre').value = titre;
            document.getElementById('modal_contenu').value = contenu;
            selectModalColor(couleur, document.querySelector(`.modal-color-opt[data-color="${{couleur}}"]`));

            document.getElementById('noteModal').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function closeNoteModal() {{
            document.getElementById('noteModal').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "notes", request=request))

@router.post("/notes/save")
async def save_note(
    request: Request,
    id: int = Form(0),
    titre: str = Form(""),
    contenu: str = Form(...),
    couleur: str = Form("green")
):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    user = getattr(request.state, "user", None)
    user_nom = user.get("nom_complet", "Admin") if user else "Admin"

    date_str = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
    clean_titre = titre.strip().upper() if titre else "MÉMO"

    if id > 0:
        conn.execute("""
            UPDATE notes 
            SET titre = ?, contenu = ?, couleur = ?
            WHERE id = ?
        """, (clean_titre, contenu.strip(), couleur, id))
    else:
        conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO notes (titre, contenu, couleur, est_epingle, date_creation, auteur)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)
        """, (clean_titre, contenu.strip(), couleur, date_str, user_nom))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/notes", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/notes/set-color")
async def set_note_color(id: int = Form(...), couleur: str = Form(...)):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    conn.execute("UPDATE notes SET couleur = ? WHERE id = ?", (couleur, id))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/notes", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

@router.post("/notes/delete")
async def delete_note(id: int = Form(...)):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    conn.execute("DELETE FROM notes WHERE id = ?", (id,))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/notes", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

FICHIER : .\pages\prix.py
==================================================


# pages/prix.py
import html
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Query, Request, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection

router = APIRouter()

def ensure_schema(conn):
    try:
        conn.execute("ALTER TABLE prix_vente ADD COLUMN date_effet TEXT")
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass

def get_row_val(row, key, default=""):
    if not row:
        return default
    try:
        val = row[key]
        return val if val is not None else default
    except Exception:
        return default

def update_scheduled_prices(conn):
    """Active automatiquement les prix dont l'échéance est arrivée."""
    ensure_schema(conn)
    try:
        scheduled = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, produit, date_effet, date FROM prix_vente WHERE statut = 'PROGRAMME'"
        ).fetchall()
        
        for item in scheduled:
            dt_raw = get_row_val(item, 'date_effet') or get_row_val(item, 'date')
            try:
                if "-" in dt_raw:
                    dt_parsed = datetime.strptime(dt_raw[:16], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") if len(dt_raw) >= 16 else datetime.strptime(dt_raw[:10], "%Y-%m-%d")
                else:
                    dt_parsed = datetime.strptime(dt_raw[:16], "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")

                if dt_parsed <= datetime.now():
                    prod = item['produit']
                    cibles = ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] if prod in ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else ['SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb']
                    placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(cibles))
                    conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
                    conn.execute("UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'ACTIF' WHERE id = ?", (item['id'],))
            except Exception:
                pass
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass

@router.get("/prix", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def prix_page(request: Request, status_filter: str = Query("Tous")):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    update_scheduled_prices(conn)
    
    query = "SELECT * FROM prix_vente WHERE 1=1"
    params = []
    if status_filter and status_filter != "Tous":
        query += " AND statut = ?"
        params.append(status_filter.upper())
    
    query += " ORDER BY id DESC"
    all_prices = conn.execute(query, params).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    diesel_history = [p for p in all_prices if p['produit'] in ['GAZOIL', 'Gazoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10']]
    ssp_history = [p for p in all_prices if p['produit'] in ['SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb']]

    def generate_price_card(p):
        is_actif = p['statut'] == "ACTIF"
        is_prog = p['statut'] == "PROGRAMME"
        
        if is_actif:
            border_cls = "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10"
            status_action = """
                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black uppercase bg-emerald-500/20 text-[#00F5A0]">
                    ● Actuel
                </span>
            """
        elif is_prog:
            border_cls = "border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10"
            status_action = """
                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black uppercase bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400">
                    ● Programmé
                </span>
            """
        else:
            border_cls = "border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15]"
            status_action = """
                <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400">
                    Historique
                </span>
            """
        
        date_aff = get_row_val(p, 'date_effet') or get_row_val(p, 'date') or '-'

        return f"""
        <div class="border {border_cls} rounded-xl p-2.5 mb-2 flex justify-between items-center transition">
            <div>
                <p class="text-base font-black font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white leading-none">
                    {p['prix']:.3f} <span class="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold text-slate-400">MAD/L</span>
                </p>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 mt-1 block">Date d'effet : <b>{date_aff}</b></span>
            </div>
            <div>
                {status_action}
            </div>
        </div>
        """

    now_iso = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")

    content = f"""
    <div class="h-full flex flex-col gap-3 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
        
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/90 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-xl bg-[#00F5A0]/10 text-[#00F5A0] flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="tag" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                </div>
                <h1 class="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Tarifs des Carburants (SSP & Diesel)
                </h1>
            </div>
            <button onclick="toggleModal(true)" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#00F5A0] hover:bg-[#00D084] text-slate-950 font-black text-xs uppercase shadow-xs transition active:scale-95">
                <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[3]"></i>
                <span>Nouveau Tarif</span>
            </button>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-3 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            
            <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center pb-2 mb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                    <span class="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-[#00F5A0] text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#00F5A0]"></span> SSP
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">{len(ssp_history)} tarif(s) enregistré(s)</span>
                </div>
                <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto max-h-[420px] pr-1">
                    {"".join([generate_price_card(p) for p in ssp_history]) if ssp_history else '<p class="text-center text-slate-400 text-xs py-8 italic">Aucun tarif enregistré</p>'}
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div class="flex justify-between items-center pb-2 mb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                    <span class="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-[#00F5A0] text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#00F5A0]"></span> Diesel (Gazoil)
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">{len(diesel_history)} tarif(s) enregistré(s)</span>
                </div>
                <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto max-h-[420px] pr-1">
                    {"".join([generate_price_card(p) for p in diesel_history]) if diesel_history else '<p class="text-center text-slate-400 text-xs py-8 italic">Aucun tarif enregistré</p>'}
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>

    <div id="modalAddPrix" class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-950/70 backdrop-blur-xs hidden z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3">
        <form action="/update-prix" method="post" class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
            <div class="p-3 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] flex justify-between items-center bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i data-lucide="tag" class="w-4 h-4 text-[#00F5A0]"></i>
                    <h3 class="text-xs font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white uppercase">Appliquer un Nouveau Tarif</h3>
                </div>
                <button type="button" onclick="toggleModal(false)" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white font-bold text-xs">✕</button>
            </div>
            
            <div class="p-4 space-y-3 text-xs">
                <div>
                    <label class="text-[10.5px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 uppercase mb-1 block">Produit</label>
                    <select name="produit" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 font-bold outline-none text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                        <option value="SSP">SSP</option>
                        <option value="GAZOIL">Diesel / Gazoil</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="text-[10.5px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0] uppercase mb-1 block">Prix (MAD / Litre) *</label>
                    <input type="number" step="0.001" name="nouveau_prix" placeholder="Ex: 14.500" required class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] focus:border-[#00F5A0] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 font-black font-mono text-[#00F5A0] text-base outline-none">
                </div>

                <div class="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042]">
                    <label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="modal_prix_immediate" name="activer_immediat" value="1" checked onchange="togglePrixModalDate(this.checked)" class="w-4 h-4 rounded accent-[#00F5A0] cursor-pointer">
                        <span class="text-[10.5px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 uppercase">Activer immédiatement</span>
                    </label>
                </div>

                <div id="modal_prix_date_wrapper" class="hidden space-y-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042]">
                    <label class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase block tracking-wider text-sky-500">
                        Date & Heure d'effet (Automatique)
                    </label>
                    <input type="datetime-local" id="input_prix_date_effet" name="date_effet" value="{now_iso}" class="w-full bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#183042] rounded-lg p-2 font-mono text-xs text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] flex justify-end gap-1.5">
                <button type="button" onclick="toggleModal(false)" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white">Annuler</button>
                <button type="submit" class="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#00F5A0] text-slate-950 font-black text-xs uppercase shadow-xs hover:bg-[#00D084] transition active:scale-95">Enregistrer</button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>

    <script>
        function togglePrixModalDate(isImmediate) {{
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('modal_prix_date_wrapper');
            const dateInput = document.getElementById('input_prix_date_effet');
            if (isImmediate) {{
                wrapper.classList.add('hidden');
                dateInput.removeAttribute('required');
            }} else {{
                wrapper.classList.remove('hidden');
                dateInput.setAttribute('required', 'required');
            }}
        }}

        function toggleModal(show) {{
            const modal = document.getElementById('modalAddPrix');
            modal.classList.toggle('hidden', !show);
            if (show) {{
                const chk = document.getElementById('modal_prix_immediate');
                chk.checked = true;
                togglePrixModalDate(true);
            }}
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "prix", request=request))

@router.post("/update-prix")
async def update_prix(
    produit: str = Form(...), 
    nouveau_prix: float = Form(...),
    activer_immediat: str = Form(None),
    date_effet: str = Form(None)
):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    ensure_schema(conn)
    cibles = ['GAZOIL', 'Gasoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] if produit in ['GAZOIL', 'Gasoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else ['SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb']
    save_name = 'GAZOIL' if produit in ['GAZOIL', 'Gasoil', 'Diesel', 'GO', 'Gasoil 10'] else 'SSP'
    placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(cibles))
    
    is_immediate = (activer_immediat == "1")
    now_dt = datetime.now()

    if is_immediate or not date_effet:
        date_formatted = now_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
        conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIF')",
            (date_formatted, date_formatted, save_name, nouveau_prix)
        )
    else:
        try:
            scheduled_dt = datetime.strptime(date_effet, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M")
            date_formatted = scheduled_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
        except Exception:
            scheduled_dt = now_dt
            date_formatted = date_effet

        if scheduled_dt <= now_dt:
            conn.execute(f"UPDATE prix_vente SET statut = 'INACTIF' WHERE produit IN ({placeholders})", cibles)
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'ACTIF')",
                (date_formatted, date_formatted, save_name, nouveau_prix)
            )
        else:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO prix_vente (date, date_effet, produit, prix, statut) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PROGRAMME')",
                (date_formatted, date_formatted, save_name, nouveau_prix)
            )

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/prix", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

FICHIER : .\pages\rapport.py
==================================================

# pages/rapport.py
import json
import html
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Form, status
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection, init_db

from pages.rapport_compteurs import router as compteurs_router
from pages.rapport_historique import router as historique_router

router = APIRouter()
router.include_router(compteurs_router)
router.include_router(historique_router)

@router.get("/rapport-quart", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def rapport_page(request: Request, tab: str = "supervision", success: bool = False, msg: str = ""):
    if tab == "compteurs":
        return RedirectResponse("/rapport-quart/compteurs")
    if tab == "historique":
        return RedirectResponse("/rapport-quart/historique")

    init_db()
    conn = get_db_connection()

    user = getattr(request.state, "user", None)
    nom_operateur = (user.get("nom_complet", "Opérateur") if user else "Opérateur").upper()
    role = str(user.get("role", "employe") if user else "employe").lower()
    is_admin = role in ["admin", "administrateur", "gerant", "comptable"]
    today_sql = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    p_ssp = conn.execute("SELECT prix FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb') AND statut='ACTIF' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    p_go = conn.execute("SELECT prix FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('GAZOIL', 'Gasoil', 'GO', 'Diesel', 'Gasoil 10') AND statut='ACTIF' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    prix_ssp = float(p_ssp['prix']) if p_ssp else 15.340
    prix_go = float(p_go['prix']) if p_go else 14.280

    pompes_ssp = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8]
    pompes_go = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7]

    def get_pump_vol(c_type, p_id):
        types_c = ('ESS', 'SSP') if c_type in ('ESS', 'SSP') else ('DIE', 'GO')
        row = conn.execute("""
            SELECT SUM(volume) as total_vol FROM transactions_jour 
            WHERE date_releve = ? AND type IN (?, ?) AND pump_id = ?
        """, (today_sql, types_c[0], types_c[1], p_id)).fetchone()
        return float(row['total_vol']) if (row and row['total_vol']) else 0.0

    total_ssp_l = sum(get_pump_vol('SSP', p) for p in pompes_ssp)
    total_go_l = sum(get_pump_vol('GO', p) for p in pompes_go)
    total_ssp_dh = total_ssp_l * prix_ssp
    total_go_dh = total_go_l * prix_go
    total_fusion_theorique = total_ssp_dh + total_go_dh
    total_volume_total = (total_ssp_l + total_go_l) or 1.0
    pct_vol_ssp = (total_ssp_l / total_volume_total) * 100
    pct_vol_go = (total_go_l / total_volume_total) * 100

    alert_banner = ""
    if success:
        alert_banner = f"""
        <div class="bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-between shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/20 mb-3 backdrop-blur-md">
            <span class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="p-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2.5" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"/></svg>
                </span>
                {html.escape(msg or 'Opération enregistrée avec succès !')}
            </span>
            <a href="/rapport-quart" class="text-xs text-emerald-400/80 hover:text-emerald-300 font-bold px-2 py-1">✕</a>
        </div>
        """

    if is_admin:
        derniere = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM cloture_quart ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
        conn.close()

        d_op = derniere['operateur'] if derniere else "ANASS"
        d_date_full = derniere['date'] if derniere else datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        date_parts = d_date_full.split()
        d_date_only = date_parts[0] if len(date_parts) > 0 else today_sql
        d_time_only = date_parts[1] if len(date_parts) > 1 else "00:00:00"

        d_esp = float(derniere['especes'] or 0) if derniere else 0.0
        d_tpe = float(derniere['tpe'] or 0) if derniere else 0.0
        d_cred = float(derniere['credits'] or 0) if derniere else 0.0
        d_fus = float(derniere['fusion'] or 0) if derniere else 0.0
        d_net = float(derniere['total_net'] or 0) if derniere else (d_esp + d_tpe + d_cred)
        d_res = float(derniere['resultat'] or 0) if derniere else (d_net - d_fus)

        total_pay = d_esp + d_tpe + d_cred
        pct_especes = (d_esp / total_pay * 100) if total_pay > 0 else 100.0
        pct_digital = ((d_tpe + d_cred) / total_pay * 100) if total_pay > 0 else 0.0

        if abs(d_res) < 0.01:
            ecart_montant_str = "0.00 DH"
            ecart_label_str = "CAISSE CONFORME"
            ecart_glow = "from-emerald-500/10 to-teal-500/5"
            ecart_text_color = "text-emerald-400"
            badge_class = "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30"
        elif d_res > 0:
            ecart_montant_str = f"+{d_res:,.2f} DH"
            ecart_label_str = "SURPLUS EN CAISSE"
            ecart_glow = "from-emerald-500/20 via-emerald-950/20 to-transparent"
            ecart_text_color = "text-[#00F5A0]"
            badge_class = "bg-emerald-500/20 text-[#00F5A0] border-emerald-500/40 shadow-emerald-500/10"
        else:
            ecart_montant_str = f"{d_res:,.2f} DH"
            ecart_label_str = "MANQUANT / DÉFICIT"
            ecart_glow = "from-rose-500/20 via-rose-950/20 to-transparent"
            ecart_text_color = "text-rose-400"
            badge_class = "bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/40 shadow-rose-500/10"

        content = f"""
        <div class="h-full flex flex-col gap-3.5 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
            {alert_banner}

                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-3.5">
                    
                    <div class="md:col-span-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800/90 backdrop-blur-xl p-4 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-xl shadow-black/20 relative overflow-hidden group">
                        <div class="absolute top-0 right-0 w-32 h-32 bg-sky-500/5 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none group-hover:bg-sky-500/10 transition-all duration-500"></div>
                        <div>
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-800/80 mb-3">
                                <span class="text-[11px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-sky-400 animate-pulse"></span>
                                    Détails du Quart
                                </span>
                                <span class="text-[10px] font-mono font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700/50">
                                    POSTE CLÔTURÉ
                                </span>
                            </div>

                            <div class="space-y-2.5">
                                <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                                    <span class="text-slate-400 text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                        <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M16 7a4 4 0 11-8 0 4 4 0 018 0zM12 14a7 7 0 00-7 7h14a7 7 0 00-7-7z"/></svg>
                                        Opérateur

                            </div>

                </div>

        """
        return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "supervision", request=request))

    clients_actifs = conn.execute("SELECT nom, code FROM clients WHERE statut='ACTIF' ORDER BY nom ASC").fetchall()
    clients_options = "".join([f'<option value="{html.escape(c["nom"])}">{html.escape(c["nom"])} ({html.escape(c["code"])})</option>' for c in clients_actifs])

    billets = [
        {"v": 200, "label": "200 DH"},
        {"v": 100, "label": "100 DH"},

    content = f"""

        {alert_banner}

        <div class="bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center shadow-lg shadow-black/20 backdrop-blur-xl">

                    {nom_operateur[:2]}
                </div>

                </div>

                <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse"></span>
                Quart Actif
            </span>

        <form action="/rapport-quart/valider" method="post" onsubmit="return confirm('Confirmer et clôturer votre caisse ?')" class="space-y-3 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between">
            <input type="hidden" name="operateur" value="{nom_operateur}">
            <input type="hidden" name="fusion" value="{total_fusion_theorique}">
            <input type="hidden" id="credits_agent_json" name="credits_json" value="[]">

            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-3.5 flex-1 overflow-y-auto pr-0.5 custom-scrollbar">
                
                <div class="lg:col-span-7 bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3.5 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-xl shadow-black/20">
                    <div class="flex justify-between items-center pb-2.5 border-b border-slate-800/80">

                            Comptage du Cash
                        </span>

                                <input type="number" min="0" placeholder="0" data-val="{p['v']}" name="qte_{str(p['v']).replace('.','_')}" oninput="calculerCaisseAgent()" class="agent-input w-full text-center bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-700 focus:border-emerald-500 rounded-lg py-0.5 font-mono font-black text-xs text-white outline-none transition">
                            </div>

                        </div>

                <div class="lg:col-span-5 flex flex-col gap-3.5">
                    <div class="bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl p-3.5 backdrop-blur-xl space-y-2">
                        <label class="block text-[10px] font-extrabold text-sky-400 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-between">
                            <span>Tickets TPE / Carte Bancaire</span>
                            <span class="text-slate-500">DH</span>
                        </label>
                        <input type="number" step="0.01" id="agent_tpe" name="tpe" value="0.00" oninput="calculerCaisseAgent()" class="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-950/80 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 focus:border-sky-500 font-mono font-bold text-sm text-white outline-none transition">
                    </div>

                    <div class="bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl p-3.5 backdrop-blur-xl flex flex-col flex-1 shadow-xl shadow-black/20">
                        <div class="flex justify-between items-center pb-2 border-b border-slate-800/80 mb-2">
                            <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-rose-400">Bons de Crédits</span>
                            <button type="button" onclick="ajouterCreditAgent()" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-rose-500/10 hover:bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1">+ Nouveau Bon</button>
                        </div>
                        <div id="liste_credits_agent" class="space-y-1.5 max-h-[120px] overflow-y-auto pr-1 custom-scrollbar"></div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-slate-900 to-slate-950 text-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl flex justify-between items-center font-mono border border-slate-800 shadow-2xl">
                        <div>
                            <span class="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block font-sans">Total Caisse Encaissé</span>
                            <span class="text-xl font-black text-[#00F5A0]" id="txt_total_agent">0,00 DH</span>
                        </div>
                        <button type="submit" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-amber-400 hover:from-amber-400 hover:to-amber-300 text-slate-950 font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-wider rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 active:scale-95 transition-all">
                            Clôturer Mon Quart
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>

    <script>
        let creditsAgent = [];
        const clientsOptions = `{clients_options}`;

        function ajouterCreditAgent() {{
            creditsAgent.push({{ id: Date.now(), nom: '', montant: 0 }});
            renderCreditsAgent();
        }}
        function supprimerCreditAgent(id) {{
            creditsAgent = creditsAgent.filter(c => c.id !== id);
            renderCreditsAgent();
            calculerCaisseAgent();
        }}
        function updateCreditAgent(id, field, val) {{
            const c = creditsAgent.find(x => x.id === id);
            if (c) c[field] = field === 'montant' ? (parseFloat(val) || 0) : val;
            calculerCaisseAgent();
        }}
        function renderCreditsAgent() {{
            let h = '';
            creditsAgent.forEach(c => {{
                h += `
                <div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-1.5 items-center bg-slate-800/40 p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/60">
                    <select onchange="updateCreditAgent(${{c.id}}, 'nom', this.value)" class="col-span-7 bg-slate-950 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-1 text-[10px] outline-none">
                        <option value="">Sélectionner Client...</option>${{clientsOptions}}
                    </select>
                    <input type="number" step="0.01" placeholder="0.00" value="${{c.montant || ''}}" oninput="updateCreditAgent(${{c.id}}, 'montant', this.value)" class="col-span-4 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-1 text-right text-rose-400 font-mono text-[10px] outline-none">
                    <button type="button" onclick="supprimerCreditAgent(${{c.id}})" class="col-span-1 text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300 font-bold text-center">✕</button>
                </div>
                `;
            }});
            document.getElementById('liste_credits_agent').innerHTML = h;
        }}
        function calculerCaisseAgent() {{
            let esp = 0;
            document.querySelectorAll('.agent-input').forEach(i => {{
                esp += ((parseFloat(i.getAttribute('data-val')) || 0) * (parseInt(i.value) || 0));
            }});
            const tpe = parseFloat(document.getElementById('agent_tpe').value) || 0;
            const cred = creditsAgent.reduce((s, c) => s + (parseFloat(c.montant) || 0), 0);
            const total = esp + tpe + cred;
            document.getElementById('txt_total_especes').innerText = esp.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' DH';
            document.getElementById('txt_total_agent').innerText = total.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' DH';
            document.getElementById('credits_agent_json').value = JSON.stringify(creditsAgent);
        }}
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "supervision", request=request))

@router.post("/rapport-quart/valider")
async def valider_rapport_post(fusion: float = Form(0.0), 
                               tpe: float = Form(0.0), 
                               operateur: str = Form("Opérateur"),
                               credits_json: str = Form("[]"),
                               qte_200: int = Form(0), qte_100: int = Form(0), qte_50: int = Form(0), 
                               qte_20: int = Form(0), qte_10: int = Form(0), qte_5: int = Form(0), 
                               qte_2: int = Form(0), qte_1: int = Form(0), qte_0_5: int = Form(0)):
    especes = (qte_200 * 200) + (qte_100 * 100) + (qte_50 * 50) + (qte_20 * 20) + (qte_10 * 10) + (qte_5 * 5) + (qte_2 * 2) + (qte_1 * 1) + (qte_0_5 * 0.5)
    try:
        total_credits = sum([float(i.get('montant', 0) or 0) for i in json.loads(credits_json)])
    except Exception:
        total_credits = 0.0

    total_net = especes + tpe + total_credits
    resultat = total_net - fusion if fusion > 0 else 0.0

    conn = get_db_connection()
    conn.execute(
        '''INSERT INTO cloture_quart (date, operateur, fusion, tpe, especes, credits, total_net, resultat, details_json, credits_json) 
           VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)''',
        (datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), operateur, fusion, tpe, especes, total_credits, total_net, resultat, "{}", credits_json)
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return RedirectResponse(url="/rapport-quart?success=true&msg=Clôture+enregistrée+avec+succès", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)

# pages/rapport_compteurs.py
import json
from datetime import datetime
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, UploadFile, File
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse

from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection, init_db
from fusion_reader import parse_fusion_transactions, parse_fusion_text

router = APIRouter()

@router.post("/rapport-quart/import-fusion")
async def import_fusion(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    try:
        content = await file.read()
        res = parse_fusion_transactions(content)
        return JSONResponse({"status": "success", "indexes": res["indexes"], "transactions": res["transactions"]})
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"status": "error", "message": str(e)}, status_code=400)

@router.post("/rapport-quart/parse-clipboard")
async def parse_clipboard(request: Request):
    try:
        data = await request.json()
        res = parse_fusion_text(data.get("text", ""))
        return JSONResponse({"status": "success", "indexes": res["indexes"], "transactions": res["transactions"]})
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"status": "error", "message": str(e)}, status_code=400)

@router.post("/rapport-quart/save-pompes")
async def save_pompes(request: Request):
    try:
        init_db()
        data = await request.json()
        now = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        date_jour = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        conn = get_db_connection()
        cursor = conn.cursor()

        for item in data.get("compteurs", []):
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT INTO compteurs (date, type, pompe_id, index_val) 
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (now, str(item.get("type")), int(item.get("pompe_id")), float(item.get("index_val", 0.0))))

        trans_rows = [
            (
                date_jour,
                str(t.get("sale_id", "")),
                str(t.get("date", t.get("end_date", date_jour))),
                str(t.get("heure", t.get("end_time", ""))),
                int(t.get("pump_id", 0)),
                str(t.get("type", "")),
                str(t.get("carburant", "")),
                float(t.get("volume", 0.0)),
                float(t.get("money", 0.0)),
                float(t.get("initial_vol", 0.0)),
                float(t.get("final_vol", 0.0))
            )
            for t in data.get("transactions", [])
        ]

        if trans_rows:
            cursor.executemany("""
                INSERT INTO transactions_jour (
                    date_releve, sale_id, date_vente, heure, pump_id, type, carburant, volume, money, initial_vol, final_vol
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, trans_rows)

        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        return JSONResponse({"status": "success", "message": f"{len(trans_rows)} transactions enregistrées !"})
    except Exception as e:
        return JSONResponse({"status": "error", "message": str(e)}, status_code=500)

@router.get("/rapport-quart/compteurs", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_compteurs(request: Request):
    init_db()
    conn = get_db_connection()
    today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
    today_sql = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    p_ssp = conn.execute("SELECT prix FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('SSP', 'Essence', 'Sans Plomb') AND statut='ACTIF' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    p_go = conn.execute("SELECT prix FROM prix_vente WHERE produit IN ('GO', 'Diesel', 'Gasoil 10') AND statut='ACTIF' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    prix_ssp = float(p_ssp['prix']) if p_ssp else 15.340
    prix_go = float(p_go['prix']) if p_go else 14.280

    pompes_ssp = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8]
    pompes_go = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7]

    def get_pump_day_stats(c_type, p_id):
        try:
            types_comp = ('ESS', 'SSP') if c_type in ('ESS', 'SSP') else ('DIE', 'GO')
            row = conn.execute("""
                SELECT SUM(volume) as total_vol, MIN(NULLIF(initial_vol, 0)) as min_init, MAX(final_vol) as max_fin
                FROM transactions_jour 
                WHERE date_releve = ? AND type IN (?, ?) AND pump_id = ?
            """, (today_sql, types_comp[0], types_comp[1], p_id)).fetchone()

            vol = float(row['total_vol']) if (row and row['total_vol'] is not None) else 0.00
            last_idx = conn.execute(
                "SELECT index_val FROM compteurs WHERE type IN (?, ?) AND pompe_id=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
                (types_comp[0], types_comp[1], p_id)
            ).fetchone()
            fb_idx = float(last_idx['index_val']) if last_idx and last_idx['index_val'] else 0.00
            init_v = float(row['min_init']) if (row and row['min_init']) else (fb_idx - vol if fb_idx > 0 else 0.00)
            fin_v = float(row['max_fin']) if (row and row['max_fin']) else fb_idx
            return vol, max(0.00, init_v), fin_v
        except Exception:
            return 0.00, 0.00, 0.00

    stats_ssp = {p: get_pump_day_stats('SSP', p) for p in pompes_ssp}
    stats_go = {p: get_pump_day_stats('GO', p) for p in pompes_go}
    total_ssp_l = sum(stats_ssp[p][0] for p in pompes_ssp)
    total_go_l = sum(stats_go[p][0] for p in pompes_go)
    total_fusion_theorique = (total_ssp_l * prix_ssp) + (total_go_l * prix_go)

    raw_trans = conn.execute("""
        SELECT id, date_releve, COALESCE(NULLIF(date_vente, ''), date_releve) as date_v,
               heure, sale_id, pump_id, type, carburant, volume, money, initial_vol, final_vol 
        FROM transactions_jour ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1500
    """).fetchall()

    transactions_list = [{
        "id": r["id"], "date": r["date_v"], "heure": r["heure"], "sale_id": r["sale_id"], "pump_id": r["pump_id"],
        "type": "SSP" if r["type"] in ["ESS", "SSP"] else "GO",
        "carburant": "SSP" if ("SSP" in str(r["carburant"]).upper() or "ESS" in str(r["carburant"]).upper()) else "GO",
        "volume": float(r["volume"] or 0.0), "money": float(r["money"] or 0.0),
        "initial_vol": float(r["initial_vol"] or 0.0), "final_vol": float(r["final_vol"] or 0.0)
    } for r in raw_trans]

    conn.close()

    content = f"""
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/xlsx@0.18.5/dist/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>

                    </div>

                            <th class="py-2 px-2.5 text-right text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0] font-bold">Montant (DH)</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="modalHistoriqueTableBody" class="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-[#183042]/50 font-mono text-[11px]"></tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <div class="p-2.5 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] flex justify-between items-center shrink-0">
                <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono">
                    Volume : <span id="totalVolumeFiltre" class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">0.00 L</span> | 
                    Montant : <span id="totalMontantFiltre" class="font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]">0.00 DH</span>

                <button onclick="toggleModalHistorique(false)" class="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">Fermer</button>

    <!-- MODALE COLLER EXCEL -->
    <div id="modalCollerExcel" class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 dark:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-xs hidden z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3">
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl p-3.5 shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-2.5">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center pb-1.5 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <h3 class="text-xs font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider">Coller Données Fusion / Excel</h3>
                <button onclick="fermerModalColler()" class="text-slate-400 hover:text-white font-bold text-xs">✕</button>
            </div>
            <textarea id="zoneColler" rows="6" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#183042] rounded-lg p-2.5 text-xs font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200 outline-none" placeholder="Collez vos lignes de vente ici (Ctrl+V)..."></textarea>
            <div class="flex justify-end gap-1.5">
                <button onclick="fermerModalColler()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold">Annuler</button>
                <button onclick="traiterZoneColler()" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider transition shadow-sm">Analyser & Sauvegarder</button>
            </div>

    <script>
        const TOUTES_LES_TRANSACTIONS = {json.dumps(transactions_list)};
        let transactionsFiltrees = [];
        let limiteAffichage = 100;

        function changerVueSecteur(vue) {{
            const ssp = document.getElementById('secteur-ssp');
            const go = document.getElementById('secteur-go');
            const grid = document.getElementById('container-grilles');
            const btnAll = document.getElementById('btn-vue-all');
            const btnSsp = document.getElementById('btn-vue-ssp');
            const btnGo = document.getElementById('btn-vue-go');

            btnAll.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white transition text-[11px]";
            btnSsp.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-emerald-500 transition flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px]";
            btnGo.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-500 transition flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px]";

            if (vue === 'ALL') {{
                ssp.classList.remove('hidden');
                go.classList.remove('hidden');
                grid.className = "grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 flex-1 pb-2 overflow-y-auto";
                btnAll.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white bg-white dark:bg-[#184E68] shadow-xs transition text-[11px]";
            }} else if (vue === 'SSP') {{
                ssp.classList.remove('hidden');
                go.classList.add('hidden');
                grid.className = "grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5 flex-1 max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full pb-2 overflow-y-auto";
                btnSsp.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0] bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-500/20 shadow-xs transition flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px]";
            }} else if (vue === 'GO') {{
                ssp.classList.add('hidden');
                go.classList.remove('hidden');
                grid.className = "grid grid-cols-1 gap-2.5 flex-1 max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full pb-2 overflow-y-auto";
                btnGo.className = "px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-[#E5A842] bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-500/20 shadow-xs transition flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px]";
            }}
        }}

        function toggleModalHistorique(show) {{
            const m = document.getElementById('modalHistorique');
            if (show) {{
                m.classList.remove('hidden');
                document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                limiteAffichage = 100;
                filtrerHistoriqueRapide();
            }} else {{
                m.classList.add('hidden');
                document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
            }}
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function reinitialiserFiltres() {{
            document.getElementById('filtreDateModal').value = "";
            document.getElementById('filtreHeureDebut').value = "";
            document.getElementById('filtreHeureFin').value = "";
            document.getElementById('filtrePompeModal').value = "ALL";
            limiteAffichage = 100;
            filtrerHistoriqueRapide();
        }}

        function filtrerHistoriqueRapide() {{
            const fDate = document.getElementById('filtreDateModal').value;
            const fHeureDeb = document.getElementById('filtreHeureDebut').value;
            const fHeureFin = document.getElementById('filtreHeureFin').value;
            const fPompe = document.getElementById('filtrePompeModal').value;

            let sumVol = 0, sumMoney = 0;
            transactionsFiltrees = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < TOUTES_LES_TRANSACTIONS.length; i++) {{
                const t = TOUTES_LES_TRANSACTIONS[i];
                if (fDate) {{
                    const [y, m, d] = fDate.split('-');
                    const fDateFR = `${{d}}/${{m}}/${{y}}`;
                    if (t.date !== fDate && t.date !== fDateFR && !t.date.startsWith(fDate)) continue;
                }}
                if (t.heure) {{
                    const hSimple = t.heure.substring(0, 5);
                    if (fHeureDeb && hSimple < fHeureDeb) continue;
                    if (fHeureFin && hSimple > fHeureFin) continue;
                }}
                if (fPompe !== 'ALL') {{
                    const [cType, pId] = fPompe.split('_');
                    if (t.type !== cType || String(t.pump_id) !== pId) continue;
                }}
                transactionsFiltrees.push(t);
                sumVol += t.volume;
                sumMoney += t.money;
            }}

            document.getElementById('totalVolumeFiltre').innerText = sumVol.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' L';
            document.getElementById('totalMontantFiltre').innerText = sumMoney.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' DH';
            rendreTableauHistorique();
        }}

        function rendreTableauHistorique() {{
            const total = transactionsFiltrees.length;
            if (total === 0) {{
                document.getElementById('modalHistoriqueTableBody').innerHTML = `
                    <tr><td colspan="9" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400 font-sans text-xs italic">Aucune transaction trouvée.</td></tr>
                `;
                return;
            }}

            let htmlRows = "";
            const limite = Math.min(limiteAffichage, total);
            for (let i = 0; i < limite; i++) {{
                const t = transactionsFiltrees[i];
                const badgeColor = t.type === 'SSP' 
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-500/10 dark:text-[#00F5A0]' 
                    : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 dark:bg-amber-500/10 dark:text-[#E5A842]';
                const moneyColor = t.type === 'SSP' ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]' : 'text-amber-600 dark:text-[#E5A842]';

                htmlRows += `
                <tr class="border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/50 hover:bg-slate-50/80 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02]">
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 font-medium">${{t.date}}</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-sky-600 dark:text-[#38BDF8] font-semibold">${{t.heure}}</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-200">#${{t.sale_id}}</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold">Pompe ${{t.pump_id}}</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5"><span class="text-[8.5px] font-black uppercase px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-md ${{badgeColor}}">${{t.carburant}}</span></td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-right text-slate-400">${{t.initial_vol.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-right text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-medium">${{t.final_vol.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-right font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${{t.volume.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}} L</td>
                    <td class="py-1.5 px-2.5 text-right font-black ${{moneyColor}}">${{t.money.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}} DH</td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }}
            document.getElementById('modalHistoriqueTableBody').innerHTML = htmlRows;
        }}

        function ouvrirModalColler() {{
            document.getElementById('zoneColler').value = "";
            document.getElementById('modalCollerExcel').classList.remove('hidden');
            setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('zoneColler').focus(), 100);
        }}

        function fermerModalColler() {{
            document.getElementById('modalCollerExcel').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        async function traiterZoneColler() {{
            const rawText = document.getElementById('zoneColler').value;
            if (!rawText.trim()) return alert("Veuillez coller les données.");
            fermerModalColler();
            await envoyerTextePourTraitement(rawText);
        }}

        async function envoyerTextePourTraitement(text) {{
            try {{
                const res = await fetch('/rapport-quart/parse-clipboard', {{
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
                    body: JSON.stringify({{ text: text }})
                }});
                const result = await res.json();
                if (res.ok && result.transactions && result.transactions.length > 0) {{
                    await sauvegarderDirectement(result.indexes, result.transactions);
                }} else {{
                    alert("⚠️ Aucune transaction exploitable trouvée.");
                }}
            }} catch (err) {{
                alert("❌ Erreur : " + err);
            }}
        }}

        async function uploadFusionFile(event) {{
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;

            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = async function(e) {{
                try {{
                    const data = new Uint8Array(e.target.result);
                    const workbook = XLSX.read(data, {{type: 'array'}});
                    const tsvText = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_csv(workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]], {{FS: "\\t"}});
                    await envoyerTextePourTraitement(tsvText);
                }} catch (err) {{
                    const tr = new FileReader();
                    tr.onload = async function(te) {{ await envoyerTextePourTraitement(te.target.result); }};
                    tr.readAsText(file, 'utf-8');
                }}
            }};
            reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
            event.target.value = "";
        }}

        async function sauvegarderDirectement(indexes, transactions) {{
            const compteurs = [];
            for (const [pId, val] of Object.entries(indexes.SSP || indexes.ESS || {{}})) {{
                compteurs.push({{ type: 'ESS', pompe_id: parseInt(pId), index_val: parseFloat(val) }});
            }}
            for (const [pId, val] of Object.entries(indexes.GO || indexes.DIE || {{}})) {{
                compteurs.push({{ type: 'DIE', pompe_id: parseInt(pId), index_val: parseFloat(val) }});
            }}

            try {{
                const response = await fetch('/rapport-quart/save-pompes', {{
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {{ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }},
                    body: JSON.stringify({{ compteurs: compteurs, transactions: transactions }})
                }});
                const res = await response.json();
                alert(response.ok ? '✅ ' + res.message : '❌ ' + res.message);
                if (response.ok) window.location.reload();
            }} catch (err) {{
                alert('❌ Erreur de sauvegarde : ' + err);
            }}
        }}
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "compteurs", request=request))

FICHIER : .\pages\rapport_dettes_fournisseurs.py
==================================================

# ==============================================================================
# FICHIER : pages/rapport_dettes_fournisseurs.py
