import html
import json
import base64
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse

from database import get_db_connection
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

def mad(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

@router.get("/centre-rapports/dettes-fournisseurs", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_rapport_dettes_fournisseurs(request: Request):
    conn = get_db_connection()

    # Récupération de tous les fournisseurs avec achats et règlements calculés
    query = """
        SELECT f.*,
               COALESCE((SELECT SUM(montant_total) FROM achats_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = f.id), 0.0) AS total_achats,
               COALESCE((SELECT SUM(montant) FROM reglements_fournisseurs WHERE fournisseur_id = f.id), 0.0) AS total_reglements
        FROM fournisseurs f
        ORDER BY f.nom ASC
    """
    rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    fournisseurs_list = []
    options_fournisseurs_html = '<option value="ALL">Tous les fournisseurs</option>'

    for r in rows:
        fid = r["id"]
        f_nom = (r["nom"] or "Fournisseur Sans Nom").strip()
        f_code = (r["code"] or f"FOUR-{fid:03d}").strip()
        f_type = (r["type_fournisseur"] or "Carburant").strip()
        solde_init = float(r["solde_initial"] or 0.0)
        tot_achats = float(r["total_achats"] or 0.0)
        tot_paye = float(r["total_reglements"] or 0.0)
        tel_val = (r["telephone"] or "—").strip()
        email_val = (r["email"] or "—").strip()
        statut_val = (r["statut"] or "Actif").strip()

        # Calcul comptable : Solde net dû au fournisseur
        solde_net = (solde_init + tot_achats) - tot_paye
        dette = solde_net if solde_net > 0 else 0.0
        avoir = abs(solde_net) if solde_net < 0 else 0.0

        fournisseurs_list.append({
            "id": fid,
            "nom": f_nom,
            "code": f_code,
            "type_fournisseur": f_type,
            "telephone": tel_val,
            "email": email_val,
            "total_achats": tot_achats,
            "total_paye": tot_paye,
            "dette": dette,
            "avoir": avoir,
            "solde_net": solde_net,
            "statut": statut_val
        })

        options_fournisseurs_html += f'<option value="{fid}">{html.escape(f_nom)} ({html.escape(f_code)})</option>'

    # Encodage sécurisé en Base64 pour le JS (aucun risque de bug de guillemets)
    fournisseurs_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(fournisseurs_list).encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    content = f"""
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
        
        <!-- 1. EN-TÊTE + LES 3 FILTRES CONNECTÉS -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs space-y-3">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-sky-400 to-cyan-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold shadow-md shadow-sky-500/20">
                        <i data-lucide="truck" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <h1 class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-sm uppercase tracking-wider">
                            État des Dettes Fournisseurs
                        </h1>
                        <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Suivi des dettes, BL d'achats et soldes d'avoirs fournisseurs</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <a href="/centre-rapports" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] dark:hover:bg-[#1b354d] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold transition">
                    <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                    <span>Retour Hub</span>
                </a>
            </div>

            <!-- FILTRES : TYPE DE FOURNISSEUR | FOURNISSEUR | FILTRE SOLDE -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 items-end">

                    <select id="filtreTypeFournisseur" onchange="filtrerDirectFournisseurs()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer transition">

    <!-- SCRIPT DU COMPOSANT : CALCULS, FILTRAGE INSTANTANÉ ET MODALE -->
    <script>
        const RAW_B64_FOURN = "{fournisseurs_b64}";
        const TOUS_LES_FOURNISSEURS = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(RAW_B64_FOURN))));

        let fournisseursFiltresActifs = [];
        let typeModalFournActif = 'ALL';
        let estPleinEcranFourn = false;

        function badgeTypeFournisseur(type) {{
            const t = (type || 'Carburant').toLowerCase();
            if (t.includes('carburant')) {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-[#E5A842] border border-amber-300/40">Carburant</span>';
            }} else if (t.includes('lubrifiant')) {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 dark:bg-purple-500/20 dark:text-purple-300 border border-purple-300/40">Lubrifiant</span>';
            }} else if (t.includes('service')) {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 dark:bg-sky-500/20 dark:text-sky-300 border border-sky-300/40">Services</span>';
            }}
            return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-700/40 dark:text-slate-200 border border-slate-300/40">' + htmlEscape(type) + '</span>';
        }}

        function htmlEscape(str) {{
            return String(str || '').replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
        }}

        function filtrerDirectFournisseurs() {{
            const fType = document.getElementById('filtreTypeFournisseur').value;
            const fFournId = document.getElementById('filtreFournisseur').value;
            const fSolde = document.getElementById('filtreSoldeFourn').value;

            fournisseursFiltresActifs = [];
            let sumDettes = 0;
            let sumAvoirs = 0;
            let sumNet = 0;

            let parType = {{}};

            for (let i = 0; i < TOUS_LES_FOURNISSEURS.length; i++) {{
                const f = TOUS_LES_FOURNISSEURS[i];

                if (fType !== 'ALL' && f.type_fournisseur.toLowerCase() !== fType.toLowerCase()) continue;
                if (fFournId !== 'ALL' && String(f.id) !== String(fFournId)) continue;

                if (fSolde === 'DETTE' && f.dette <= 0.001) continue;
                if (fSolde === 'AVOIR' && f.avoir <= 0.001) continue;
                if (fSolde === 'SOLDE' && (f.dette > 0.001 || f.avoir > 0.001)) continue;

                fournisseursFiltresActifs.push(f);

                sumDettes += f.dette;
                sumAvoirs += f.avoir;
                sumNet += f.solde_net;

                const t = f.type_fournisseur || 'Carburant';
                if (!parType[t]) parType[t] = {{ count: 0, dettes: 0, avoirs: 0 }};
                parType[t].count++;
                parType[t].dettes += f.dette;
                parType[t].avoirs += f.avoir;
            }}

            // 1. Mise à jour des 4 KPIs
            document.getElementById('kpiNbFourn').innerText = fournisseursFiltresActifs.length;
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalDettesFourn').innerText = sumDettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalAvoirsFourn').innerText = sumAvoirs.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});
            document.getElementById('kpiSoldeNetFourn').innerText = sumNet.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});

            // 2. Remplissage du tableau Récapitulatif par Type de Fournisseur (Image 4)
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recapFournTbody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';

            for (const [nomType, info] of Object.entries(parType)) {{
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-50/50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02] cursor-pointer";
                tr.onclick = function() {{ ouvrirModalFourn(nomType); }};

                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">${{badgeTypeFournisseur(nomType)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">${{info.count}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">${{info.dettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]">${{info.avoirs.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
                        <button type="button" onclick="ouvrirModalFourn('${{nomType}}')" 
                                class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#184E68] hover:bg-sky-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1 mx-auto cursor-pointer shadow-xs">
                            <i data-lucide="list" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                            <span>Détails</span>
                        </button>
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            }}

            document.getElementById('tfootNbFourn').innerText = fournisseursFiltresActifs.length;
            document.getElementById('tfootDettesFourn').innerText = sumDettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});
            document.getElementById('tfootAvoirsFourn').innerText = sumAvoirs.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});

            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function ouvrirModalFourn(type) {{
            typeModalFournActif = type;
            document.getElementById('modalRechercheLibreFourn').value = '';
            majPillsTypeFourn();
            appliquerFiltresModalFourn();
            document.getElementById('modal-dettes-fourn').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}

        function setModalTypeFourn(type) {{
            typeModalFournActif = type;
            majPillsTypeFourn();
            appliquerFiltresModalFourn();
        }}

        function majPillsTypeFourn() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-type-pill-fourn').forEach(btn => {{
                btn.className = "btn-type-pill-fourn px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white shrink-0";
            }});
            const actif = document.getElementById('btn-type-fourn-' + typeModalFournActif);
            if (actif) actif.className = "btn-type-pill-fourn px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-sm shrink-0";
            document.getElementById('modal-type-titre-fourn').innerText = (typeModalFournActif === 'ALL') ? 'TOUS LES TYPES' : typeModalFournActif.toUpperCase();
        }}

        function appliquerFiltresModalFourn() {{
            const search = document.getElementById('modalRechercheLibreFourn').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            let lignes = fournisseursFiltresActifs;

            if (typeModalFournActif !== 'ALL') {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(f => f.type_fournisseur.toLowerCase() === typeModalFournActif.toLowerCase());
            }}

            if (search) {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(f => 
                    f.nom.toLowerCase().includes(search) || 
                    f.code.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                    f.telephone.toLowerCase().includes(search)
                );
            }}

            afficherLignesModalFourn(lignes);
        }}

        function afficherLignesModalFourn(lignes) {{
            const tbody = document.getElementById('modalTbodyFourn');
            const empty = document.getElementById('modalEmptyFourn');
            const compteur = document.getElementById('modalCompteurFourn');
            const totDettesEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalDettesVal');
            const totAvoirsEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalAvoirsVal');
            const totNetEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalNetVal');

            compteur.innerText = `${{lignes.length}} fournisseur(s)`;

            let sumDettes = 0;
            let sumAvoirs = 0;
            let sumNet = 0;

            if (lignes.length === 0) {{
                tbody.innerHTML = '';
                empty.classList.remove('hidden');
                totDettesEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                totAvoirsEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                totNetEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                return;
            }}

            empty.classList.add('hidden');
            let h = '';
            lignes.forEach(f => {{
                sumDettes += f.dette;
                sumAvoirs += f.avoir;
                sumNet += f.solde_net;

                h += `
                <tr class="hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors border-b border-[#183042]/40">
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-sky-400 text-xs">${{htmlEscape(f.code)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-100 uppercase">${{htmlEscape(f.nom)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">${{badgeTypeFournisseur(f.type_fournisseur)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-slate-300 font-semibold">${{f.total_achats.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-teal-400 font-semibold">${{f.total_paye.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-rose-500">${{f.dette.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-emerald-400">${{f.avoir.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-white">${{f.solde_net.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <a href="/fournisseurs?id=${{f.id}}" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400 inline-flex items-center justify-center transition" title="Ouvrir dossier fournisseur">
                            <i data-lucide="external-link" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                        </a>
                    </td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }});

            tbody.innerHTML = h;
            totDettesEl.innerText = sumDettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            totAvoirsEl.innerText = sumAvoirs.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            totNetEl.innerText = sumNet.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function toggleAgrandirModalFourn() {{
            const box = document.getElementById('modal-box-fourn');
            const icon = document.getElementById('icon-maximize-fourn');
            estPleinEcranFourn = !estPleinEcranFourn;

            if (estPleinEcranFourn) {{
                box.classList.remove('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.add('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'minimize-2');
            }} else {{
                box.classList.add('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.remove('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'maximize-2');
            }}
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function fermerModalFourn() {{
            document.getElementById('modal-dettes-fourn').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {{
            if (e.key === 'Escape') fermerModalFourn();
        }});

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            filtrerDirectFournisseurs();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="centre_rapports", request=request))

# ==============================================================================
# FICHIER : pages/rapport_encaissements.py (CORRIGÉ & TESTÉ)
# ==============================================================================

import html
import json
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse

from database import get_db_connection
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

def mad(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

def normaliser_date_iso(date_str: str) -> str:
    """Transforme n'importe quel format (DD/MM/YYYY ou YYYY-MM-DD) en YYYY-MM-DD pur."""
    if not date_str:
        return ""
    clean = str(date_str).strip().split(" ")[0]
    if "/" in clean:
        parts = clean.split("/")
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return f"{parts[2]}-{parts[1].zfill(2)}-{parts[0].zfill(2)}"
    elif "-" in clean:
        parts = clean.split("-")
        if len(parts) == 3:
            if len(parts[0]) == 4:
                return f"{parts[0]}-{parts[1].zfill(2)}-{parts[2].zfill(2)}"
            else:
                return f"{parts[2]}-{parts[1].zfill(2)}-{parts[0].zfill(2)}"
    return clean

def formater_date_fr(date_str: str) -> str:
    iso = normaliser_date_iso(date_str)
    if not iso or len(iso) < 10:
        return date_str or "—"
    parts = iso.split("-")
    return f"{parts[2]}/{parts[1]}/{parts[0]}"

@router.get("/centre-rapports/encaissements", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_rapport_encaissements(
    request: Request,
    client_id: Optional[str] = "ALL",
    date_debut: Optional[str] = None,
    date_fin: Optional[str] = None
):
    today = datetime.now()
    d_debut = date_debut or (today - timedelta(days=120)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    d_fin = date_fin or today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    conn = get_db_connection()
    
    # 1. Récupération des clients pour le select
    clients_rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, nom, code FROM clients ORDER BY nom ASC").fetchall()
    clients_options = '<option value="ALL">Tous les clients</option>'
    for c in clients_rows:
        c_nom = html.escape(c["nom"] or "")
        c_code = html.escape(c["code"] or "")
        clients_options += f'<option value="{c["id"]}">{c_nom} ({c_code})</option>'

    # 2. Récupération de tous les encaissements
    query = """
        SELECT e.*, 
               COALESCE(NULLIF(c.nom, ''), NULLIF(e.client_nom_libre, ''), 'Client Passager') as client_nom, 
               COALESCE(c.code, 'PASSAGER') as client_code
        FROM client_encaissements e
        LEFT JOIN clients c ON e.client_id = c.id
        ORDER BY e.date_paiement DESC, e.id DESC
    """
    rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    encaissements_list = []
    for r in rows:
        d_p = str(r["date_paiement"] or "")
        d_iso = normaliser_date_iso(d_p)
        
        m_raw = (r["mode_paiement"] or "Espèces").strip()
        m_lower = m_raw.lower()

        if "chèque" in m_lower or "cheque" in m_lower:
            m_norm = "Chèque"
        elif "vir" in m_lower:
            m_norm = "Virement"
        elif "tpe" in m_lower or "carte" in m_lower:
            m_norm = "Carte Bancaire / TPE"
        else:
            m_norm = "Espèces"

        st_val = (r["statut"] or "Validé").strip()

        encaissements_list.append({
            "id": r["id"],
            "client_id": str(r["client_id"] or 0),
            "date_iso": d_iso,
            "date_fr": formater_date_fr(d_p),
            "client_nom": r["client_nom"],
            "client_code": r["client_code"],
            "mode": m_norm,
            "reference": r["reference"] or "-",
            "montant": float(r["montant"] or 0.0),
            "statut": st_val,
            "banque": r["banque"] or "-",
            "description": r["description"] or "-"
        })

    # Encodage sécurisé en Base64 pour éliminer tout bug de parsing JS
    encaissements_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(encaissements_list).encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    content = f"""
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
        
        <!-- 1. EN-TÊTE DU RAPPORT -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs space-y-3">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                        <i data-lucide="wallet" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </div>
                    <h1 class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-sm uppercase tracking-wider">
                        Rapport des Encaissements & Règlements
                    </h1>
                </div>

                <a href="/centre-rapports" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold transition">
                    <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                    <span>Retour Hub</span>
                </a>
            </div>

            <!-- FILTRES RESPONSIVES INSTANTANÉS -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3 items-end">
                <div class="sm:col-span-4">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Client</label>
                    <select id="filtreClient" onchange="filtrerDirect()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                        {clients_options}
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Mode de Paiement</label>
                    <select id="filtreMode" onchange="filtrerDirect()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                        <option value="ALL">Tous les modes</option>
                        <option value="Espèces">Espèces</option>
                        <option value="Chèque">Chèque</option>
                        <option value="Virement">Virement</option>
                        <option value="Carte Bancaire / TPE">TPE / Carte</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Statut</label>

                        <option value="ALL">Tous les statuts</option>
                        <option value="Validé">Validé</option>
                        <option value="En attente">En attente</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Date début</label>
                    <input type="date" id="filtreDateDebut" value="{d_debut}" onchange="filtrerDirect()" oninput="filtrerDirect()" 
                           class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Date fin</label>
                    <input type="date" id="filtreDateFin" value="{d_fin}" onchange="filtrerDirect()" oninput="filtrerDirect()" 
                           class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 2. LES 4 CARTES KPI SYNTHÉTIQUES -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] border-t-4 border-t-purple-500 shadow-xs text-center">
                <div id="kpiTotalMnt" class="text-xl font-black font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white">0,00 DH</div>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400 mt-1 block">Total Global Encaissé</span>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] border-t-4 border-t-blue-500 shadow-xs text-center">
                <div id="kpiNbTotal" class="text-xl font-black font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white">0</div>
                <span class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400 mt-1 block">Nombre d'Encaissements</span>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] border-t-4 border-t-teal-500 shadow-xs text-center">
                <div id="kpiEspMnt" class="text-xl font-black font-mono text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400">0,00 DH</div>
                <div class="flex justify-center gap-2 text-[10.5px] font-bold text-slate-400 mt-1">
                    <span>Espèces</span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span id="kpiEspCount" class="font-mono">0 opérations</span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] border-t-4 border-t-amber-500 shadow-xs text-center">
                <div id="kpiChqMnt" class="text-xl font-black font-mono text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">0,00 DH</div>
                <div class="flex justify-center gap-2 text-[10.5px] font-bold text-slate-400 mt-1">

        </div>

    </div>

    <!-- ============================================================================== -->
    <!-- 4. MODALE POPUP DE DÉTAILS DES ENCAISSEMENTS -->
    <!-- ============================================================================== -->
    <div id="modal-encaissements" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4 hidden transition-all">
        
        <div id="modal-box" class="bg-[#0A1622] border border-[#1b3449] rounded-2xl w-full max-w-5xl h-[88vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl overflow-hidden transition-all duration-200">
            
            <!-- EN-TÊTE MODALE -->
            <div class="px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#1b3449] bg-[#071018] flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-400 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                        <i data-lucide="wallet" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </div>
                    <span class="font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-200">
                        Détails des Encaissements
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div class="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/15 border border-purple-400/30 flex items-center gap-2 shadow-xs">
                    <i data-lucide="calendar" class="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400"></i>
                    <span id="modal-date-titre" class="text-sm font-black font-mono tracking-widest text-purple-300">
                        ----/--/--
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button type="button" onclick="toggleAgrandirModal()" title="Agrandir / Réduire" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer transition">
                        <i id="icon-maximize" data-lucide="maximize-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="fermerModal()" title="Fermer" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:text-rose-400 text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer transition">
                        <i data-lucide="x" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- FILTRES INTERNES MODALE -->
            <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-[#0d1d2c] border-b border-[#1b3449] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 shrink-0">

                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalMode('TOUS')" id="btn-mode-TOUS" class="btn-mode-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-purple-600 text-white">Tous</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalMode('Espèces')" id="btn-mode-Espèces" class="btn-mode-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Espèces</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalMode('Chèque')" id="btn-mode-Chèque" class="btn-mode-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Chèque</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalMode('Virement')" id="btn-mode-Virement" class="btn-mode-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Virement</button>

                </div>

                    <thead class="bg-[#122435] sticky top-0 z-20 border-b-2 border-[#1E3A52] shadow-sm">

                    </tbody>

                    Aucun encaissement trouvé.

    <!-- MOTEUR JS INTERACTIF SÉCURISÉ -->
    <script>
        const RAW_B64 = "{encaissements_b64}";
        const DONNEES = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(RAW_B64))));
        
        let encaissementsFiltres = [];
        let modeModalActif = 'TOUS';
        let estPleinEcran = false;

        function badgeMode(mode) {{
            const m = (mode || '').toLowerCase();
            if (m.includes('chèque') || m.includes('cheque')) {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30">Chèque</span>';
            }} else if (m.includes('virement')) {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/30">Virement</span>';
            }} else if (m.includes('carte') || m.includes('tpe')) {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/30">TPE / Carte</span>';
            }}
            return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-teal-500/15 text-teal-400 border border-teal-500/30">Espèces</span>';
        }}

        function filtrerDirect() {{
            const fClient = document.getElementById('filtreClient').value;
            const fMode = document.getElementById('filtreMode').value;
            const fStatut = document.getElementById('filtreStatut').value;
            const dDeb = document.getElementById('filtreDateDebut').value;
            const dFin = document.getElementById('filtreDateFin').value;

            const labelDeb = dDeb ? dDeb.split('-').reverse().join('/') : '--';
            const labelFin = dFin ? dFin.split('-').reverse().join('/') : '--';
            document.getElementById('labelPeriode').innerText = `Du ${{labelDeb}} au ${{labelFin}}`;
            document.getElementById('modal-date-titre').innerText = `${{labelDeb}} ➔ ${{labelFin}}`;

            encaissementsFiltres = [];
            let totalMnt = 0;
            let totEspMnt = 0, totEspCount = 0;
            let totChqMnt = 0, totChqCount = 0;
            let parMode = {{}};

            for (let i = 0; i < DONNEES.length; i++) {{
                const e = DONNEES[i];

                // Comparaison fiable sur les dates ISO (YYYY-MM-DD)
                if (dDeb && e.date_iso && e.date_iso < dDeb) continue;
                if (dFin && e.date_iso && e.date_iso > dFin) continue;
                if (fClient !== 'ALL' && String(e.client_id) !== String(fClient)) continue;
                if (fMode !== 'ALL' && e.mode.toLowerCase() !== fMode.toLowerCase()) continue;
                if (fStatut !== 'ALL' && e.statut.toLowerCase() !== fStatut.toLowerCase()) continue;

                encaissementsFiltres.push(e);
                totalMnt += e.montant;

                const m = e.mode;
                if (!parMode[m]) parMode[m] = {{ count: 0, mnt: 0 }};
                parMode[m].count++;
                parMode[m].mnt += e.montant;

                if (m === 'Espèces') {{
                    totEspMnt += e.montant;
                    totEspCount++;
                }} else {{
                    totChqMnt += e.montant;
                    totChqCount++;
                }}
            }}

            // 1. Mise à jour des 4 cartes KPI
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalMnt').innerText = totalMnt.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            document.getElementById('kpiNbTotal').innerText = encaissementsFiltres.length;
            document.getElementById('kpiEspMnt').innerText = totEspMnt.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            document.getElementById('kpiEspCount').innerText = `${{totEspCount}} opérations`;
            document.getElementById('kpiChqMnt').innerText = totChqMnt.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            document.getElementById('kpiChqCount').innerText = `${{totChqCount}} opérations`;

            // 2. Remplissage du tableau de synthèse par mode
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recapTableBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';

            for (const [modeNom, info] of Object.entries(parMode)) {{
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-50/50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02]";
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4">${{badgeMode(modeNom)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">${{info.count}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${{info.mnt.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <button type="button" onclick="ouvrirModalMode('${{modeNom}}')" 
                                class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#184E68] hover:bg-sky-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1 mx-auto cursor-pointer shadow-xs">
                            <i data-lucide="list" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                            <span>Détails</span>
                        </button>
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            }}

            document.getElementById('tfootCount').innerText = encaissementsFiltres.length;
            document.getElementById('tfootMnt').innerText = totalMnt.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';

            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function ouvrirModalMode(mode) {{
            modeModalActif = mode;
            document.getElementById('modalRechercheLibre').value = '';
            majPillsMode();
            appliquerFiltresModal();
            document.getElementById('modal-encaissements').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}

        function setModalMode(mode) {{
            modeModalActif = mode;
            majPillsMode();
            appliquerFiltresModal();
        }}

        function majPillsMode() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-mode-pill').forEach(btn => {{
                btn.className = "btn-mode-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white";
            }});
            const actif = document.getElementById('btn-mode-' + modeModalActif);
            if (actif) actif.className = "btn-mode-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-purple-600 text-white shadow-sm";
        }}

        function appliquerFiltresModal() {{
            const search = document.getElementById('modalRechercheLibre').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            let lignes = encaissementsFiltres;

            if (modeModalActif !== 'TOUS') {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(l => l.mode.toLowerCase() === modeModalActif.toLowerCase());
            }}

            if (search) {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(l => 
                    l.client_nom.toLowerCase().includes(search) || 
                    l.reference.toLowerCase().includes(search) || 
                    l.date_fr.includes(search)
                );
            }}

            afficherLignesModal(lignes);
        }}

        function afficherLignesModal(lignes) {{
            const tbody = document.getElementById('modalTbody');
            const empty = document.getElementById('modalEmpty');
            const compteur = document.getElementById('modalCompteur');
            const totalMntEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalMnt');

            compteur.innerText = `${{lignes.length}} ligne(s)`;

            let sum = 0;
            if (lignes.length === 0) {{
                tbody.innerHTML = '';
                empty.classList.remove('hidden');
                totalMntEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                return;
            }}

            empty.classList.add('hidden');
            let h = '';
            lignes.forEach(l => {{
                sum += l.montant;
                h += `
                <tr class="hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors border-b border-[#183042]/40">
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-purple-400 text-xs">${{l.date_fr}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-100 uppercase">${{l.client_nom}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">${{badgeMode(l.mode)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center font-mono text-slate-400">${{l.reference}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-white">${{l.montant.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10.5px] font-extrabold bg-teal-500/15 text-teal-400 border border-teal-500/30">
                            ${{l.statut}}
                        </span>
                    </td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }});

            tbody.innerHTML = h;
            totalMntEl.innerText = sum.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
        }}

        function toggleAgrandirModal() {{
            const box = document.getElementById('modal-box');
            const icon = document.getElementById('icon-maximize');
            estPleinEcran = !estPleinEcran;

            if (estPleinEcran) {{
                box.classList.remove('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.add('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'minimize-2');
            }} else {{
                box.classList.add('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.remove('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'maximize-2');
            }}
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function fermerModal() {{
            document.getElementById('modal-encaissements').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {{
            if (e.key === 'Escape') fermerModal();
        }});

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            filtrerDirect();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="centre_rapports", request=request))
==================================================

# ==============================================================================
# FICHIER : pages/rapport_etat_stocks.py (AVEC MODALE HISTORIQUE EXACTE)
# ==============================================================================

import html
import json
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Form
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse

from database import get_db_connection, get_fournisseurs, get_prix_carburant_a_date
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

def mad(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

def format_l(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.0f}".replace(",", " ")
    except Exception:
        return "0"

CITERNES_CONFIG = {
    "C1": {
        "nom": "C1",
        "carburant": "Essence",
        "badge_color": "bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 dark:bg-sky-500/20 dark:text-sky-300 border-sky-300/40",
        "capacite": 31600.0,
        "seuil_alerte": 5000.0,
        "stock_initial": 15000.0
    },
    "C2": {
        "nom": "C2",
        "carburant": "Diesel",
        "badge_color": "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-[#E5A842] border-amber-300/40",
        "capacite": 60500.0,
        "seuil_alerte": 8000.0,
        "stock_initial": 35000.0
    }
}

PRODUITS_DIVERS_REF = [
    {"produit": "10W40 (5L)", "categorie": "Lubrifiant", "stock_initial": 45, "prix_unitaire": 250.0, "seuil": 10},
    {"produit": "10W40 (1L)", "categorie": "Lubrifiant", "stock_initial": 80, "prix_unitaire": 55.0, "seuil": 15},
    {"produit": "15W40 CF4 (5L)", "categorie": "Lubrifiant", "stock_initial": 30, "prix_unitaire": 200.0, "seuil": 8},
    {"produit": "15W40 CF4 (1L)", "categorie": "Lubrifiant", "stock_initial": 55, "prix_unitaire": 42.0, "seuil": 12},
    {"produit": "SAE 50 (5L)", "categorie": "Lubrifiant", "stock_initial": 25, "prix_unitaire": 185.0, "seuil": 5},
    {"produit": "Liquide Refroidissement", "categorie": "Entretien", "stock_initial": 40, "prix_unitaire": 60.0, "seuil": 10},
    {"produit": "Lave-Glace", "categorie": "Entretien", "stock_initial": 50, "prix_unitaire": 25.0, "seuil": 10}
]

@router.api_route("/centre-rapports/etat-stocks", methods=["GET", "POST"], response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_etat_stocks(
    request: Request,
    station: Optional[str] = Form("OKAY ENERGY"),
    type_stock: Optional[str] = Form("TOUS"),
    date_debut: Optional[str] = Form(None),
    date_fin: Optional[str] = Form(None)
):
    if request.method == "GET":
        qp = request.query_params
        station = qp.get("station", "OKAY ENERGY")
        type_stock = qp.get("type_stock", "TOUS")
        date_debut = qp.get("date_debut")
        date_fin = qp.get("date_fin")

    today = datetime.now()
    d_debut = date_debut or (today - timedelta(days=30)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    d_fin = date_fin or today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    conn = get_db_connection()

    # 1. Total des tirages pompes sur la période
    pompes_sorties = conn.execute("""
        SELECT 
            SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('ESS', 'SSP') THEN volume ELSE 0 END) as sorties_essence,
            SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('DIE', 'GO', 'GAZOIL') THEN volume ELSE 0 END) as sorties_diesel
        FROM transactions_jour
        WHERE COALESCE(NULLIF(substr(date_vente, 1, 10), ''), date_releve) BETWEEN ? AND ?
    """, (d_debut, d_fin)).fetchone()

    sorties_ess = float(pompes_sorties["sorties_essence"] or 0.0) if pompes_sorties else 0.0
    sorties_die = float(pompes_sorties["sorties_diesel"] or 0.0) if pompes_sorties else 0.0

    # 2. Entrées d'achats BL
    achats_lignes = conn.execute("""
        SELECT a.date, a.produit, a.volume_litres, a.lignes_json
        FROM achats_fournisseurs a
        WHERE a.date BETWEEN ? AND ?
    """, (d_debut, d_fin)).fetchall()

    entrees_ess = 0.0
    entrees_die = 0.0

    for a in achats_lignes:
        p_main = (a["produit"] or "").upper()
        v_main = float(a["volume_litres"] or 0.0)
        lignes_json = a["lignes_json"] or "[]"
        try:
            items = json.loads(lignes_json)
        except Exception:
            items = []

        if items:
            for it in items:
                p_it = str(it.get("produit", "")).upper()
                q_it = float(it.get("qte", 0.0))
                if any(k in p_it for k in ["ESS", "SSP", "SUPER"]):
                    entrees_ess += q_it
                elif any(k in p_it for k in ["DIE", "GASOIL", "GAZOIL", "GO"]):
                    entrees_die += q_it
        else:
            if any(k in p_main for k in ["ESS", "SSP", "SUPER"]):
                entrees_ess += v_main
            elif any(k in p_main for k in ["DIE", "GASOIL", "GAZOIL", "GO"]):
                entrees_die += v_main

    # 3. Stock Actuel Citernes
    c1_cap = CITERNES_CONFIG["C1"]["capacite"]
    c1_init = CITERNES_CONFIG["C1"]["stock_initial"]
    c1_actuel = max(0.0, min(c1_cap, c1_init + entrees_ess - sorties_ess))
    c1_pct = (c1_actuel / c1_cap * 100.0) if c1_cap > 0 else 0.0

    c2_cap = CITERNES_CONFIG["C2"]["capacite"]
    c2_init = CITERNES_CONFIG["C2"]["stock_initial"]
    c2_actuel = max(0.0, min(c2_cap, c2_init + entrees_die - sorties_die))
    c2_pct = (c2_actuel / c2_cap * 100.0) if c2_cap > 0 else 0.0

    total_capacite_carb = c1_cap + c2_cap
    total_stock_carb = c1_actuel + c2_actuel
    taux_remplissage_global = (total_stock_carb / total_capacite_carb * 100.0) if total_capacite_carb > 0 else 0.0

    # 4. Génération de l'historique par date pour la modale
    # Reconstitution journalière chronologique
    ventes_par_jour_raw = conn.execute("""
        SELECT 
            COALESCE(NULLIF(substr(date_vente, 1, 10), ''), date_releve) as jour,
            SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('ESS', 'SSP') THEN volume ELSE 0 END) as v_ess,
            SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('DIE', 'GO', 'GAZOIL') THEN volume ELSE 0 END) as v_die
        FROM transactions_jour
        WHERE COALESCE(NULLIF(substr(date_vente, 1, 10), ''), date_releve) BETWEEN ? AND ?
        GROUP BY jour
        ORDER BY jour DESC
    """, (d_debut, d_fin)).fetchall()

    dict_ventes = {r["jour"]: (float(r["v_ess"] or 0), float(r["v_die"] or 0)) for r in ventes_par_jour_raw}

    historique_modal = []
    # Dates entre date_fin et date_debut (ordre décroissant)
    try:
        dt_start = datetime.strptime(d_debut, "%Y-%m-%d")
        dt_end = datetime.strptime(d_fin, "%Y-%m-%d")
    except Exception:
        dt_start = today - timedelta(days=30)
        dt_end = today

    curr_dt = dt_end
    run_c1 = c1_actuel
    run_c2 = c2_actuel

    while curr_dt >= dt_start:
        j_iso = curr_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        j_fr = curr_dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
        v_ess_j, v_die_j = dict_ventes.get(j_iso, (0.0, 0.0))

        prix_die = get_prix_carburant_a_date("GAZOIL", j_iso)
        prix_ess = get_prix_carburant_a_date("SSP", j_iso)

        pct_die = (run_c2 / c2_cap * 100.0) if c2_cap > 0 else 0.0
        val_die = run_c2 * prix_die

        pct_ess = (run_c1 / c1_cap * 100.0) if c1_cap > 0 else 0.0
        val_ess = run_c1 * prix_ess

        # Ligne Diesel
        historique_modal.append({
            "date": j_fr,
            "date_iso": j_iso,
            "carburant": "Diesel",
            "type_carb": "DIE",
            "capacite": c2_cap,
            "stock": run_c2,
            "taux": pct_die,
            "valeur": val_die
        })

        # Ligne Essence
        historique_modal.append({
            "date": j_fr,
            "date_iso": j_iso,
            "carburant": "Essence",
            "type_carb": "ESS",
            "capacite": c1_cap,
            "stock": run_c1,
            "taux": pct_ess,
            "valeur": val_ess
        })

        # Recul théorique
        run_c1 = max(0.0, min(c1_cap, run_c1 + v_ess_j))
        run_c2 = max(0.0, min(c2_cap, run_c2 + v_die_j))

        curr_dt -= timedelta(days=1)

    # 5. Produits boutique
    produits_calcules = []
    valeur_stock_produits = 0.0
    alertes_count = 0

    if c1_actuel <= CITERNES_CONFIG["C1"]["seuil_alerte"]:
        alertes_count += 1
    if c2_actuel <= CITERNES_CONFIG["C2"]["seuil_alerte"]:
        alertes_count += 1

    for prod in PRODUITS_DIVERS_REF:
        qte_restante = prod["stock_initial"]
        val_produit = qte_restante * prod["prix_unitaire"]
        valeur_stock_produits += val_produit
        is_bas = qte_restante <= prod["seuil"]
        if is_bas:
            alertes_count += 1

        produits_calcules.append({
            "produit": prod["produit"],
            "categorie": prod["categorie"],
            "stock_actuel": qte_restante,
            "prix_unitaire": prod["prix_unitaire"],
            "valeur_stock": val_produit,
            "is_bas": is_bas
        })

    conn.close()

    def get_bar_color(pct: float) -> str:
        if pct < 20.0:
            return "bg-rose-500"
        elif pct < 45.0:
            return "bg-amber-500"
        return "bg-emerald-500"

    c1_bar_color = get_bar_color(c1_pct)
    c2_bar_color = get_bar_color(c2_pct)
    global_bar_color = get_bar_color(taux_remplissage_global)

    c1_badge_statut = '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10.5px] font-bold bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-500 border border-rose-500/30">⚠ Bas</span>' if c1_actuel <= CITERNES_CONFIG["C1"]["seuil_alerte"] else '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10.5px] font-bold bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500">Normal</span>'
    c2_badge_statut = '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10.5px] font-bold bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-500 border border-rose-500/30">⚠ Bas</span>' if c2_actuel <= CITERNES_CONFIG["C2"]["seuil_alerte"] else '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10.5px] font-bold bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500">Normal</span>'

    lignes_produits_html = ""
    for p in produits_calcules:
        badge_p = '<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10.5px] font-bold bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-500 border border-rose-500/30">Stock Faible</span>' if p["is_bas"] else '<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10.5px] font-bold bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500">Optimal</span>'
        lignes_produits_html += f"""
        <tr class="border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/50 hover:bg-slate-50/50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02] text-xs font-semibold">
            <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{html.escape(p['produit'])}</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{html.escape(p['categorie'])}</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{p['stock_actuel']} unités</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">{mad(p['prix_unitaire'])} DH</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{mad(p['valeur_stock'])} DH</td>
            <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">{badge_p}</td>
        </tr>
        """

    historique_json_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(historique_modal).encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")

    content = f"""

            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">

                <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#071018] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[#1e3a52]">

                    <button type="button" onclick="setFiltreCarbModal('ALL')" id="btn-modal-carb-ALL" class="btn-carb-pill-modal px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-purple-600 text-white">Tous</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setFiltreCarbModal('Essence')" id="btn-modal-carb-Essence" class="btn-carb-pill-modal px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Essence</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setFiltreCarbModal('Diesel')" id="btn-modal-carb-Diesel" class="btn-carb-pill-modal px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Diesel</button>
                </div>

                <div class="relative w-48 sm:w-64">
                    <input type="text" id="modalRechercheDateStock" oninput="appliquerFiltresModalStock()" placeholder="Rechercher une date (ex: 31/10)..." 
                           class="w-full bg-[#071018] border border-[#1e3a52] rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-purple-300 placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-purple-400">
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- STATISTIQUES RAPIDES DU FILTRE MODALE -->
            <div class="px-5 py-2 bg-[#091520] border-b border-[#1b3449]/70 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 shrink-0">
                <span id="modalStockCompteur" class="text-purple-400 font-mono">0 ligne(s)</span>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-4 font-mono">
                    <span>Valeur Sélectionnée : <strong id="modalStockTotalVal" class="text-white font-black">0,00 DH</strong></span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- TABLEAU EXACT DE L'IMAGE 1 AVEC LES EN-TÊTES IDENTIQUES -->
            <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-[#071018]">
                <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                    <thead class="bg-[#122435] sticky top-0 z-20 border-b-2 border-[#1E3A52] shadow-sm">
                        <tr class="text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] font-black uppercase text-white tracking-wider">
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-purple-300">Date</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-white">Type Carburant</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-right text-white">Capacité (L)</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-right text-white">Stock Actuel (L)</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-left w-56 text-teal-300">Taux Remplissage</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-right text-white">Valeur Stock</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="modalStockTbody" class="divide-y divide-[#183042]/50 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 font-mono">
                    </tbody>
                </table>
                <div id="modalStockEmpty" class="text-center py-16 text-slate-500 font-bold hidden font-sans">
                    Aucun historique de stock trouvé pour les critères sélectionnés.
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- MOTEUR JAVASCRIPT COMPLET ET SYNCHRONE -->
    <script>
        const RAW_HISTO_B64 = "{historique_json_b64}";
        const DONNEES_HISTORIQUE = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(RAW_HISTO_B64))));

        let filtreCarbModalActif = 'ALL';
        let estPleinEcranStock = false;

        function badgeCarbModal(carb) {{
            if (carb.toLowerCase() === 'essence') {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 dark:bg-sky-500/20 dark:text-sky-300 border border-sky-300/40">Essence</span>';
            }}
            return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-[#E5A842] border border-amber-300/40">Diesel</span>';
        }}

        function ouvrirModalHistoriqueStock(carburantParDefaut) {{
            filtreCarbModalActif = carburantParDefaut || 'ALL';
            document.getElementById('modalRechercheDateStock').value = '';
            majPillsCarbModal();
            appliquerFiltresModalStock();
            document.getElementById('modal-historique-stock').classList.remove('hidden');
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function fermerModalHistoriqueStock() {{
            document.getElementById('modal-historique-stock').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        function setFiltreCarbModal(carb) {{
            filtreCarbModalActif = carb;
            majPillsCarbModal();
            appliquerFiltresModalStock();
        }}

        function majPillsCarbModal() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-carb-pill-modal').forEach(btn => {{
                btn.className = "btn-carb-pill-modal px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white";
            }});
            const actif = document.getElementById('btn-modal-carb-' + filtreCarbModalActif);
            if (actif) actif.className = "btn-carb-pill-modal px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-purple-600 text-white shadow-sm";
        }}

        function appliquerFiltresModalStock() {{
            const search = document.getElementById('modalRechercheDateStock').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            let lignes = DONNEES_HISTORIQUE;

            if (filtreCarbModalActif !== 'ALL') {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(l => l.carburant.toLowerCase() === filtreCarbModalActif.toLowerCase());
            }}

            if (search) {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(l => l.date.includes(search) || l.date_iso.includes(search));
            }}

            afficherLignesModalStock(lignes);
        }}

        function afficherLignesModalStock(lignes) {{
            const tbody = document.getElementById('modalStockTbody');
            const empty = document.getElementById('modalStockEmpty');
            const compteur = document.getElementById('modalStockCompteur');
            const totalValEl = document.getElementById('modalStockTotalVal');

            compteur.innerText = `${{lignes.length}} ligne(s)`;

            let sumValeur = 0;
            if (lignes.length === 0) {{
                tbody.innerHTML = '';
                empty.classList.remove('hidden');
                totalValEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                return;
            }}

            empty.classList.add('hidden');
            let h = '';
            lignes.forEach(l => {{
                sumValeur += l.valeur;
                const barColor = l.taux < 20 ? 'bg-rose-500' : (l.taux < 45 ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-500');
                const textColor = l.taux < 20 ? 'text-rose-400' : (l.taux < 45 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-emerald-400');

                h += `
                <tr class="hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors border-b border-[#183042]/40">
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-200">${{l.date}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-sans">${{badgeCarbModal(l.carburant)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right text-slate-300 font-bold">${{l.capacite.toLocaleString('fr-FR')}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-black text-white">${{l.stock.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <div class="flex-1 bg-[#071018] h-2.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-[#1e3a52]">
                                <div class="${{barColor}} h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300" style="width: ${{Math.min(100, Math.max(0, l.taux))}}%"></div>
                            </div>
                            <span class="${{textColor}} font-black text-xs w-14 text-right">${{l.taux.toFixed(1)}}%</span>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-black text-slate-100">${{l.valeur.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }});

            tbody.innerHTML = h;
            totalValEl.innerText = sumValeur.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
        }}

        function toggleAgrandirModalStock() {{
            const box = document.getElementById('modal-box-stock');
            const icon = document.getElementById('icon-maximize-stock');
            estPleinEcranStock = !estPleinEcranStock;

            if (estPleinEcranStock) {{
                box.classList.remove('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.add('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'minimize-2');
            }} else {{
                box.classList.add('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.remove('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'maximize-2');
            }}
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {{
            if (e.key === 'Escape') fermerModalHistoriqueStock();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="centre_rapports", request=request))
==================================================
FICHIER : .\pages\rapport_grand_livre.py
