# ==============================================================================
# FICHIER : pages/rapport_grand_livre.py (CORRIGÉ & ROBUSTE)
# ==============================================================================

import html
import json
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse

from database import get_db_connection
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

def mad(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

def normaliser_date_iso(date_str: str) -> str:
    if not date_str:
        return ""
    clean = str(date_str).strip().split(" ")[0]
    if "/" in clean:
        p = clean.split("/")
        if len(p) == 3:
            return f"{p[2]}-{p[1].zfill(2)}-{p[0].zfill(2)}"
    return clean

def formater_date_fr(date_str: str) -> str:
    iso = normaliser_date_iso(date_str)
    if not iso or len(iso) < 10:
        return date_str or "—"
    p = iso.split("-")
    return f"{p[2]}/{p[1]}/{p[0]}"

@router.get("/centre-rapports/grand-livre", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_grand_livre(
    request: Request,
    date_debut: Optional[str] = None,
    date_fin: Optional[str] = None
):
    today = datetime.now()
    d_debut_defaut = (today - timedelta(days=90)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    d_fin_defaut = today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    val_debut = date_debut or d_debut_defaut
    val_fin = date_fin or d_fin_defaut

    conn = get_db_connection()

    # 1. Conversion directe en dictionnaires Python pour éviter l'erreur sqlite3.Row.get()
    clients_rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM clients ORDER BY nom ASC").fetchall()]
    factures_rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM factures_clients ORDER BY date_facture ASC, id ASC").fetchall()]
    encaissements_rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM client_encaissements ORDER BY date_paiement ASC, id ASC").fetchall()]

    # 2. Fournisseurs & leurs écritures
    fournisseurs_rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM fournisseurs ORDER BY nom ASC").fetchall()]
    achats_rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM achats_fournisseurs ORDER BY date ASC, id ASC").fetchall()]
    reglements_rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM reglements_fournisseurs ORDER BY date ASC, id ASC").fetchall()]

    conn.close()

    # =========================================================================
    # RECONSTITUTION CHRONOLOGIQUE DES COMPTES DE TIERS
    # =========================================================================
    tiers_data = []

    # A. TRAITEMENT DES CLIENTS
    for c in clients_rows:
        cid = c["id"]
        c_nom = (c.get("nom") or "Client Sans Nom").strip()
        c_code = (c.get("code") or f"CL{cid:03d}").strip()
        solde_actuel = float(c.get("solde") or 0.0)

        mouvements = []

        # Factures / Ventes à crédit -> DÉBIT
        for f in factures_rows:
            if f.get("client_id") == cid:
                d_brute = f.get("date_facture") or ""
                num_piece = f.get("numero_facture") or f"FAC-{f.get('id')}"
                if not num_piece.startswith("VC-") and not num_piece.startswith("FAC"):
                    num_piece = f"VC-{num_piece}"
                montant = float(f.get("montant_ttc") or 0.0)
                mouvements.append({
                    "date_iso": normaliser_date_iso(d_brute),
                    "date_fr": formater_date_fr(d_brute),
                    "num_piece": num_piece,
                    "libelle": f.get("notes") or "Vente à crédit",
                    "debit": montant,
                    "credit": 0.0
                })

        # Encaissements / Règlements -> CRÉDIT
        for e in encaissements_rows:
            if e.get("client_id") == cid:
                d_brute = e.get("date_paiement") or ""
                ref = (e.get("reference") or "").strip()
                num_piece = ref if ref else f"ENC-{e.get('id')}"
                mode = e.get("mode_paiement") or "Espèces"
                montant = float(e.get("montant") or 0.0)
                mouvements.append({
                    "date_iso": normaliser_date_iso(d_brute),
                    "date_fr": formater_date_fr(d_brute),
                    "num_piece": num_piece,
                    "libelle": f"Encaissement ({mode})",
                    "debit": 0.0,
                    "credit": montant
                })

        # Tri chronologique des écritures
        mouvements.sort(key=lambda x: (x["date_iso"], x["num_piece"]))

        tiers_data.append({
            "id": f"cli_{cid}",
            "type_tiers": "Client",
            "nom": c_nom,
            "code": c_code,
            "solde_initial": 0.0,
            "solde_actuel": solde_actuel,
            "mouvements": mouvements
        })

    # B. TRAITEMENT DES FOURNISSEURS
    for f in fournisseurs_rows:
        fid = f["id"]
        f_nom = (f.get("nom") or "Fournisseur").strip()
        f_code = (f.get("code") or f"FOUR-{fid:03d}").strip()
        solde_init = float(f.get("solde_initial") or 0.0)

        mouvements = []

        # Achats / BL -> CRÉDIT (dette envers le fournisseur)
        for a in achats_rows:
            if a.get("fournisseur_id") == fid:
                d_brute = a.get("date") or ""
                num_piece = a.get("numero_facture") or a.get("reference_bl") or f"BL-{a.get('id')}"
                montant = float(a.get("montant_total") or 0.0)
                prod = a.get("produit") or "Carburant"
                mouvements.append({
                    "date_iso": normaliser_date_iso(d_brute),
                    "date_fr": formater_date_fr(d_brute),
                    "num_piece": num_piece,
                    "libelle": f"Achat / BL ({prod})",
                    "debit": 0.0,
                    "credit": montant
                })

        # Règlements -> DÉBIT (diminution de la dette)
        for r in reglements_rows:
            if r.get("fournisseur_id") == fid:
                d_brute = r.get("date") or ""
                num_piece = r.get("reference_paiement") or f"REG-{r.get('id')}"
                mode = r.get("mode_paiement") or "Virement"
                montant = float(r.get("montant") or 0.0)
                mouvements.append({
                    "date_iso": normaliser_date_iso(d_brute),
                    "date_fr": formater_date_fr(d_brute),
                    "num_piece": num_piece,
                    "libelle": f"Règlement ({mode})",
                    "debit": montant,
                    "credit": 0.0
                })

        mouvements.sort(key=lambda x: (x["date_iso"], x["num_piece"]))

        # Calcul solde fournisseur actuel
        tot_ach = sum(m["credit"] for m in mouvements)
        tot_pay = sum(m["debit"] for m in mouvements)
        solde_fourn = (solde_init + tot_ach) - tot_pay

        tiers_data.append({
            "id": f"fourn_{fid}",
            "type_tiers": "Fournisseur",
            "nom": f_nom,
            "code": f_code,
            "solde_initial": solde_init,
            "solde_actuel": solde_fourn,
            "mouvements": mouvements
        })

    # Encodage sécurisé en Base64 pour le JS
    tiers_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(tiers_data).encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    content = f"""
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
        
        <!-- 1. EN-TÊTE + BARRE DE FILTRES -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs space-y-3">
            
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black uppercase text-slate-800 dark:text-white tracking-wider">
                    <i data-lucide="filter" class="w-4 h-4 text-purple-500"></i>
                    <span>Filtres</span>
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button type="button" onclick="basculerTousAccordions(true)" class="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] dark:hover:bg-[#1b354d] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition cursor-pointer">
                        Déplier Tout
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="basculerTousAccordions(false)" class="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] dark:hover:bg-[#1b354d] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition cursor-pointer">
                        Replier Tout
                    </button>
                    <a href="/centre-rapports" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold transition">
                        <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                        <span>Retour Hub</span>
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- LES 4 CHAMPS DU FILTRE -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3 items-end">
                <div class="sm:col-span-3">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Société *</label>
                    <select id="filtreSociete" onchange="filtrerGrandLivre()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                        <option value="PETROLAZ" selected>PETROLAZ</option>
                        <option value="OKAY">OKAY ENERGY</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-3">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Type de Tiers</label>
                    <select id="filtreTypeTiers" onchange="filtrerGrandLivre()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="ALL" selected>Tous les tiers</option>
                        <option value="Client">Clients</option>
                        <option value="Fournisseur">Fournisseurs</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-3">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Date début</label>
                    <input type="date" id="filtreDateDebut" value="{val_debut}" onchange="filtrerGrandLivre()" oninput="filtrerGrandLivre()" 
                           class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-3">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Date fin</label>
                    <input type="date" id="filtreDateFin" value="{val_fin}" onchange="filtrerGrandLivre()" oninput="filtrerGrandLivre()" 
                           class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>

        <!-- 2. CONTENEUR DES ACCORDÉONS TIERS -->
        <div id="grandLivreContainer" class="space-y-3.5">
        </div>

        <div id="emptyGrandLivre" class="hidden text-center py-16 bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] rounded-2xl text-slate-400 font-bold italic">
            Aucun mouvement enregistré pour la sélection et la période indiquées.
        </div>

    </div>

    <!-- MOTEUR JS INTERACTIF DU GRAND LIVRE -->
    <script>
        const RAW_TIERS_B64 = "{tiers_b64}";
        const TOUS_LES_TIERS = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(RAW_TIERS_B64))));

        let etatsAccordions = {{}};

        function basculerTousAccordions(ouvrir) {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.tier-content-box').forEach(el => {{
                el.classList.toggle('hidden', !ouvrir);
            }});
            document.querySelectorAll('.chevron-tier').forEach(icon => {{
                icon.style.transform = ouvrir ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)';
            }});
        }}

        function toggleTier(tierId) {{
            const box = document.getElementById('box-' + tierId);
            const chevron = document.getElementById('chevron-' + tierId);
            if (!box) return;

            const estCache = box.classList.contains('hidden');
            box.classList.toggle('hidden', !estCache);
            etatsAccordions[tierId] = estCache;

            if (chevron) {{
                chevron.style.transform = estCache ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)';
            }}
        }}

        function badgeTiers(type) {{
            if (type === 'Client') {{
                return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-sky-100 text-sky-800 dark:bg-sky-500/20 dark:text-sky-300 border border-sky-300/40"><i data-lucide="user" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Client</span>';
            }}
            return '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-500/20 dark:text-[#E5A842] border border-amber-300/40"><i data-lucide="truck" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Fournisseur</span>';
        }}

        function filtrerGrandLivre() {{
            const fType = document.getElementById('filtreTypeTiers').value;
            const dDeb = document.getElementById('filtreDateDebut').value;
            const dFin = document.getElementById('filtreDateFin').value;

            const container = document.getElementById('grandLivreContainer');
            const emptyBox = document.getElementById('emptyGrandLivre');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            let nombreTiersAffiches = 0;

            TOUS_LES_TIERS.forEach(tier => {{
                // 1. Filtre type de tiers
                if (fType !== 'ALL' && tier.type_tiers !== fType) return;

                // 2. Filtre mouvements dans la période
                let mouvementsFiltres = [];
                let soldeInitialPeriode = tier.solde_initial || 0.0;

                tier.mouvements.forEach(m => {{
                    if (dDeb && m.date_iso && m.date_iso < dDeb) {{
                        if (tier.type_tiers === 'Client') {{
                            soldeInitialPeriode += (m.debit - m.credit);
                        }} else {{
                            soldeInitialPeriode += (m.credit - m.debit);
                        }}
                        return;
                    }}
                    if (dFin && m.date_iso && m.date_iso > dFin) return;

                    mouvementsFiltres.push(m);
                }});

                // Si aucun mouvement dans la période et aucun solde, on masque pour alléger
                if (mouvementsFiltres.length === 0 && Math.abs(soldeInitialPeriode) < 0.01 && Math.abs(tier.solde_actuel) < 0.01) {{
                    return;
                }}

                nombreTiersAffiches++;

                // CALCUL PROGRESSIF DU SOLDE LIGNE PAR LIGNE
                let soldeCourant = soldeInitialPeriode;
                let totalDebit = 0;
                let totalCredit = 0;

                let lignesTableHtml = '';

                mouvementsFiltres.forEach(m => {{
                    totalDebit += m.debit;
                    totalCredit += m.credit;

                    if (tier.type_tiers === 'Client') {{
                        soldeCourant += (m.debit - m.credit);
                    }} else {{
                        soldeCourant += (m.credit - m.debit);
                    }}

                    const debitStr = m.debit > 0 
                        ? `<span class="text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 font-bold">${{m.debit.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</span>`
                        : `<span class="text-slate-400">-</span>`;

                    const creditStr = m.credit > 0 
                        ? `<span class="text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0] font-bold">${{m.credit.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</span>`
                        : `<span class="text-slate-400">-</span>`;

                    const soldeStr = `<span class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${{soldeCourant.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</span>`;

                    lignesTableHtml += `
                    <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02] border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/50 text-xs">
                        <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">${{m.date_fr}}</td>
                        <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400">${{htmlEscape(m.num_piece)}}</td>
                        <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">${{htmlEscape(m.libelle)}}</td>
                        <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono">${{debitStr}}</td>
                        <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono">${{creditStr}}</td>
                        <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono">${{soldeStr}}</td>
                    </tr>
                    `;
                }});

                if (mouvementsFiltres.length === 0) {{
                    lignesTableHtml = `<tr><td colspan="6" class="py-4 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs italic">Aucun mouvement sur la période sélectionnée.</td></tr>`;
                }}

                const soldeActuelAffiche = (mouvementsFiltres.length > 0) ? soldeCourant : soldeInitialPeriode;
                const estDeplie = etatsAccordions[tier.id] !== false;

                const cardHtml = `
                <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] rounded-2xl shadow-xs overflow-hidden transition-all duration-200">
                    
                    <!-- EN-TÊTE ACCORDÉON TIERS -->
                    <div onclick="toggleTier('${{tier.id}}')" class="px-4 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-slate-50/70 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02] border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/60 transition">
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                            <i id="chevron-${{tier.id}}" data-lucide="chevron-down" class="chevron-tier w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200" style="transform: ${{estDeplie ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)'}}"></i>
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <h3 class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs sm:text-sm uppercase tracking-wide">
                                    ${{htmlEscape(tier.nom)}}
                                </h3>
                                ${{badgeTiers(tier.type_tiers)}}
                                <span class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 font-bold">(${{htmlEscape(tier.code)}})</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- SOLDES EN HAUT À DROITE -->
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs font-mono">
                            <div class="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Solde initial: <strong class="text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">${{soldeInitialPeriode.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</strong>
                            </div>
                            <span class="text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-700">|</span>
                            <div class="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Solde actuel: <strong class="text-slate-900 dark:text-white font-black">${{soldeActuelAffiche.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</strong>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- TABLEAU DES MOUVEMENTS -->
                    <div id="box-${{tier.id}}" class="tier-content-box overflow-x-auto ${{estDeplie ? '' : 'hidden'}}">
                        <table class="w-full text-left min-w-[700px]">
                            <thead class="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] text-[10.5px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042]">
                                <tr>
                                    <th class="py-2.5 px-4 w-28">DATE</th>
                                    <th class="py-2.5 px-4 w-32">N° PIÈCE</th>
                                    <th class="py-2.5 px-4">LIBELLÉ</th>
                                    <th class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right w-32">DÉBIT</th>
                                    <th class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right w-32">CRÉDIT</th>
                                    <th class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right w-32">SOLDE</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-[#183042]/50 font-semibold">
                                ${{lignesTableHtml}}
                            </tbody>
                            <tfoot class="bg-slate-50/80 dark:bg-[#070E15] border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] text-xs font-black">
                                <tr>
                                    <td colspan="3" class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right uppercase text-slate-900 dark:text-white">TOTAUX</td>
                                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">
                                        ${{totalDebit.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]">
                                        ${{totalCredit.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                                        ${{soldeActuelAffiche.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            </tfoot>
                        </table>
                    </div>

                </div>
                `;

                container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', cardHtml);
            }});

            emptyBox.classList.toggle('hidden', nombreTiersAffiches > 0);
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function htmlEscape(str) {{
            return String(str || '').replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
        }}

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            filtrerGrandLivre();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="centre_rapports", request=request))

# pages/rapport_historique.py
import json
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
from ui_utils import layout
from database import get_db_connection, init_db

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("/rapport-quart/historique", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_historique(request: Request):
    init_db()
    conn = get_db_connection()
    historique_clotures = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM cloture_quart ORDER BY id DESC").fetchall()

    clotures_list = [{
        "id": c["id"],
        "date": c["date"],
        "operateur": c["operateur"] or "Agent",
        "especes": float(c["especes"] or 0.0),
        "tpe": float(c["tpe"] or 0.0),
        "credits": float(c["credits"] or 0.0),
        "total_net": float(c["total_net"] or 0.0),
        "fusion": float(c["fusion"] or 0.0),
        "resultat": float(c["resultat"] or 0.0),
        "credits_json": c["credits_json"] or "[]"
    } for c in historique_clotures]
    conn.close()

    content = f"""
    <div class="h-full flex flex-col gap-2.5 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
        <!-- BARRE FILTRES -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 shadow-xs shrink-0">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Date:</span>
                    <input type="date" id="filtreDateQuart" oninput="filtrerHistoriqueQuarts()" class="bg-transparent text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs outline-none font-mono">
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Agent:</span>
                    <input type="text" id="filtreAgentQuart" oninput="filtrerHistoriqueQuarts()" placeholder="Tous..." class="bg-transparent text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs outline-none font-bold w-24 sm:w-32">
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Bilan:</span>
                    <select id="filtreStatutQuart" onchange="filtrerHistoriqueQuarts()" class="bg-transparent text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-xs outline-none font-bold">
                        <option value="ALL" class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26]">Tous</option>
                        <option value="EQUILIBRE" class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26]">Équilibré (0.00)</option>
                        <option value="SURPLUS" class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26]">Surplus (+)</option>
                        <option value="DEFICIT" class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26]">Manquant (-)</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <button onclick="resetFiltresQuarts()" title="Réinitialiser" class="p-1.5 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-[#070E15] text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] hover:bg-slate-200 transition">
                    <i data-lucide="rotate-ccw" class="w-3 h-3"></i>
                </button>
            </div>

            <div class="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 pr-1">
                <span id="countQuartsAffiches" class="text-sky-500 font-bold">0</span> quart(s)
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- TABLE DES CLÔTURES -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] overflow-hidden shadow-xs flex-1 flex flex-col">
            <div class="overflow-x-auto flex-1">
                <table class="w-full text-left min-w-[780px]">
                    <thead class="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] text-[9.5px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-400 sticky top-0 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042]">
                        <tr>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3">Date</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3">Agent</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 text-right">Espèces</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 text-right">TPE</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 text-right">Crédits</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-black">Total Net</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-sky-500 font-bold">Fusion Pompes</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 text-center">Écart</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody id="tableBodyHistoriqueQuarts" class="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-[#183042]/50 font-mono text-xs"></tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            <div class="p-2 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-2 shrink-0 font-mono text-xs">
                <div class="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 text-[11px]">
                    Total Encaissé : <span id="sommeTotalNetQuarts" class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">0.00 DH</span> | 
                    Total Fusion : <span id="sommeFusionQuarts" class="font-black text-sky-500">0.00 DH</span>
                </div>
                <div class="text-[11px]">
                    Écart Global : <span id="sommeEcartQuarts" class="font-black">0.00 DH</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const TOUTES_LES_CLOTURES = {json.dumps(clotures_list)};
        let cloturesFiltrees = [];

        function resetFiltresQuarts() {{
            document.getElementById('filtreDateQuart').value = "";
            document.getElementById('filtreAgentQuart').value = "";
            document.getElementById('filtreStatutQuart').value = "ALL";
            filtrerHistoriqueQuarts();
        }}

        function filtrerHistoriqueQuarts() {{
            const fDate = document.getElementById('filtreDateQuart').value;
            const fAgent = (document.getElementById('filtreAgentQuart').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const fStatut = document.getElementById('filtreStatutQuart').value;

            cloturesFiltrees = [];
            let sumNet = 0, sumFusion = 0, sumEcart = 0;

            for (let i = 0; i < TOUTES_LES_CLOTURES.length; i++) {{
                const c = TOUTES_LES_CLOTURES[i];
                if (fDate && !c.date.startsWith(fDate)) continue;
                if (fAgent && !c.operateur.toLowerCase().includes(fAgent)) continue;

                const res = c.resultat;
                if (fStatut === 'EQUILIBRE' && Math.abs(res) >= 0.01) continue;
                if (fStatut === 'SURPLUS' && res <= 0.01) continue;
                if (fStatut === 'DEFICIT' && res >= -0.01) continue;

                cloturesFiltrees.push(c);
                sumNet += c.total_net;
                sumFusion += c.fusion;
                sumEcart += c.resultat;
            }}

            document.getElementById('countQuartsAffiches').innerText = cloturesFiltrees.length;
            document.getElementById('sommeTotalNetQuarts').innerText = sumNet.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' DH';
            document.getElementById('sommeFusionQuarts').innerText = sumFusion.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' DH';
            
            const ecartEl = document.getElementById('sommeEcartQuarts');
            if (Math.abs(sumEcart) < 0.01) {{
                ecartEl.className = "font-black text-emerald-500";
                ecartEl.innerText = "0.00 DH (Équilibré)";
            }} else if (sumEcart > 0) {{
                ecartEl.className = "font-black text-emerald-500";
                ecartEl.innerText = "+" + sumEcart.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + " DH (Surplus)";
            }} else {{
                ecartEl.className = "font-black text-rose-500";
                ecartEl.innerText = sumEcart.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}}) + " DH (Manquant)";
            }}
            rendreTableauQuarts();
        }}

        function rendreTableauQuarts() {{
            const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBodyHistoriqueQuarts');
            if (!tbody) return;
            if (cloturesFiltrees.length === 0) {{
                tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="8" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400 font-sans text-xs italic">Aucune clôture de quart trouvée.</td></tr>`;
                return;
            }}

            let html = "";
            for (let i = 0; i < cloturesFiltrees.length; i++) {{
                const c = cloturesFiltrees[i];
                const res = c.resultat;
                let badge = Math.abs(res) < 0.01 
                    ? '<span class="px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500 font-bold text-[9.5px]">Équilibré</span>'
                    : (res > 0 ? `<span class="px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500 font-bold text-[9.5px]">+${{res.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}} DH</span>`
                               : `<span class="px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-500 font-bold text-[9.5px]">${{res.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}} DH</span>`);

                html += `
                <tr class="border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/50 hover:bg-slate-50/80 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02]">
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 font-sans text-slate-500 text-[11px]">${{c.date}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 font-sans font-black text-slate-800 dark:text-white uppercase text-[11px]">${{c.operateur}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0] font-bold text-[11px]">+${{c.especes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-sky-500 font-semibold text-[11px]">+${{c.tpe.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-amber-500 font-semibold text-[11px]">+${{c.credits.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-[11px]">${{c.total_net.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-[#38BDF8] text-[11px]">${{c.fusion.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2}})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center">${{badge}}</td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }}
            tbody.innerHTML = html;
        }}

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            filtrerHistoriqueQuarts();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, "historique", request=request))

FICHIER : .\pages\rapport_soldes_clients.py

# ==============================================================================
# FICHIER : pages/rapport_soldes_clients.py
# ==============================================================================

import html
import json
import base64
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse

from database import get_db_connection
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

def mad(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

@router.get("/centre-rapports/soldes-clients", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_rapport_soldes_clients(request: Request):
    conn = get_db_connection()

    # Récupération de tous les clients avec solde calculé
    query = """
        SELECT c.*,
               COALESCE((SELECT SUM(montant) FROM client_encaissements WHERE client_id = c.id), 0.0) AS total_encaisse,
               COALESCE((SELECT SUM(montant_ttc) FROM factures_clients WHERE client_id = c.id), 0.0) AS total_factures
        FROM clients c
        ORDER BY c.nom ASC
    """
    rows = conn.execute(query).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    clients_list = []
    options_clients_html = '<option value="ALL">Tous les clients</option>'

    for r in rows:
        cid = r["id"]
        c_nom = (r["nom"] or "Client Sans Nom").strip()
        c_code = (r["code"] or "CL-000").strip()
        c_type = (r["type_client"] or "Entreprise").strip()
        solde_raw = float(r["solde"] or 0.0)
        plafond_val = float(r["plafond"] or 0.0)
        tel_val = (r["telephone"] or "—").strip()
        ice_val = (r["ice"] or "—").strip()
        statut_val = (r["statut"] or "ACTIF").strip()

        # Règle comptable : solde > 0 => Dette (créance station) | solde < 0 => Crédit (avance client)
        dette = solde_raw if solde_raw > 0 else 0.0
        credit = abs(solde_raw) if solde_raw < 0 else 0.0

        clients_list.append({
            "id": cid,
            "nom": c_nom,
            "code": c_code,
            "type_client": c_type,
            "telephone": tel_val,
            "ice": ice_val,
            "plafond": plafond_val,
            "solde_net": solde_raw,
            "dette": dette,
            "credit": credit,
            "statut": statut_val
        })

        options_clients_html += f'<option value="{cid}">{html.escape(c_nom)} ({html.escape(c_code)})</option>'

    # Encodage sécurisé en Base64 pour traitement JS sans bug de quotes
    clients_b64 = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(clients_list).encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    content = f"""
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
        
        <!-- 1. EN-TÊTE + FILTRES (RESPONSIVE & CONNECTÉ) -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs space-y-3">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-pink-500 to-rose-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold shadow-md shadow-pink-500/20">
                        <i data-lucide="users" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <h1 class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-sm uppercase tracking-wider">
                            État des Soldes Clients
                        </h1>
                        <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Suivi des créances clients, dettes en cours et soldes créditeurs</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <a href="/centre-rapports" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold transition">
                    <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                    <span>Retour Hub</span>
                </a>
            </div>

            <!-- LES 3 FILTRES DEMANDÉS (TYPE DE CLIENT - CLIENT - FILTRE SOLDE) -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 items-end">
                <div>
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Type de Client</label>
                    <select id="filtreTypeClient" onchange="filtrerDirectSoldes()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="ALL">Tous les types</option>
                        <option value="Entreprise">Entreprise</option>
                        <option value="Particulier">Particulier</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Client</label>
                    <select id="filtreClient" onchange="filtrerDirectSoldes()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        {options_clients_html}
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Filtre Solde</label>
                    <select id="filtreSolde" onchange="filtrerDirectSoldes()" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="ALL">Tous</option>
                        <option value="DETTE">Débiteurs (Dettes > 0)</option>
                        <option value="CREDIT">Créditeurs (Crédits > 0)</option>
                        <option value="SOLDE">Soldés (Solde = 0)</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 2. LES 4 CARTES KPIS CONFORMES À L'IMAGE 4 -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            <!-- Carte 1 : CLIENTS (Bordure bleue) -->

                <div id="kpiTotalCredits" class="text-2xl font-black font-mono text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]">0,00</div>

        <!-- 3. TABLEAU RÉCAPITULATIF PAR TYPE DE CLIENT (IMAGE 4) -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs overflow-hidden">

                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-center">Nombre</th>

                    <tbody id="recapSoldesTbody" class="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-[#183042]/50 text-xs font-semibold">
                    </tbody>
                    <tfoot class="bg-rose-50/70 dark:bg-rose-950/20 border-t-2 border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/40 text-xs font-black">
                        <tr>
                            <td class="py-3 px-4 uppercase text-slate-900 dark:text-white">TOTAL</td>
                            <td id="tfootNb" class="py-3 px-4 text-center text-slate-900 dark:text-white font-mono">0</td>
                            <td id="tfootDettes" class="py-3 px-4 text-right text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 font-mono">0,00</td>
                            <td id="tfootCredits" class="py-3 px-4 text-right text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0] font-mono">0,00</td>
                            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                                <button type="button" onclick="ouvrirModalSoldes('ALL')" 
                                        class="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#184E68] hover:bg-sky-600 text-white font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wide transition shadow-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 mx-auto cursor-pointer">
                                    <i data-lucide="list" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                                    <span>Détails</span>
                                </button>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    </tfoot>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>

    </div>

    <!-- ============================================================================== -->
    <!-- 4. MODALE POPUP DE DÉTAILS DES CLIENTS (MÊME STRUCTURE QUE ENCAISSEMENTS) -->
    <!-- ============================================================================== -->
    <div id="modal-soldes" class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4 hidden transition-all">
        
        <div id="modal-box" class="bg-[#0A1622] border border-[#1b3449] rounded-2xl w-full max-w-5xl h-[88vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl overflow-hidden transition-all duration-200">
            
            <!-- EN-TÊTE MODALE -->
            <div class="px-5 py-3 border-b border-[#1b3449] bg-[#071018] flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">

                    <span class="font-black text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-200">
                        Détails des Soldes Clients

                <div class="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-pink-500/15 border border-pink-400/30 flex items-center gap-2 shadow-xs">
                    <i data-lucide="shield-check" class="w-4 h-4 text-pink-400"></i>

                        TOUS LES TYPES
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button type="button" onclick="toggleAgrandirModal()" title="Agrandir / Réduire" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer transition">
                        <i id="icon-maximize" data-lucide="maximize-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="fermerModalSoldes()" title="Fermer" class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:text-rose-400 text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer transition">
                        <i data-lucide="x" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- FILTRES INTERNES MODALE -->
            <div class="px-5 py-3 bg-[#0d1d2c] border-b border-[#1b3449] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 shrink-0">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#071018] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[#1e3a52]">
                    <span class="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-slate-400 px-2">Type:</span>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalType('ALL')" id="btn-type-ALL" class="btn-type-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-pink-600 text-white">Tous</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalType('Entreprise')" id="btn-type-Entreprise" class="btn-type-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Entreprise</button>
                    <button type="button" onclick="setModalType('Particulier')" id="btn-type-Particulier" class="btn-type-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white">Particulier</button>
                </div>

                <div class="relative w-48 sm:w-64">
                    <input type="text" id="modalRechercheLibre" oninput="appliquerFiltresModal()" placeholder="Rechercher client, code, ICE..." 
                           class="w-full bg-[#071018] border border-[#1e3a52] rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-pink-300 placeholder-slate-500 outline-none focus:border-pink-400">
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- COMPTEUR MODALE -->
            <div class="px-5 py-2 bg-[#091520] border-b border-[#1b3449]/70 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 shrink-0">
                <span id="modalCompteur" class="text-pink-400 font-mono">0 client(s)</span>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-4 font-mono">
                    <span>Dettes : <strong id="modalTotalDettes" class="text-rose-400 font-black">0,00 DH</strong></span>
                    <span>Crédits : <strong id="modalTotalCredits" class="text-emerald-400 font-black">0,00 DH</strong></span>
                    <span>Net : <strong id="modalTotalNet" class="text-white font-black">0,00 DH</strong></span>

            <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto bg-[#071018]">

                        <tr class="text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] font-black uppercase text-white tracking-wider">
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-sky-300">Code</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-white">Client</th>
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-center text-white">Type</th>

    <!-- MOTEUR JS INTERACTIF ET SYNCHRONE -->
    <script>
        const RAW_B64 = "{clients_b64}";
        const TOUS_LES_CLIENTS = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(RAW_B64))));
        
        let clientsFiltresActifs = [];
        let typeModalActif = 'ALL';
        let estPleinEcran = false;

        function badgeTypeClient(type) {{
            if ((type || '').toLowerCase() === 'entreprise') {{
                return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-500/20 dark:text-[#00F5A0] border border-emerald-300/40">Entreprise</span>';
            }}
            return '<span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-500/20 dark:text-blue-300 border border-blue-300/40">Particulier</span>';
        }}

        function filtrerDirectSoldes() {{
            const fType = document.getElementById('filtreTypeClient').value;
            const fClientId = document.getElementById('filtreClient').value;
            const fSolde = document.getElementById('filtreSolde').value;

            clientsFiltresActifs = [];
            let sumDettes = 0;
            let sumCredits = 0;
            let sumNet = 0;

            let parType = {{}};

            for (let i = 0; i < TOUS_LES_CLIENTS.length; i++) {{
                const c = TOUS_LES_CLIENTS[i];

                if (fType !== 'ALL' && c.type_client.toLowerCase() !== fType.toLowerCase()) continue;
                if (fClientId !== 'ALL' && String(c.id) !== String(fClientId)) continue;

                if (fSolde === 'DETTE' && c.dette <= 0.001) continue;
                if (fSolde === 'CREDIT' && c.credit <= 0.001) continue;
                if (fSolde === 'SOLDE' && (c.dette > 0.001 || c.credit > 0.001)) continue;

                clientsFiltresActifs.push(c);

                sumDettes += c.dette;
                sumCredits += c.credit;
                sumNet += c.solde_net;

                const t = c.type_client || 'Entreprise';
                if (!parType[t]) parType[t] = {{ count: 0, dettes: 0, credits: 0 }};
                parType[t].count++;
                parType[t].dettes += c.dette;
                parType[t].credits += c.credit;
            }}

            // 1. Mise à jour des 4 KPIs
            document.getElementById('kpiNbClients').innerText = clientsFiltresActifs.length;
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalDettes').innerText = sumDettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});
            document.getElementById('kpiTotalCredits').innerText = sumCredits.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});
            document.getElementById('kpiSoldeNet').innerText = sumNet.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});

            // 2. Remplissage du tableau Récapitulatif par Type de Client (Image 4)
            const tbody = document.getElementById('recapSoldesTbody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';

            for (const [nomType, info] of Object.entries(parType)) {{
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = "hover:bg-slate-50/50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02] cursor-pointer";
                tr.onclick = function() {{ ouvrirModalSoldes(nomType); }};

                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">${{badgeTypeClient(nomType)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">${{info.count}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">${{info.dettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-[#00F5A0]">${{info.credits.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }})}}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
                        <button type="button" onclick="ouvrirModalSoldes('${{nomType}}')" 
                                class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#184E68] hover:bg-sky-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1 mx-auto cursor-pointer shadow-xs">
                            <i data-lucide="list" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                            <span>Détails</span>
                        </button>
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            }}

            document.getElementById('tfootNb').innerText = clientsFiltresActifs.length;
            document.getElementById('tfootDettes').innerText = sumDettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});
            document.getElementById('tfootCredits').innerText = sumCredits.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }});

            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function ouvrirModalSoldes(type) {{
            typeModalActif = type;
            document.getElementById('modalRechercheLibre').value = '';
            majPillsType();
            appliquerFiltresModal();
            document.getElementById('modal-soldes').classList.remove('hidden');
        }}

        function setModalType(type) {{
            typeModalActif = type;
            majPillsType();
            appliquerFiltresModal();
        }}

        function majPillsType() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-type-pill').forEach(btn => {{
                btn.className = "btn-type-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white";
            }});
            const actif = document.getElementById('btn-type-' + typeModalActif);
            if (actif) actif.className = "btn-type-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-pink-600 text-white shadow-sm";
            document.getElementById('modal-type-titre').innerText = (typeModalActif === 'ALL') ? 'TOUS LES TYPES' : typeModalActif.toUpperCase();
        }}

        function appliquerFiltresModal() {{
            const search = document.getElementById('modalRechercheLibre').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            let lignes = clientsFiltresActifs;

            if (typeModalActif !== 'ALL') {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(c => c.type_client.toLowerCase() === typeModalActif.toLowerCase());
            }}

            if (search) {{
                lignes = lignes.filter(c => 
                    c.nom.toLowerCase().includes(search) || 
                    c.code.toLowerCase().includes(search) || 
                    c.ice.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
                    c.telephone.toLowerCase().includes(search)
                );
            }}

            afficherLignesModal(lignes);
        }}

        function afficherLignesModal(lignes) {{
            const tbody = document.getElementById('modalTbody');
            const empty = document.getElementById('modalEmpty');
            const compteur = document.getElementById('modalCompteur');
            const totDettesEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalDettes');
            const totCreditsEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalCredits');
            const totNetEl = document.getElementById('modalTotalNet');

            compteur.innerText = `${{lignes.length}} client(s)`;

            let sumDettes = 0;
            let sumCredits = 0;
            let sumNet = 0;

            if (lignes.length === 0) {{
                tbody.innerHTML = '';
                empty.classList.remove('hidden');
                totDettesEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                totCreditsEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                totNetEl.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                return;
            }}

            empty.classList.add('hidden');
            let h = '';
            lignes.forEach(c => {{
                sumDettes += c.dette;
                sumCredits += c.credit;
                sumNet += c.solde_net;

                h += `
                <tr class="hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors border-b border-[#183042]/40">
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-sky-400 text-xs">${{c.code}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-100 uppercase">${{c.nom}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">${{badgeTypeClient(c.type_client)}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-rose-500">${{c.dette.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-emerald-400">${{c.credit.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-white">${{c.solde_net.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }})}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10.5px] font-extrabold ${{c.statut === 'ACTIF' ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30' : 'bg-slate-500/15 text-slate-400 border border-slate-500/30'}}">
                            ${{c.statut}}
                        </span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <a href="/clients/${{c.id}}" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white/5 hover:bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400 inline-flex items-center justify-center transition" title="Voir fiche client">
                            <i data-lucide="external-link" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                        </a>
                    </td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }});

            tbody.innerHTML = h;
            totDettesEl.innerText = sumDettes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            totCreditsEl.innerText = sumCredits.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            totNetEl.innerText = sumNet.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{ minimumFractionDigits: 2 }}) + ' DH';
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function toggleAgrandirModal() {{
            const box = document.getElementById('modal-box');
            const icon = document.getElementById('icon-maximize');
            estPleinEcran = !estPleinEcran;

            if (estPleinEcran) {{
                box.classList.remove('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.add('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'minimize-2');
            }} else {{
                box.classList.add('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.remove('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'maximize-2');
            }}
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function fermerModalSoldes() {{
            document.getElementById('modal-soldes').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {{
            if (e.key === 'Escape') fermerModalSoldes();
        }});

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {{
            filtrerDirectSoldes();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="centre_rapports", request=request))

FICHIER : .\pages\rapport_ventes_pompes.py

# ==============================================================================
# FICHIER : pages/rapport_ventes_pompes.py (FILTRES PAR QUARTS/CARB + HEADERS CLAIRS)
# ==============================================================================

import html
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Form
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse

from database import get_db_connection, get_fournisseurs
from ui_utils import layout

router = APIRouter()

def mad(val: float) -> str:
    try:
        return f"{float(val or 0.0):,.2f}".replace(",", " ").replace(".", ",")
    except Exception:
        return "0,00"

# ==============================================================================
# 1. API AJAX : DÉTAIL DES TIRAGES D'UN JOUR
# ==============================================================================
@router.get("/api/rapport/jour-details")
async def api_jour_details(date_jour: str):
    conn = get_db_connection()
    
    c_quart = conn.execute("""
        SELECT SUM(especes) as tot_esp, SUM(tpe) as tot_tpe, SUM(total_net) as tot_net
        FROM cloture_quart WHERE date LIKE ? || '%'
    """, (date_jour,)).fetchone()
    
    tot_net = float(c_quart['tot_net'] or 0.0) if c_quart else 0.0
    r_tpe = (float(c_quart['tot_tpe'] or 0.0) / tot_net) if tot_net > 0 else 0.125
    r_esp = 1.0 - r_tpe

    rows = conn.execute("""
        SELECT heure, date_vente, sale_id, pump_id, type, volume, money
        FROM transactions_jour
        WHERE (date_releve = ? OR date_vente LIKE ? || '%')
        ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 500
    """, (date_jour, date_jour)).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    lignes = []
    for r in rows:
        h = str(r["heure"] or (r["date_vente"].split()[-1] if r["date_vente"] and " " in r["date_vente"] else "--:--"))[:8]
        mnt = float(r["money"] or 0.0)
        c_nom = "SSP" if r["type"] in ["ESS", "SSP"] else "DIESEL"

        lignes.append({
            "heure": h,
            "sale_id": r["sale_id"] or "--",
            "pump": f"Pompe {r['pump_id']}",
            "carb": c_nom,
            "vol_raw": float(r["volume"] or 0.0),
            "mnt_raw": mnt,
            "vol": mad(r["volume"]),
            "mnt": mad(mnt),
            "tpe": mad(mnt * r_tpe),
            "esp": mad(mnt * r_esp)
        })

    return JSONResponse({"status": "success", "date": date_jour, "lignes": lignes})

# ==============================================================================
# 2. PAGE PRINCIPALE
# ==============================================================================
@router.api_route("/centre-rapports/ventes-carburants", methods=["GET", "POST"], response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def page_ventes_carburants(
    request: Request,
    fournisseur_id: Optional[int] = Form(None),
    date_debut: Optional[str] = Form(None),
    date_fin: Optional[str] = Form(None)
):
    if request.method == "GET":
        qp = request.query_params
        fournisseur_id = int(qp.get("fournisseur_id", 0)) if qp.get("fournisseur_id") else 0
        date_debut = qp.get("date_debut")
        date_fin = qp.get("date_fin")

    today = datetime.now()
    d_debut = date_debut or (today - timedelta(days=30)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    d_fin = date_fin or today.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    fournisseurs = get_fournisseurs()
    okay_energy_id = next((f["id"] for f in fournisseurs if "OKAY" in f.get("nom", "").upper()), 0)
    if not fournisseur_id and okay_energy_id:
        fournisseur_id = okay_energy_id

    conn = get_db_connection()

    ratios = conn.execute("""
        SELECT SUM(especes) as tot_esp, SUM(tpe) as tot_tpe, SUM(total_net) as tot_net
        FROM cloture_quart WHERE substr(date, 1, 10) BETWEEN ? AND ?
    """, (d_debut, d_fin)).fetchone()

    tot_net = float(ratios['tot_net'] or 0.0) if ratios else 0.0
    r_tpe = (float(ratios['tot_tpe'] or 0.0) / tot_net) if tot_net > 0 else 0.125
    r_esp = 1.0 - r_tpe

    query_synth = """
        SELECT 
            COALESCE(NULLIF(substr(date_vente, 1, 10), ''), date_releve) as jour,
            COUNT(id) as nb_tirages,
            SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('DIE', 'GO', 'GAZOIL') THEN volume ELSE 0 END) as vol_die,
            SUM(CASE WHEN type IN ('ESS', 'SSP') THEN volume ELSE 0 END) as vol_ssp,
            SUM(volume) as vol_tot,
            SUM(money) as mnt_tot
        FROM transactions_jour
        WHERE COALESCE(NULLIF(substr(date_vente, 1, 10), ''), date_releve) BETWEEN ? AND ?
        GROUP BY jour
        ORDER BY jour DESC
        LIMIT 60
    """
    lignes_jours = conn.execute(query_synth, (d_debut, d_fin)).fetchall()
    conn.close()

    tot_vol_die = sum(float(j["vol_die"] or 0) for j in lignes_jours)
    tot_vol_ssp = sum(float(j["vol_ssp"] or 0) for j in lignes_jours)
    tot_volume = sum(float(j["vol_tot"] or 0) for j in lignes_jours)
    tot_montant = sum(float(j["mnt_tot"] or 0) for j in lignes_jours)
    tot_tpe = tot_montant * r_tpe
    tot_especes = tot_montant * r_esp

    tableau_jours_html = ""
    for j in lignes_jours:
        j_date = j["jour"]
        mnt = float(j["mnt_tot"] or 0)
        tpe_val = mnt * r_tpe
        esp_val = mnt * r_esp

        tableau_jours_html += f"""
        <tr class="border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]/50 hover:bg-sky-50/50 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.02] text-xs font-semibold">
            <td class="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400">{j_date}</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold text-slate-500">{j['nb_tirages']}</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{mad(j['vol_die'])} L</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{mad(j['vol_ssp'])} L</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{mad(mnt)} DH</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400">{mad(tpe_val)} DH</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400">{mad(esp_val)} DH</td>
            <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                <button type="button" onclick="ouvrirModalTirages('{j_date}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-sky-600 hover:text-white dark:bg-[#132738] dark:hover:bg-sky-600 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1 mx-auto cursor-pointer">
                    <i data-lucide="list" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                    <span>Tirages</span>
                </button>
            </td>
        </tr>
        """

    if not tableau_jours_html:
        tableau_jours_html = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-center py-8 text-slate-400 italic">Aucune donnée trouvée sur cette période.</td></tr>'

    options_fournisseurs = "".join(
        f'<option value="{f["id"]}" {"selected" if f["id"] == fournisseur_id else ""}>{html.escape(f["nom"])}</option>'
        for f in fournisseurs
    )

    content = f"""
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
        
        <!-- EN-TÊTE + FILTRES -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-[#0E1B26] p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-[#183042] shadow-xs space-y-3">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#183042]">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-500 flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                        <i data-lucide="fuel" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                    </div>
                    <h1 class="font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white text-sm uppercase tracking-wider">
                        Rapport Carburants & Synthèse Journalière
                    </h1>
                </div>

                <a href="/centre-rapports" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-[#132738] text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold transition">
                    <i data-lucide="arrow-left" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                    <span>Retour Hub</span>
                </a>
            </div>

            <form method="POST" action="/centre-rapports/ventes-carburants" class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3 items-end">
                <div class="sm:col-span-4">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Station</label>
                    <select name="fournisseur_id" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                        {options_fournisseurs}
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-3">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Date début</label>
                    <input type="date" name="date_debut" value="{d_debut}" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-3">
                    <label class="block text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400 mb-1">Date fin</label>
                    <input type="date" name="date_fin" value="{d_fin}" class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#070E15] border border-slate-300 dark:border-[#1E3A52] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white outline-none">
                </div>

                <div class="sm:col-span-2">
                    <button type="submit" class="w-full h-[38px] rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 font-bold uppercase text-xs text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-amber-500 hover:from-orange-600 hover:to-amber-600 shadow-md transition cursor-pointer active:scale-95">
                        <i data-lucide="filter" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                        <span>Actualiser</span>

        <!-- 4 CARTES KPI -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">

                <div class="text-xl font-black font-mono text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{mad(tot_volume)} L</div>

                    <!-- EN-TÊTE DU TABLEAU : TAILLE AGRANDIE, COULEUR BLANCHE ÉCLATANTE -->

                        <tr class="text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] font-black uppercase text-white tracking-wider">
                            <th class="py-3 px-4 text-sky-300">Heure</th>

                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- SCRIPTS DES FILTRES INTERACTIFS ET DU PLEIN ÉCRAN -->
    <script>
        let toutesLesLignesJour = [];
        let filtreActifCarb = 'TOUS';
        let filtreActifQuart = 'TOUS';
        let estPleinEcran = false;

        async function ouvrirModalTirages(dateJour) {{
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-tirages');
            const titre = document.getElementById('modal-date-titre');
            const loading = document.getElementById('modal-loading');
            const table = document.getElementById('modal-table');
            const inputHeure = document.getElementById('filtre-heure-libre');

            titre.innerText = dateJour;
            inputHeure.value = '';
            
            // Réinitialiser les boutons de filtres
            resetFiltresUI();

            loading.classList.remove('hidden');
            table.classList.add('hidden');
            modal.classList.remove('hidden');

            try {{
                const res = await fetch(`/api/rapport/jour-details?date_jour=${{dateJour}}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                
                if (data.status === 'success' && data.lignes) {{
                    toutesLesLignesJour = data.lignes;
                    appliquerTousLesFiltres();
                    loading.classList.add('hidden');
                    table.classList.remove('hidden');
                }}
            }} catch(e) {{
                loading.innerText = "Erreur de chargement des tirages.";
            }}
        }}

        function resetFiltresUI() {{
            filtreActifCarb = 'TOUS';
            filtreActifQuart = 'TOUS';
            majBoutonsCarb();
            majBoutonsQuart();
        }}

        function setFiltreCarb(carb) {{
            filtreActifCarb = carb;
            majBoutonsCarb();
            appliquerTousLesFiltres();
        }}

        function majBoutonsCarb() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-carb-pill').forEach(btn => {{
                btn.className = "btn-carb-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white";
            }});
            const actif = document.getElementById('btn-carb-' + filtreActifCarb);
            if (actif) actif.className = "btn-carb-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-extrabold uppercase transition bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 shadow-sm";
        }}

        function setFiltreQuart(quart) {{
            filtreActifQuart = quart;
            majBoutonsQuart();
            appliquerTousLesFiltres();
        }}

        function majBoutonsQuart() {{
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-quart-pill').forEach(btn => {{
                btn.className = "btn-quart-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition text-slate-300 hover:text-white";
            }});
            const actif = document.getElementById('btn-quart-' + filtreActifQuart);
            if (actif) actif.className = "btn-quart-pill px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition bg-sky-500 text-slate-950 shadow-sm";
        }}

        function appliquerTousLesFiltres() {{
            let res = toutesLesLignesJour;

            // 1. Filtre par Carburant
            if (filtreActifCarb !== 'TOUS') {{
                res = res.filter(l => l.carb.toUpperCase() === filtreActifCarb);
            }}

            // 2. Filtre par Créneaux / Quarts
            if (filtreActifQuart === 'MATIN') {{
                res = res.filter(l => {{
                    const h = parseInt(l.heure.split(':')[0], 10);
                    return h >= 6 && h < 14;
                }});
            }} else if (filtreActifQuart === 'SOIR') {{
                res = res.filter(l => {{
                    const h = parseInt(l.heure.split(':')[0], 10);
                    return h >= 14 && h < 22;
                }});
            }} else if (filtreActifQuart === 'NUIT') {{
                res = res.filter(l => {{
                    const h = parseInt(l.heure.split(':')[0], 10);
                    return h >= 22 || h < 6;
                }});
            }}

            // 3. Filtre par heure libre
            const heureLibre = document.getElementById('filtre-heure-libre').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            if (heureLibre) {{
                res = res.filter(l => l.heure.toLowerCase().includes(heureLibre));
            }}

            afficherLignes(res);
        }}

        function afficherLignes(lignes) {{
            const tbody = document.getElementById('modal-tbody');
            const compteur = document.getElementById('modal-compteur');
            const totVol = document.getElementById('modal-tot-vol');
            const totMnt = document.getElementById('modal-tot-mnt');

            compteur.innerText = `${{lignes.length}} tirage(s)`;

            let sommeVol = 0;
            let sommeMnt = 0;

            if (lignes.length === 0) {{
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="text-center py-12 text-slate-500 italic font-bold">Aucun tirage correspondant aux filtres.</td></tr>';
                totVol.innerText = '0,00 L';
                totMnt.innerText = '0,00 DH';
                return;
            }}

            let html = '';
            lignes.forEach(l => {{
                sommeVol += l.vol_raw;
                sommeMnt += l.mnt_raw;

                html += `
                <tr class="hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors border-b border-[#183042]/40">
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-sky-400 text-xs">${{l.heure}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-300">#${{l.sale_id}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-200">${{l.pump}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 font-black ${{l.carb === 'SSP' ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}}">${{l.carb}}</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-slate-100">${{l.vol}} L</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-black text-white">${{l.mnt}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-purple-400">${{l.tpe}} DH</td>
                    <td class="py-2.5 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-teal-400">${{l.esp}} DH</td>
                </tr>
                `;
            }});

            tbody.innerHTML = html;
            totVol.innerText = sommeVol.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' L';
            totMnt.innerText = sommeMnt.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {{minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2}}) + ' DH';
        }}

        function toggleAgrandirModal() {{
            const box = document.getElementById('modal-box');
            const icon = document.getElementById('icon-maximize');
            estPleinEcran = !estPleinEcran;

            if (estPleinEcran) {{
                box.classList.remove('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.add('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'minimize-2');
            }} else {{
                box.classList.add('max-w-5xl', 'h-[88vh]', 'rounded-2xl');
                box.classList.remove('max-w-none', 'h-full', 'rounded-none');
                icon.setAttribute('data-lucide', 'maximize-2');
            }}
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();
        }}

        function fermerModalTirages() {{
            document.getElementById('modal-tirages').classList.add('hidden');
        }}

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {{
            if (e.key === 'Escape') fermerModalTirages();
        }});
    </script>
    """
    return HTMLResponse(layout(content, active_tab="centre_rapports", request=request))

COLOR_PALETTES = [
    {
        "border": "border-l-[4px] border-l-blue-500",
        "avatar_bg": "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/20",
    },

        "border": "border-l-[4px] border-l-emerald-500",
        "avatar_bg": "bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20",
    },

        "border": "border-l-[4px] border-l-amber-500",
        "avatar_bg": "bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20",
    },

        "border": "border-l-[4px] border-l-purple-500",
        "avatar_bg": "bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/20",
